### [1] [Generalized reverberation theory in diffuse sound fields: Introducing mean residual free path for macroscopic and microscopic unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35145)
*Toshiki Hanyu*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文对修正混响理论进行了全面推广与数学奠基：从能量微分方程直接推导宏观模型，通过卷积重构证明微观模型与宏观模型的一致性，并揭示Eyring低估源于多次反射中时间方差展开(σ_n²=nσ²)的结构性遗漏，经射线追踪仿真验证其尺度不变精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sabine理论在完全吸声房间中无法预测零混响时间；Eyring公式虽修正此问题并被Knudsen扩展到空气吸收，但其方法存在根本性理论矛盾。作者此前提出的宏观与微观修正模型虽能解决缺陷，却缺乏数学统一、严格的微分方程推导，且Eyring低估的机制尚未阐明。

Method: 从基本能量微分方程直接推导宏观模型，纳入空气吸收与广义平均剩余自由程；将微观模型重构为序贯卷积过程，数学证明其与宏观模型的基本一致性；从理论上分析多次反射时间方差展开的结构性遗漏，并用射线追踪仿真进行验证。

Result: 证明了宏观与微观模型在数学上的一致性；揭示Eyring低估普遍源于多次反射中时间方差展开(σ_n²=nσ²)的结构性遗漏，且与所假设的概率分布无关；射线追踪仿真验证了所提理论的尺度不变精度。

Conclusion: 该数学自洽的框架确立了未来非扩散声场广义混响理论的理论极限。

Abstract: Sabine's foundational theory established the cornerstone of modern architectural acoustics. However, it fails to predict zero reverberation time in perfectly absorptive rooms. Eyring subsequently addressed this issue by proposing a new formula, which Knudsen later extended to incorporate air absorption. Nevertheless, Eyring's underlying approach contains fundamental theoretical contradictions. To resolve these flaws, the author previously introduced macroscopic and microscopic models for a revised reverberation theory; however, their mathematical unification, rigorous derivation from differential equations, and the mechanism behind Eyring's underestimation remained unclarified. This paper presents a comprehensive generalization and mathematical foundation of the revised theory. The macroscopic model is derived directly from fundamental energy differential equations, incorporating air absorption and a generalized mean residual free path. By reformulating the microscopic model as a sequential convolution process, its fundamental consistency with the macroscopic model is mathematically demonstrated. Crucially, it is shown that Eyring's underestimation can be interpreted as stemming universally from the structural omission of temporal variance expansion (σ_{n}^{2}=nσ^{2}) inherent to multiple reflections, regardless of the assumed probability distribution. Finally, ray-tracing simulations validate the scale-invariant accuracy of the proposed theory. Ultimately, this mathematically consistent framework establishes the theoretical limit for future generalized theories in non-diffuse sound fields.

</details>


### [2] [Similarities between the speed limit in relativity and the sound barrier in inviscid fluid flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35198)
*F Salmon*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文以教学式综述系统梳理了Prandtl-Glauert-Lorentz变换所揭示的可压缩势流理论与狭义相对论之间的数学对应关系，并借助附加质量概念将该类比推广至整个狭义相对论数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该数学对应自20世纪初便隐含于空气动力学文献中，但两个学科社群对其认识不足，且从未有统一综述加以整理，因此有必要在面向双方的统一框架下予以系统呈现。

Method: 采用自成体系、便于教学且兼顾两个学科读者的方式：先追溯可压缩空气动力学理论与狭义相对论平行发展的历史；再建立洛伦兹变换与Prandtl-Glauert坐标变换之间的形式等价；随后在声学框架下重新诠释时间膨胀、多普勒效应与洛伦兹因子等相对论概念；最后利用附加质量概念作进一步推广。

Result: 确立了洛伦兹变换与Prandtl-Glauert坐标变换的形式等价性，并在声学框架下成功重释了时间膨胀、多普勒效应和洛伦兹因子等相对论概念。

Conclusion: 借助附加质量概念，该类比可被进一步扩展，表明狭义相对论的整个数学框架与线性化可压缩势流理论完全对应，从而统一了两个原本彼此隔离的理论体系。

Abstract: The Prandtl-Glauert-Lorentz transformation establishes a deep mathematical correspondence between compressible potential flow theory and special relativity. Despite being implicit in the aerodynamic literature since the early twentieth century, this analogy remains largely unrecognised across the two communities and has never been surveyed in a unified manner. This review presents, in a self-contained and pedagogical framework accessible to both communities, the historical, mathematical, and applied dimensions of this correspondence. After tracing the parallel developments that gave rise to compressible aerodynamic theory and special relativity, we establish the formal equivalence between the Lorentz transformation and the Prandtl-Glauert coordinate change, and reinterpret relativistic concepts such as time dilation, the Doppler effect, and the Lorentz factor within the acoustic framework. Thanks to the added mass concept, we finally extend this analogy and show that the whole mathematical framework of special relativity is exactly mirrored by the linearised compressible potential flow theory.

</details>

### [3] [Open-Qwen-Music: An Auditable Framework for LLM-Based Music Composition and Diffusion Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31652)
*Yangbin Yu,Mingyu Yang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Open-Qwen-Music是首个完全开源的"LLM语义作曲+扩散声学渲染"文本到音乐生成系统复现，包含数据、训练与推理全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同类最强系统（如Qwen-Music）保持闭源；主流开源音乐生成项目仅发布权重与推理代码，缺失训练语料与端到端训练实现，导致无法独立、可控地研究语义表示中的信息损失与预测误差如何经自回归规划传播至声学渲染。

Method: 遵循Qwen-Music报告的跨模块接口进行开放重建：25 Hz单码本音乐分词器 + 3B参数自回归Music LLM + 扩散渲染器（输出48 kHz立体声）。发布完整数据与工件，包括训练数据集及溯源清单、数据预处理/标注/训练/推理/评估全流程、配置，以及每个可学习模块的预训练权重；工件清单在完整工作流中绑定各工件身份。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首个LLM作曲加扩散渲染的文本到音乐系统的完全开放发布；由此建立模块化架构的可复现实现，为组件级分析与后续评测提供实证基础。作者明确声明其不构成与Qwen-Music质量持平的证据。

Conclusion: 该工作定位为透明、可执行的研究基线与社区起点，而非质量对标；Open-Qwen-Music为持续项目，后续将继续提升生成质量、可控性与鲁棒性。所有发布工件见指定GitHub仓库。

Abstract: We present Open-Qwen-Music, an open reconstruction of Qwen-Music and a fully specified research system for text-to-music generation that couples LLM-based semantic composition with diffusion-based acoustic rendering. The system comprises a 25 Hz single-codebook music tokenizer, a 3B-parameter autoregressive Music LLM, and a diffusion renderer producing 48 kHz stereo audio, following the cross-module interfaces reported by Qwen-Music. The strongest systems of this design remain closed, and prominent open music-generation projects release weights and inference code without their training corpora or end-to-end training implementations. This limits independent and controlled study of how information loss and prediction errors propagate from semantic representation through autoregressive planning to acoustic rendering. To our knowledge, Open-Qwen-Music is the first fully open release of an LLM-composition-plus-diffusion-rendering text-to-music system. Beyond model weights and inference code, the release includes the training datasets and provenance manifests, complete data-processing, annotation, training, inference, and evaluation pipelines, configurations, and pretrained weights for every learned module. Artifact manifests bind the identities of these artifacts across the complete workflow. Together, these artifacts establish a reproducible implementation of the modular architecture and provide an empirical basis for component-level analysis and future evaluation. We present the system as a transparent, executable research baseline and a starting point for the community, not as evidence of quality parity with Qwen-Music. Open-Qwen-Music is an ongoing effort, and we will continue to improve its generation quality, controllability, and robustness. All release artifacts are available at https://github.com/biang15343100-source/Open-Qwen-Music.

</details>


### [4] [Normalise or condition? Noise-floor front-ends for on-board keyword spotting under UAV rotor ego-noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31699)
*Yida Lin,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文研究小型多旋翼无人机在真实旋翼自噪声（负信噪比）下的十词小规模关键词识别（KWS），通过对比经典噪声鲁棒前端、测试时自适应及两种跟踪分频带噪声底的前端，发现归一化前端在连续噪声中误报激增，而条件化前端能保持基线误报率并将增益转化为检测率，且整体流水线在Jetson Orin NX上仅需3 ms/100 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机机身上的麦克风被旋翼自噪声主导，语音指令到达时处于负信噪比，导致关键词识别极具挑战。现有前端在单片段准确率上看似有效，但作者怀疑其在连续噪声流场景中的误报行为存在显著差异，需要系统评估不同前端在真实自噪声下的表现，尤其是流式误报率这一关键工程指标。

Method: 在十词命令词表上训练一个小足迹KWS模型，采用一架四旋翼训练、另一架测试的跨平台设定。对比方法包括：经典噪声鲁棒前端（CMN、PCEN、谱减法）、测试时自适应，以及两种跟踪决策窗之前两秒内分频带自噪声底的前端——一种是减去噪声底（归一化），另一种是将噪声底作为第二输入通道馈入网络（条件化）。评估指标为逐片段准确率，以及在4.4小时连续旋翼噪声上的流式误报率。

Result: 逐片段准确率上，各前端平均提升2-3个点，在-15 dB时可提升至14个点，彼此差别不大。但在连续旋翼噪声上差异显著：归一化前端在同一阈值下误报频率约为普通log-mel的十倍，在每小时一次误报的预算下甚至低于基线（0 dB时-9个点）。条件化前端保持基线误报率并把增益转化为检测率（-10 dB下三个随机种子上+8个点）；叠加PCEN后取得最佳逐片段准确率与误报率，且电平锚定变体对麦克风增益不变。真实无人机+干扰源录音暴露了残余失效模式，即环境声与旁者语音，训练负样本可将其减半。

Conclusion: 条件化前端（尤其是叠加PCEN及电平锚定变体）在负信噪比自噪声场景下兼顾逐片段准确率与流式误报率，优于归一化前端。单一脚本可在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX飞行计算机上复现全部机载数值，完整流水线在CPU上每100 ms跳步仅耗3 ms，满足实时部署需求。

Abstract: A microphone on the airframe of a small multi-rotor UAV is dominated by rotor ego-noise, so spoken flight commands arrive at negative signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). We study small-footprint keyword spotting (KWS) for a ten-word command vocabulary under real ego-noise, training on one quadrotor and testing on another. Besides per-clip accuracy we measure the streaming false-alarm rate on 4.4 h of continuous rotor noise. We compare classical noise-robust front-ends (CMN, PCEN, spectral subtraction), test-time adaptation, and two front-ends that track the per-band ego-noise floor over the two seconds preceding the decision window and either subtract it (normalisation) or feed it to the network as a second input channel (conditioning). Per clip, all front-ends look alike: +2-3 points on average, up to +14 at -15 dB. On continuous rotor noise they differ sharply. Normalising front-ends fire about ten times more often than plain log-mel at the same threshold and end up below it at a budget of one false alarm per hour (-9 points at 0 dB). Conditioning keeps the baseline's false-alarm rate and turns its gain into detections (+8 points at -10 dB over three seeds); on top of PCEN it gives the best per-clip accuracy and false-alarm rate, and a level-anchored variant is also invariant to the microphone gain. Real drone+interferer recordings expose the remaining failure mode, environmental sounds and bystander speech, which training negatives halve. A single script reproduces all on-device numbers on the NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX flight computer, where the complete pipeline costs 3 ms per 100 ms hop on the CPU.

</details>


### [5] [Video-to-Music Generation for Gameplay Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31810)
*Felipe Marra,Lucas N. Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文构建SNES游戏视频-音乐数据集（217.6小时游戏视频、485小时纯配乐），训练将视频特征直接送入MusicGen解码器的编码器-解码器Transformer，比较T5、ViT、ViViT三种编码策略。冻结编码器优于或等同于微调版本，冻结ViViT综合最优；模型在客观指标上超越所有基线，听感评测中优于GVMGen、与OSSL相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频到音乐生成在电影和音乐视频领域已有进展，但游戏领域存在独特挑战：帧为渲染图形、音乐多为合成音频、配乐按关卡循环而非跟随屏幕事件。现有模型缺乏面向游戏场景的数据与验证。

Method: 1）构建新数据集：217.6小时Super Nintendo游戏视频与485小时干净配乐（无音效和旁白），通过音频指纹与游戏音频匹配。2）基于该数据集训练简单的编码器-解码器Transformer，将视频特征直接传给MusicGen解码器。3）对比三种编码策略：文本描述（T5）、独立帧（ViT）、时空块（ViViT），每种编码器在冻结与微调两种设定下测试，解码器始终微调。

Result: 冻结编码器在所有指标上均匹配或超越微调版本，冻结ViViT取得最佳整体效果。与SOTA基线相比，本模型参数量最多少18%，却在客观指标上全面超越基线；听感研究（N=96）中优于GVMGen，与OSSL表现相当。

Conclusion: 在游戏视频到音乐生成任务中，直接向MusicGen解码器传递冻结视频特征（尤其是ViViT时空编码）是一种高效且有效的方案，能够以更小参数量达到或超越现有基线。

Abstract: Video-to-music models have advanced considerably in the last few years, particularly in film and music video applications. In this paper, we investigate this problem in the video game domain, which introduces new challenges for these models: video frames are rendered graphics, music is mostly synthetic audio, and soundtracks loop across entire levels rather than following on-screen events. We introduce a new dataset of 217.6 hours of Super Nintendo (SNES) gameplay video paired with 485 hours of clean soundtracks, free of sound effects and voice-overs, matched to gameplay audio via audio fingerprinting. With this dataset, we train a simple encoder-decoder transformer that passes video features directly to a MusicGen decoder, comparing different encoding strategies: textual descriptions (T5), independent frames (ViT), or spatiotemporal patches (ViViT). Each encoder is tested both frozen and fine-tuned, while the decoder is always fine-tuned. Frozen encoders match or outperform their fine-tuned counterparts on every metric, and the frozen ViViT achieves the best overall results. We compare this model with state-of-the-art baselines using both objective metrics and a listening study (N = 96). Despite having up to 18\% fewer parameters, our model outperforms all baselines on objective metrics, surpasses GVMGen in the listening study, and performs comparably to OSSL.

</details>


### [6] [CORD-KWS: Calibrated, Order-Aware Detection for Open-Vocabulary Keyword Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31869)
*Ramesh Gundluru,Adarsh Arigala,Sri Rama Murty Kodukula*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CORD-KWS训练框架，通过校准检测头与帧级CTC目标弥补嵌入式KWS的分数校准与音素顺序信息缺失，在LibriPhrase上取得最优EER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇关键词检索需在无需重训练下检测任意关键词。基于交叉注意力的模型性能最优，但需对每个音频-关键词对进行成对交互并重复计算融合表示，计算开销大；基于嵌入的模型通过独立编码与相似度打分避免该开销，但在LibriPhrase-hard等困难基准上表现较差。作者旨在证明这一差距主要源于训练方式而非模型容量限制：对比学习只促使正确关键词排序高于负样本，却未显式校准绝对相似度分数，而固定阈值跨关键词检测恰恰依赖绝对分数。

Method: 提出CORD-KWS（Calibrated, Order-aware Detection KWS）训练框架，在保持原有编码器、余弦打分与O(1)关键词注册的前提下引入两个互补目标：(1) 校准检测头，直接监督绝对相似度分数；(2) 帧级CTC目标，保留音素顺序信息。

Result: 在LibriPhrase-easy与LibriPhrase-hard上分别取得0.43%和9.64%的EER，优于现有基于嵌入和基于交叉注意力的模型。

Conclusion: 嵌入式与交叉注意力式KWS之间的性能差距主要是训练问题而非容量问题；通过校准绝对分数并保留顺序信息，嵌入式模型可在低计算成本下超越交叉注意力模型。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary keyword spotting (KWS) must detect arbitrary keywords without retraining. Cross-attention-based models achieve state-of-the-art performance but require pairwise interaction between the audio and each keyword, recomputing the fused representation for every audio--keyword pair. Embedding-based models avoid this computational cost through independent encoding and similarity scoring, yet remain inferior on challenging benchmarks such as LibriPhrase-hard. We show that this gap is primarily a training issue rather than a limitation of model capacity. While contrastive learning encourages correct keywords to rank above negatives, it does not explicitly calibrate absolute similarity scores, which are critical for applying a fixed threshold across keyword detection. We propose Calibrated, Order-aware Detection KWS (CORD-KWS), a training framework that introduces two complementary objectives while retaining the same encoders, cosine scoring, and O(1) keyword enrollment: a calibrated detection head that supervises absolute scores, and a frame-level CTC objective that preserves phonetic order information. CORD-KWS achieves EERs of 0.43% and 9.64% on LibriPhrase-easy and LibriPhrase-hard, respectively, outperforming existing embedding- and cross-attention-based models.

</details>


### [7] [NVAlign: Direct-Gradient Optimization for Non-Verbal Control in Continuous Autoregressive Flow Matching Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31892)
*Qiaolin Wang,Pedro Sandoval-Segura,Anunaya Joshi,Edvardas Jurkonis,Jake Downie*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: NVAlign：面向连续自回归流匹配TTS的NVAlign直接梯度后训练框架，通过NV-ASR奖励模型与两步梯度代理提升非语言发声标签跟随精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代TTS虽能生成高自然度语音并支持内联非语言发声（NVV）标签，但对这些事件的精确控制仍具挑战。关键空白在于：连续自回归流匹配（autoregressive flow-matching）TTS架构缺乏成熟的后训练方法来控制非语言发声。

Method: 提出NVAlign直接梯度后训练框架：先在带NVV标注的语音上对TTS模型与NVV感知ASR（NV-ASR）模型做监督微调（SFT）；随后冻结NV-ASR作为奖励模型；采用两步梯度代理（two-step gradient surrogate）实现奖励经流匹配采样器的高效反向传播，联合更新自回归主干与声学流头；并引入保真度惩罚与参考速度正则以保持说话人相似度和语音质量。

Result: 在NVV-SuperBench与人类听测评估中，NVAlign相比SFT与Flow-GRPO基线提升了标签跟随准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接奖励梯度优化可有效改善连续自回归流匹配TTS中的非语言发声控制能力。

Abstract: While modern text-to-speech (TTS) systems generate highly natural speech and support inline non-verbal vocalization (NVV) tags, accurate control over these events remains challenging. A key gap is the lack of established post-training methods for non-verbal control in continuous autoregressive flow-matching TTS. To this end, we present NVAlign, a direct-gradient post-training framework for NVV tag-following in this architecture. We first perform supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of TTS models and an NVV-aware automatic speech recognition (NV-ASR) model on NVV-annotated speech, then freeze the NV-ASR model to serve as the reward model for post-training. A two-step gradient surrogate enables efficient reward backpropagation through the flow-matching sampler to jointly update the autoregressive backbone and acoustic flow head. Fidelity penalties and reference-velocity regularization help preserve speaker similarity and speech quality. Results from NVV-SuperBench and human listening evaluations show that NVAlign improves tag-following accuracy over SFT and Flow-GRPO baselines. These findings demonstrate that direct reward-gradient optimization can improve non-verbal control in continuous autoregressive flow-matching TTS. Audio samples are available at https://nvalign.github.io/.

</details>


### [8] [Duplex-MPE: Benchmarking Multi-Party Interaction in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31948)
*Chengqian Ma,Wenhao Feng,Weixuan Jin,Gaole Dai,Tianyu Xie,Yuexiao Ma,Zhaolu Kang,Xiangyu Zhao,Xiawu Zheng,Fei Chao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出Duplex-MPE基准，用于评估全双工语音助手在多人共享对话中何时应答、静默或停止说话的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音基准主要围绕单一指定用户评估轮流发言、打断处理和多轮对话，而忽视了助手参与多人共享会话的场景，无法衡量其在该场景下应答、静默与停止的决策能力。

Method: 构建包含2,000个场景的Duplex-MPE基准，每个场景含3至4名人类说话者和1个助手，且对同一请求分别提供显式与隐式称谓的配对版本；模型接收不含转录文本和话轮边界的连续对话音频。采用四项指标衡量：新应答发起、答案准确性、静默保持，以及人类自行解决问题时停止说话。评估五个开源语音系统（MiniCPM-o 4.5、Moshi、FLM-Audio、Voila、Freeze-Omni），并设置基于转录文本的Gemini 3.1 Pro作为参照。

Result: MiniCPM-o 4.5在三项评分能力上领先；其他系统频繁发言的同时可能出现答案不准确或无法保持静默的问题。基于转录文本的Gemini 3.1 Pro参照对显式请求的应答频率比隐式请求高64.3个百分点，而配对检验未在语音系统中检测到显著的应答率差异。

Conclusion: Duplex-MPE填补了多人共享对话下全双工语音助手应答决策评估的空白，揭示当前开源语音系统在隐式称谓理解、静默保持与适时停止方面仍存在明显不足。

Abstract: Real-time full-duplex speech models can listen while speaking, enabling natural interaction without rigid turn boundaries. Existing benchmarks evaluate turn-taking, interruption handling and multi-round dialogue, but largely centre on a designated user rather than an assistant participating in a shared conversation among several people. We introduce Duplex-MPE to evaluate when such an assistant should answer, remain silent or stop speaking. The benchmark contains 2,000 scenarios with three or four human speakers and one assistant, each paired across explicit and implicit addressing of the same request. Models receive continuous conversation audio without transcripts or supplied turn boundaries. Four scores measure fresh response initiation, answer accuracy, silence preservation and stopping when a human resolves a request. We evaluate five open-weight speech systems: MiniCPM-o 4.5, Moshi, FLM-Audio, Voila and Freeze-Omni. MiniCPM-o 4.5 leads on three scored capabilities, while frequent speech from other systems can coexist with inaccurate answers or failures to remain silent. A transcript-based Gemini 3.1 Pro reference responds 64.3 percentage points more often to explicit than implicit requests; paired tests detect no significant response-rate difference for the speech systems.

</details>


### [9] [VoiceNet: Fine-Grained Voice Understanding Beyond Emotion at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32016)
*Christoph Schuhmann,Robert Kaczmarczyk,Gollam Rabby,Felix Friedrich,Maurice Kraus,Gijs Wijngaard,Kourosh Nadi,Huu Nguyen,Kristian Kersting,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出VoiceNet——一个基于许可宽松的真实场景语音、人工标注的表征级语音表现理解基准，含情绪（40类）与说话风格（57属性）两个子集，并发布Emolia情绪标注语料；同时训练VoiceCLAP-Small/Large两个语音-文本对比基线，在VoiceNet-Emo上超越现有CLAP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表达性语音合成已超越表达性语音感知：系统能生成细粒度的声音表现，但缺乏可评分的公开基准。现有反向问题（感知/理解）基准多停留在6–9类基本情绪，且主要基于表演性语音，无法评估细粒度语音表现理解。

Method: 构建VoiceNet人工标注表征级基准，基于许可宽松的真实场景语音，含两个子集：VoiceNet-Emo采用40类情绪分类法、每项3位专家评分；VoiceNet-Ext为初步子集，标注57个说话风格属性（语速、声带紧张度、气声、音区等）。发布Emolia（Emilia语料库的情绪标注版本），含经精平衡的子集并辅以密集MOSS-Audio Thinking标注。训练两个语音-文本对比基线：110M参数的VoiceCLAP-Small（用于大规模快速数据筛选）与7B的VoiceCLAP-Large（追求SOTA性能）。

Result: 两个VoiceCLAP基线均优于现有CLAP基线，后者在VoiceNet-Emo上接近随机水平。在VoiceNet-Emo上，VoiceCLAP-Large与专家总体共识的一致性高于专家彼此间的一致性（此为与多数标签比较的结果，并非超越人类情绪感知的证据）。所有评测系统均为语音-文本嵌入模型，VoiceNet评估的是表征级属性识别与检索，而非端到端口语对话行为。

Conclusion: 将未经整理语音语料聚类、筛选为覆盖多样说话风格与情绪的子集仍是开放挑战，VoiceCLAP嵌入为此提供了有前景的工具。VoiceNet、Emolia与VoiceCLAP均已公开供研究使用。

Abstract: Expressive speech synthesis has outpaced expressive speech perception: systems now render fine-grained vocal performances that no public benchmark can score. Most benchmarks for this inverse problem stop at six to nine basic emotion categories, largely on acted speech. This paper introduces VoiceNet, a human-annotated representation-level benchmark for voice performance understanding on permissively-licensed in-the-wild speech. VoiceNet has two subsets: VoiceNet-Emo applies a 40-emotion taxonomy with three expert ratings per item, and VoiceNet-Ext, a preliminary subset, scores 57 talking-style attributes including speaking rate, vocal tension, breathiness, and register. The paper also releases Emolia, an emotion-annotated version of the Emilia corpus, with a curated rebalanced subset enriched by dense MOSS-Audio Thinking annotations. Two voice-text contrastive baselines train on this data: a 110M-parameter VoiceCLAP-Small for fast large-scale data filtering and a 7B VoiceCLAP-Large for state-of-the-art performance. Both outperform existing CLAP baselines, which sit near chance on VoiceNet-Emo. On VoiceNet-Emo, VoiceCLAP-Large aligns more closely with the aggregate expert consensus than individual experts agree with one another: a comparison against the majority label rather than evidence of surpassing human emotion perception. All systems evaluated here are voice-text embedding models: VoiceNet scores representation-level attribute recognition and retrieval, not end-to-end spoken-dialogue behaviour. Clustering and filtering uncurated speech corpora into subsets that span diverse talking styles and emotions remains an open challenge; VoiceCLAP embeddings offer a promising tool for this task. VoiceNet, Emolia, and VoiceCLAP are publicly available for research use.

</details>


### [10] [Tracing Decoder Artifacts for Compact Synthetic Speech Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32050)
*Yi Chen Liu,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级前端筛选器，利用语音生成器解码器引入的频谱伪影直接测量，实现低成本的合成语音预筛，配合大模型级联可大幅降低检测能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成语音检测的高精度方法越来越依赖大型预训练模型，对每条录音都调用成本过高；希望在保留大检测器的前提下，用一个廉价紧凑的前端筛选器过滤输入，仅将可疑录音转发给昂贵模型。

Method: 分析学习式上采样与逆短时傅里叶变换合成产生的可预测频谱伪影，直接在生成波形上测量其强度；由于伪影强度随生成器变化，将解码器引导的频谱测量与短时频谱形状、时间变化等互补描述子结合，输入紧凑的梯度提升树模型进行分类。

Result: 在七种语音生成器和两种真人语音源上，等错误率为0.021%，模型存储约151 KiB；作为1.15亿参数检测器的模拟级联第一级时，在0.050%合成语音漏检率下，估计检测能耗降低84.4%。

Conclusion: 解码器引导的声学证据可用于低成本前端筛选，在不替换大型检测器的前提下显著降低整体检测成本与能耗。

Abstract: Recent advances in speech synthesis and voice cloning have increased the need for reliable synthetic-speech detection, yet high-accuracy detectors increasingly rely on large pretrained models that are costly to invoke on every recording. Rather than replacing such detectors, we investigate a compact front-end screen that processes all inputs cheaply and forwards only suspicious recordings for more expensive analysis. To enable lightweight screening without a large learned encoder, we exploit spectral traces introduced by speech-generation operations. We analyze how learned upsampling and inverse short-time Fourier transform synthesis can produce predictable spectral artifacts and measure their presence directly in generated waveforms. Because the strength of these artifacts varies across generators, we combine decoder-guided spectral measurements with complementary descriptors of short-time spectral shape and temporal variation in a compact gradient-boosted tree. Across seven speech generators and two human-speech sources, the proposed screen achieves an equal error rate of 0.021\% with an estimated model storage of 151 KiB. When used as the first stage of a simulated cascade with a 1.15-billion-parameter detector, it reduces estimated detection energy by 84.4\% while operating at a 0.050\% synthetic-speech miss rate, demonstrating the potential of decoder-guided acoustic evidence for low-cost front-end screening.

</details>


### [11] [Do Audio LLMs Listen Before They Act? Diagnosing Acoustic-Context Gating in Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32536)
*Yanjie Zhang,Nanchen Hu,Yushi Sun*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 VGBench（1018 项）诊断基准，评估音频语言模型在侧谈、自语与说话人切换场景下的"动作级被指向性"；现有模型与免训练适配普遍难以在说话人切换时抑制动作（最高静默率仅 14%），而经监督后训练的 VoxGate 可将切换命令静默率提升至 91.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频语言模型能识别语音命令并调用工具，但智能体需先判断声学与对话上下文是否值得采取行动。现有评测缺乏对"是否应当行动"这一动作级门控能力的系统诊断，尤其缺少对说话人切换、侧谈、自语等情境的受控测试。

Method: 构建 VGBench 基准：1018 个条目，统一动作空间（静默/工具调用/自然语言回答）。说话人切换对固定指定词，通过声源、距离渲染与时间边界实现受控的"佩戴者→旁观者"迁移。评测 6 个原始音频 LLM 与 3 种免训练适配；再以 VoxGate 作为后训练案例，采用监督训练与探索性 GRPO 阶段，并做因子化控制分析。

Result: 原始模型与免训练适配常能识别目标工具，却极少在切换条件下抑制动作，最高原始静默率仅 14%。VoxGate 监督训练对切换命令静默率达 91.3%，并对所有近距离佩戴者命令及纯文本控制均选择正确工具。GRPO 阶段切换性能相近，侧谈准确率由 68.4% 升至 70.9%，自语静默率由 52.0% 升至 60.0%。因子化控制显示独立的声源变化效应，且对远场操纵的敏感性随声学渲染而异。

Conclusion: 该基准测量的是多线索的声学上下文门控能力，而非孤立的说话人身份识别。

Abstract: Audio language models can recognize spoken commands and invoke tools, but an agent must first decide whether the acoustic and conversational context warrants action. We introduce VGBench, a 1,018-item diagnostic benchmark for action-level addressedness across side-talk, self-talk, and speaker-switch scenarios. Each item uses a shared action space comprising silence, a tool call, and a natural-language answer. Speaker-switch pairs hold the specified words fixed while source, distance rendering, and a temporal boundary define a controlled wearer-to-bystander shift. Six raw Audio LLMs and three training-free adaptations often identify the target tool yet rarely withhold action under this shift; the highest raw switch mute rate is 14%. We then use VoxGate as a post-training case study. Supervised training mutes 91.3% of switched commands while choosing the correct tool for all nearby wearer commands and text-only controls. An exploratory GRPO stage has similar switch performance; side-talk accuracy rises from 68.4% to 70.9%, and self-talk muting from 52.0% to 60.0%. Factorized controls identify an independent source-change effect, while sensitivity to the far-field manipulation varies across acoustic renderings. The benchmark therefore measures multi-cue acoustic-context gating rather than isolated speaker identity.

</details>


### [12] [SAGE: Semantic Audio Generative Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32755)
*Francesco Brigante,Luca Cerovaz,Davide Marincione,Giorgio Strano,Luca Zhou,Emanuele Rodolà,Michele Mancusi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: SAGE是一种仅用公开音乐数据训练的紧凑型变分自编码器，通过蒸馏预训练音频-文本模型的嵌入来塑造潜空间，在重建质量、语义结构和推理速度三方面取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音频自编码器在重建质量、潜空间语义结构和推理速度之间存在三难困境，通常只能兼顾其中一到两个方面。

Method: 提出SAGE（语义音频生成编码器），一个105M参数的紧凑型变分自编码器，仅用公开音乐数据训练，通过蒸馏预训练音频-文本模型的嵌入来塑造其潜表示。

Result: 推理成本与Stable Audio Open相当，达到比自身大8倍、慢4倍的SAME-L的听测质量，并在客观感知和分布重建指标上超过两者；同时在全部19项潜语义探测任务（域内和域外）上达到最优。

Conclusion: SAGE是一种轻量级音频自编码器，在所评估方法中实现了三难困境的最佳平衡，兼具高重建保真度、最先进的语义结构和快速推理。

Abstract: Audio autoencoders compress waveforms into compact latent representations that serve as the interface between raw audio and downstream models. Current systems navigate a three-way trade-off between reconstruction quality, semantic structure of the latent space, and inference speed, typically favoring one or two of these at the expense of the others. This paper introduces SAGE, Semantic Audio Generative Encoder: a compact variational autoencoder, trained solely on publicly available music, that shapes its latent by distilling embeddings from a pretrained audio-text model. This 105M-parameter model runs at the inference cost of Stable Audio Open and reaches the listening-test quality of SAME-L, an autoencoder 8x larger and 4x slower, while surpassing both on objective perceptual and distributional metrics of reconstruction. Furthermore, it sets the state of the art on all nineteen probing tasks of latent semantics, in domain and out of domain. These results establish SAGE as a lightweight audio autoencoder that strikes the best balance of the three-way trade-off among those we evaluate, combining high reconstruction fidelity, state-of-the-art semantic structure, and fast inference.

</details>


### [13] [DEFINE: Exemplar-Guided Accent Control for Zero-Shot TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32777)
*Ambuj Mehrish,Abhinaba Roy,Alex Ivanov,Tawsif Ahmed,Dorien Herremans*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: DEFINE 是一个端到端零样本 TTS 框架，通过分离的音色与口音示例解耦说话人身份和口音，并用单一推理期权重连续控制口音强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本 TTS 虽能从短参考音频复现未见说话人，但通常将说话人身份与口音纠缠在同一参考中，无法独立控制。

Method: 基于 F5-TTS，采用参数高效 LoRA 适配；用示例编码器将短口音示例映射到条件空间，并以学习到的口音原型进行监督；说话人身份与目标口音分别由不同音频示例条件化，推理时用单一引导权重控制口音强度，无需口音标签或后处理波形转换。

Result: 在已见口音上，提高口音引导权重使口音探针准确率从 6.5% 提升至 19.6%；单一 DEFINE 模型在已见和域外口音上可匹配双模型 TTS-语音转换级联的口音迁移性能，同时获得更高的说话人相似度和相当的预测语音质量，但在留出（held-out）口音上未达到该效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在单一零样本 TTS 模型中，说话人身份与口音可基于音频示例独立控制，且对训练中未见的口音亦具备一定泛化能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS) can reproduce an unseen speaker from a short reference recording, but typically entangles speaker identity and accent within the same reference. We introduce DEFINE, an end-to-end framework that decouples these factors by conditioning speaker identity and target accent on separate audio exemplars. A single inference-time guidance weight continuously controls accent strength without retraining. Built on F5-TTS with parameter-efficient LoRA adaptation, DEFINE maps short accent exemplars into a conditioning space using an exemplar encoder supervised through learned accent prototypes, requiring neither accent labels at inference time nor post-synthesis waveform conversion. On seen accents, increasing accent guidance improves accent-probe accuracy from 6.5% to 19.6%. More importantly, a single DEFINE model generalizes accent control beyond its training accent set: on seen and out-of-domain accents, though not on held-out accents, it matches the accent transfer performance of a two-model TTS-voice-conversion cascade while achieving higher speaker similarity and comparable predicted speech quality. These results demonstrate that speaker identity and accent can be independently controlled from audio exemplars within a single zero-shot TTS model, including for accents unseen during training.

</details>


### [14] [Finding Emotions Where They Belong: Rethinking Audio Emotion Recognition through Masked Temporal Affective Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32804)
*Abdelrahman Mohamed,Lars Kai Hansen,Zheng-Hua Tan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文将音频情感识别（AER）重新定义为时间情感定位（TAG）任务，构建带时间标注的数据集并提出M-TAG训练目标，微调出EMO-TAG模型，实验表明现有基线模型情感识别尚可但时间定位能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AER对整段录音分配单一标签，当存在多说话人和多个情感事件时，标签的时间范围模糊不清，无法定位情感发生的具体语音片段。

Method: 将AER重构为时间情感定位（TAG），把情感关联到有时间边界的语音片段和声调描述；整理现有情感数据集的时间标注版本，构建含2-4个情感语音片段（含重叠语音）的录音。提出M-TAG监督目标：在全序列语言建模基础上，结合注意力掩码下的情感与时间戳交叉熵损失；掩码变换情感标签token可见上下文以减少捷径依赖，时间戳损失加入距离感知权重惩罚较大时间误差。据此微调出EMO-TAG模型。

Result: 在情感识别与情感时间定位上，将EMO-TAG与三个AER及音频-语言基线（Flamingo-Next、Audio-Reasoner、AffectGPT）对比。结果显示现有模型虽情感识别表现有竞争力，但情感时间定位能力有限。

Conclusion: 以更长格式和标准语言建模目标训练会同时损害情感识别与时间定位性能，且声调描述可能为情感预测提供捷径；M-TAG通过掩码与距离感知时间戳损失缓解这些问题，但现有模型在情感时间定位上仍有明显不足。

Abstract: Audio emotion recognition (AER) typically assigns a single label to an entire recording, leaving the temporal scope of that label ambiguous when multiple speakers and affective events are present. We address this limitation by reformulating AER as a Temporal Affective Grounding (TAG) task that associates emotions with temporally bounded speech spans and vocal tone descriptions. To support this formulation, we curate temporally annotated versions of existing emotion recognition datasets and construct recordings containing two to four affective speech spans, including overlapping speech. Training in this longer format with a standard language-modeling objective can degrade both emotion recognition and temporal grounding performance, while tone descriptions can provide shortcuts for emotion prediction. To address these challenges, we introduce Masked Temporal Affective Grounding (M-TAG), a supervised training objective that combines full-sequence language modeling with emotion and timestamp cross-entropy losses under attention masking. The masking varies the context visible to emotion-label tokens to reduce reliance on shortcuts and improve generalization, while the timestamp loss incorporates a distance-aware weight to penalize larger temporal errors. We evaluate EMO-TAG, a model fine-tuned using our dataset and objective, on emotion recognition and affective temporal-grounding against three AER and audio-language baselines: Flamingo-Next, Audio-Reasoner, and AffectGPT. Our results show that existing models achieve limited affective temporal-grounding despite competitive emotion recognition performance.

</details>


### [15] [Whisper-Flash: Acoustically Conditioned Parallel Drafting for Faster Whisper Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32869)
*Huapeng Zhou,Huayu Wang,Junkai Wu,Kangqi Wang,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Whisper-Flash 提出一种两层草稿模型，利用语音识别中“待写字词已被说出”的特性，从编码音频与已接受的解码状态一次前向预测 8 个 token，在输出完全一致的前提下加速 Whisper 解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper 编码器并行读取整段语音，但解码器逐 token 自回归生成，主导了推理时间；推测解码可缩短该循环且不改变输出，但需要一个高效草稿模型。论文动机源于语音识别的一个特性：尚未写出的词其实已经包含在音频中，因此草稿可直接依据音频进行预测。

Method: 提出 Whisper-Flash：一个两层草稿器，读取 Whisper 的编码音频表示与已被接受的解码器状态，在单次前向中提出 8 个候选 token，再由原模型一次前向并行验证，保持输出不变。

Result: 在完整 LibriSpeech 测试集上，Whisper-Flash 每秒可处理的音频量为贪心解码的 3.16×/2.85×，且输出完全相同；在 batch size 最高 96 以及温度采样下仍保持加速。消融实验表明，直接访问音频信息最为关键。

Conclusion: 利用音频中已包含未来词这一先验，轻量草稿器可显著加速 Whisper 推理而不损失精度，且加速在较大批量和采样解码下依然稳健；音频直接访问是性能提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Whisper is a widely used encoder-decoder model for speech recognition. Its encoder reads an utterance in one parallel pass, but its decoder writes the transcript one token at a time, which dominates inference time. Speculative decoding shortens such loops without changing their output: a small drafter guesses several upcoming tokens, and the original model verifies them all in one forward pass. We present Whisper-Flash, a two-layer drafter built on a property of speech recognition: the words still to be written have already been spoken. It reads Whisper's encoded audio and accepted decoder states and proposes eight tokens in a single forward pass. On the complete LibriSpeech test sets, Whisper-Flash processes $3.16\times/2.85\times$ as much audio per second as greedy decoding with identical outputs, and it remains faster at batch sizes up to 96 and under temperature sampling. Ablations show that direct access to the audio matters most.

</details>


### [16] [SCISSOR: Score-Conditioned Instrument Source Separation for Orchestral Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33265)
*Yiheng Lu,Hao-Wen Dong*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出SCISSOR——一种乐谱条件化的管弦乐音源分离方法，利用对齐乐谱为每个音源构造逐帧查询（query），与共享音频表示匹配，并通过乐器与背景槽位的softmax竞争分配重叠时频证据，从而在处理共享音高、谐波与音色干扰时恢复乐器声部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管弦乐分离中，共享音高、谐波与相近音色使音源身份难以辨识。已有的乐谱知情方法通常将钢琴卷帘（piano roll）直接拼接至音频特征后再预测掩码，这种方式难以在音符缺失时保持音源身份，且对乐谱损坏较为敏感。

Method: SCISSOR使用对齐乐谱为每个音源生成逐帧查询（frame-wise query），每个查询与共享音频表示进行匹配；通过乐器槽位与背景槽位上的softmax，对重叠的时频证据进行联合分配。该查询机制在乐谱中音符缺失时仍能保持乐器身份。模型在SynthSOD以及少量URMP与PHENICX-Anechoic录音上训练。

Result: 在留存的真实录音上，SCISSOR取得最高平均SDR。仅用SynthSOD训练时，在SynthSOD与零样本PHENICX-Anechoic上领先，并在零样本URMP上优于其纯音频对照模型。相比所评估的基于乐谱的基线，SCISSOR在乐谱损坏情况下性能下降更少。

Conclusion: 以乐谱生成逐帧查询并进行槽位竞争分配的框架，能有效利用乐谱信息提升管弦乐音源分离性能，且在跨数据集泛化与乐谱损坏鲁棒性方面均具优势。

Abstract: Orchestral separation recovers instrument sections from mixtures in which shared pitches, harmonics, and timbres obscure source identity. An aligned score provides instrument labels, note pitches, and activity times. A score-informed approach appends piano rolls to audio features before mask prediction. We introduce SCISSOR (Score-Conditioned Instrument Source Separation for Orchestral Recordings), which uses the score to form a frame-wise query for each source. Each query matches a shared audio representation, and a softmax over instrument and background slots jointly assigns overlapping time-frequency evidence. The queries retain instrument identity even when notes are missing from the score. After training on SynthSOD and a small set of URMP and PHENICX-Anechoic recordings, SCISSOR achieves the highest average SDR on held-out real recordings. With SynthSOD-only training, it leads on SynthSOD and zero-shot PHENICX-Anechoic, and improves on its audio-only control on zero-shot URMP. SCISSOR also degrades less under score corruption than the evaluated score-based baselines.

</details>


### [17] [Identity-Assisted Association of Unordered DOA Estimates for Neural Speech Source Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33373)
*Bing Yang,Di Liang,Xiaofei Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种身份辅助关联方法，将说话人身份嵌入与空间特征融合，通过统一神经网络跟踪器实现语音源轨迹的稳定跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音源跟踪面临间歇性语音、空间距离接近及复杂声学条件下的数据关联歧义问题，导致DOA估计难以映射到一致的说话人轨迹。

Method: 将说话人身份嵌入直接作为模型输入的补充线索与空间特征结合，利用长期时不变的声音身份特征与短期空间线索连续性维持身份一致性；设计统一神经跟踪器，其中时间自注意力模块捕捉各源的时间演化，源自注意力模块区分相互竞争的源轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提神经跟踪器在缓解语音源跟踪中的关联混淆方面具有优越性。

Conclusion: 身份辅助关联结合统一神经跟踪器可有效提升语音源跟踪的关联可靠性。

Abstract: Tracking speech sources remains a challenge due to ambiguous data association arising from intermittent speech, close spatial proximity, and complex acoustic conditions. To address these issues, we propose an identity-assisted association that maps unordered direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimates to speaker-consistent source trajectories for reliable speech source tracking. Specifically, speaker identity embeddings are directly integrated into the model input as a complementary cue to spatial features. This enables maintaining identity consistency by combining long-term time-invariant vocal identity characteristics with the short-term continuity of spatial cues. To effectively process these heterogeneous inputs while accommodating their distinct characteristics, we design a unified neural tracker. Within this model, time self-attention modules capture the temporal evolution of each source, while source self-attention modules distinguish between competing source tracks. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed neural tracker in mitigating association confusion for speech source tracking.

</details>


### [18] [What Survives the Codec Shift: Pooled No-Vocals Residuals for Speech Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33375)
*Jiajun Xu,Menglu Li,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 比较12种声学表示在codec shift下的判别性，发现XLS-R与no-vocals残差统计互补，并提出双视角检测器MN-P，整体EER降54.2%、未见codec降60.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音合成从基于声码器转向神经codec，使语音深伪检测器（尤其依赖语音导向表示的）泛化更难。生成机制变化后，哪些声学表示仍保留判别信息尚不清楚。

Method: 用共享的低容量线性分类器对12种声学表示进行对比，以识别codec shift下的有效证据；基于发现提出MN-P双视角检测器，通过自适应门控融合utterance级pooled no-vocals表示与token级XLS-R特征。

Result: 分层XLS-R在合并测试集上最优；pooled no-vocals残差统计在未见codec条件下最优。MN-P相对最佳重训练SOTA系统整体EER降低54.2%，codec未见条件降低60.9%，在不同检测器后端上增益一致。

Conclusion: pooled no-vocals残差统计为跨生成条件的语音深伪检测提供了有效互补证据，双视角融合可显著提升泛化性能。

Abstract: The transition from vocoder-based to neural-codec speech synthesis makes generalization more difficult for speech deepfake detectors, particularly those relying on speech-oriented representations. It remains unclear which acoustic representations retain discriminative information when the generation mechanism changes. We therefore compare 12 acoustic representations using a shared low-capacity linear classifier to identify effective evidence under codec shift. The analysis shows that hierarchical XLS-R leads on the pooled test set, while pooled no-vocals residual statistics perform best on the unseen-codec condition, revealing complementary behavior across generation conditions. Building on this finding, we propose MN-P, a dual-view detector that integrates an utterance-level pooled no-vocals representation with token-level XLS-R features through adaptive gating. The proposed MN-P reduces EER by 54.2% overall and by 60.9% on the codec-unseen condition relative to the best-performing retrained state-of-the-art system, with consistent gains across different detector backends. These results indicate that pooled no-vocals residual statistics provide effective complementary evidence for cross-generation speech deepfake detection.

</details>


### [19] [CORA: A Protocol for Diagnosing Boundary Robustness in Text-to-Audio Retrieval under Query Reformulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33433)
*Jae Min Woo,Kyongmin Kong,Bogyung Jeong,Minjeong Kim,HaeJun Yoo,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CORA诊断协议，将同一目标音频的caption改写为5种意图保持的查询形式，用新指标RankDrop揭示Recall@k掩盖的检索失败，发现鲁棒性与目标边界优势的保持相关，而非文本空间位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2A检索评估仅使用caption风格查询，但同一用户意图可有多种表达形式；Recall@k这类指标无法暴露查询改写下的失败，需要一种意图保持的诊断协议与更敏感的度量。

Method: CORA将每条源caption改写为五种意图保持形式（Command、Question、Indirect、Key phrase、Statement），固定目标音频不变，通过跨查询形式追踪同一目标定义RankDrop指标；并用Pearson相关系数r分析RankDrop与文本空间移动、Target Alignment Loss、Target Boundary Margin Degradation的关系，并在OEA检索器上验证。

Result: RankDrop与原始文本空间移动仅弱相关（r=0.084），而与Target Alignment Loss（r=0.508）和Target Boundary Margin Degradation（r=0.615）强相关；OEA检索器呈现同样模式，RankDrop更多由边界退化解释（r=0.472/0.478）而非查询位移（r=0.084/0.046）。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的T2A检索要求在查询改写下保持目标相对竞争音频的边界优势；CORA与RankDrop提供了比Recall@k更能暴露脆弱性的诊断视角。

Abstract: Text-to-Audio (T2A) retrievers are typically evaluated with caption style queries, but the same user intent can be expressed in many forms. We introduce CORA (Caption-Offset Retrieval for Audio), a caption anchored diagnostic protocol that rewrites each source caption into five intent preserving forms (Command, Question, Indirect, Key phrase, and Statement) while fixing the target audio. By tracking the same target across query forms, CORA defines RankDrop, a metric revealing failures hidden by Recall@k. Using Pearson's correlation coefficient r, we find that RankDrop is weakly associated with raw text space movement (r=0.084), but strongly associated with Target Alignment Loss and Target Boundary Margin Degradation (r=0.508 and r=0.615). The same pattern appears in OEA retrievers, where RankDrop is better explained by boundary degradation (r=0.472/0.478) than by query movement (r=0.084/0.046). Overall, these results suggest that robust T2A retrieval requires preserving the target's boundary advantage over competing audio under reformulation.

</details>


### [20] [Mask-Induced Displacement in Audio XAI via Logit Trajectory Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33486)
*Nico García-Peguinho,David Kelly,Fabrizio Smeraldi,Anna Xambó Sedó*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出logit空间的轨迹分解，检验扰动式XAI中"填充值可忽略"假设，发现填充并非声学中性且掩码下大量输出位移为离轴分量，致归因结果依赖装置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扰动式音频XAI通过掩蔽频谱区域并归因输出变化来估计特征重要性，但通常默认填充（mask replacement）是可忽略的。本文旨在系统检验这一被广泛接受但缺乏验证的假设。

Method: 提出logit空间轨迹分解：将输出位移分为沿轴分量（连接完全填充/遮挡频谱与完全保留原图的方向）与离轴分量（垂直方向位移）。在3种填充、3种音频分类器、1100条AudioSet片段上进行评估。

Result: 无任何填充在全遮挡下是声学中性的：Zero填充激活静音，Gaussian Noise填充激活宽带噪声。部分掩码下41-77%的输出位移为离轴，残差方向在掩码保留比例上稳定，且对每种"模型-填充"组合具有特异性。归因方法因采样与加权策略不同而不同程度暴露于离轴位移，体现装置依赖性。当离轴残差稳定且低维时，其结构可用于缓解。

Conclusion: 填充值假设不成立，"填充中性"是一个隐含且被违反的假设；扰动式归因结果具有装置依赖性，而离轴残差的稳定性与低维性为纠偏或缓解提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Perturbation-based XAI methods for audio classifiers often estimate feature importance by masking spectrogram regions and crediting output changes to the retained signal. Yet they typically assume the fill (the mask replacement) is negligible. We propose logit-space trajectory decomposition to examine this assumption. An on-axis component captures output along a line connecting the fully filled (occluded) spectrogram to the fully retained original; an off-axis component captures perpendicular displacement. We evaluate across three fills, three audio classifiers, and 1,100 AudioSet clips. We demonstrate that no fill is acoustically neutral under full occlusion: Zero fill activates silence and Gaussian Noise fill activates broadband noise. Under partial masking, 41-77% of output displacement is off-axis, with the direction of the residual stable across mask retention fraction and specific to each model-fill combination. Attribution methods are unevenly exposed to off-axis displacement through their sampling and weighting strategies, revealing apparatus-dependence. Where the off-axis residual is stable and low-dimensional, its structure affords mitigation.

</details>


### [21] [DuraS2ST: Chain-of-Thought and Reinforcement Learning for Duration-Aligned Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33742)
*Yayue Deng,Dingdong Wang,Yuxuan Hu,Jinyu Li,Yanqing Liu,Yuanyuan Wang,Weidong Chen,Helen M. Meng,Shujie Liu,Xixin Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 DuraS2ST，一种时长对齐推理框架，让单一语音语言模型先生成显式思维链规划目标措辞与音素长度，再合成语音词元，实现翻译质量与时长一致性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频配音等时间敏感场景下的语音到语音翻译（S2ST）不仅要求语义保真和说话人保持，还需严格时长一致性以避免视听错位。但现有 S2ST 系统生成目标语音时缺乏显式时序规划，时长控制成为未解决的难题。

Method: 1）DuraS2ST 框架：单一语音语言模型先生成显式思维链（CoT），规划目标措辞与音素长度，再合成对应语音词元；2）构建 DuraSet-440K 高质量时长对齐 CoT 语料用于监督初始化；3）多模态多维强化学习优化，引入时长边界奖励（Duration Margin Reward）平衡翻译质量与时长一致性，以及模态感知奖励归因（Modality-Aware Reward Attribution）将奖励分配给合适的词元跨度。

Result: 在 CVSS-T 数据集上，DuraS2ST 在翻译质量与时长一致性之间取得良好平衡，优于有竞争力的开源与商业基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式时长规划的 CoT 推理与多维强化学习，DuraS2ST 有效缓解 S2ST 中的时长控制难题，适用于视频配音等时间敏感应用，并公开项目页面。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) in time-sensitive applications such as video dubbing requires not only semantic fidelity and speaker preservation, but also strict duration consistency to avoid audio-visual misalignment. However, existing S2ST systems largely generate target speech without explicit temporal planning, making duration control an unresolved challenge. We introduce DuraS2ST, a duration-aligned reasoning framework that enables a single speech language model to first generate an explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) for planning target wording and phonetic length, and then synthesize the corresponding speech tokens. To support this paradigm, we construct DuraSet-440K, a high-quality duration-aligned CoT corpus for supervised initialization. We further optimize the model with multi-modal multi-dimensional reinforcement learning, using a Duration Margin Reward to balance translation quality and duration consistency, and Modality-Aware Reward Attribution to assign rewards to appropriate token spans. Experiments on CVSS-T show that DuraS2ST achieves a strong balance between translation quality and duration consistency, outperforming competitive open-source and commercial baselines. Project page: https://github.com/Mia11939/DuraS2ST.

</details>


### [22] [Transformer-based Neural Beamforming for Real-Time Speech Enhancement on Smart Low-Power Hearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33755)
*Luca Bompani,Marco Fariselli,Giovanni Oltrecolli,Francesco Conti*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在MCU级可听戴设备上实时运行的多通道Transformer神经波束成形流水线,采用六麦克风与三阶段混合精度方案,实现15ms帧延迟、优异语音增强指标与约20小时电池续航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能可听戴设备需要在低功耗、资源受限的MCU上实现准确、高效、低延迟的空间波束成形以增强语音并抑制噪声与干扰,但在严格实时约束下处理多路输入源极具挑战。

Method: 采用六麦克风与三阶段混合精度方案(float32 MVDR、int8 CNN、float16 Transformer),其中CNN估计MVDR权重,轻量Transformer进行逐帧校正;通过将权重估计与波束成形时间切片并行,并加入语音活动检测模块在静音期旁路流水线。

Result: 单帧延迟15ms,每564ms刷新完整CNN权重;STOI 97.65%、SI-SNR 20.26 dB、宽带PESQ 3.676,平均功耗45.9mW;SAD准确率98.5%、每次推理0.62mJ;在100mAh电池下续航可达约20小时,超过16小时全天目标。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在MCU级设备上部署了实时多通道基于Transformer的神经波束成形流水线,在精度、延迟与功耗间取得良好平衡,验证了可听戴设备端侧实时神经波束成形的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate, efficient, and low-latency spatial beamforming is a key component in emerging smart hearable devices, enhancing speech while suppressing noise and interference. However, handling multiple input sources under strict real-time constraints poses significant challenges for the low-power, resource-constrained microcontroller units (MCUs) used in hearables. We present an optimized methodology for the real-time execution of a neural-network-based minimum variance distortionless response (MVDR) beamformer on MCUs. Using six microphones and a three-stage mixed-precision scheme (float32 MVDR, int8 CNN, float16 Transformer), the pipeline pairs a CNN that estimates the MVDR weights with a lightweight Transformer that applies a per-frame correction. By time-slicing weight estimation with beamforming, it achieves a 15~ms per-frame latency while refreshing a complete set of CNN-derived weights every 564~ms. The deployed mixed-precision pipeline attains a short-time objective intelligibility (STOI) of 97.65\%, a scale-invariant signal-to-noise ratio (SI-SNR) of 20.26~dB, and a wideband PESQ of 3.676 at an average power of 45.9~mW. A speech activity detection (SAD) module (98.5\% accuracy, 0.62~mJ per inference) bypasses the pipeline during silence; under realistic deployment conditions, the system exceeds the 16~h all-day target on a 100~mAh battery, with an estimated lifetime of up to $\sim$20~h. To our knowledge, this is the first real-time multi-channel Transformer-based neural beamforming pipeline deployed on an MCU-class device.

</details>


### [23] [Tokens Change, Structure Endures: Spectral Watermarking for Generated Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33774)
*Kanghwi Lee,Kyeongseok Jeong,Jeongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 Redwing：一种抗重分词（retokenization）的免训练 token 级音频水印方法，利用重分词中 token 替换关系构建图的拉普拉斯基，联合优化嵌入与检测函数，在 Moshi 上八次 Mimi 重合成后达到 80.7% TPR（1% FPR），远超 KGW（8.3%）与 WMAR（≤7.3%），并在三种其他神经编解码器与 TTS 模型上泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成语音的溯源需求推动了神经音频水印的发展，但现有方法多依赖单独训练的水印生成器。token 级水印作为免训练替代方案直接在生成过程中嵌入，其主要缺陷是重分词：将生成语音解码为波形再重新编码会改变 token 身份，从而削弱水印。因此需要一种对重分词变化鲁棒的设计。

Method: Redwing（REtokenization-Durable Watermarking IN Generation）首先采集重分词条件下观察到的 token 替换关系，构建替换图；对该图求拉普拉斯算子得到一组基，使得易相互替换的 token 在该基上取值相近。在此基上联合优化嵌入函数与检测函数，目标是在重分词过程中保留水印信号，同时约束嵌入失真以及未加水印语音上检测器的波动（变异性）。

Result: 在 Moshi 全双工系统上，经过八次连续 Mimi 重合成后，Redwing 在标定 1% FPR 下达到 80.7% TPR，而 KGW 为 8.3%，WMAR 至多 7.3%。在另外三种神经编解码器上八次传递后同样取得最高 TPR（77.5%–93.0%）。增益也可泛化到 TTS 模型，且语音质量代价与 KGW 接近。

Conclusion: 结果表明重分词不仅是噪声来源：其转移结构可被利用为设计原则，用于构建鲁棒的 token 级水印。Redwing 验证了以替换图拉普拉斯基为核心的设计在多种编解码器与生成模型上的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Watermarking is a promising tool for establishing the provenance of AI-generated speech. While many neural audio watermarking methods rely on a separately trained watermark generator, token-level watermarking is a training-free alternative that operates directly during generation. Its main weakness is retokenization: decoding generated speech to a waveform and encoding it again can change token identities and erode the watermark. To make the watermark robust to these changes, we propose Redwing, REtokenization-Durable Watermarking IN Generation. It builds a graph from the token substitutions observed under retokenization, whose Laplacian yields a basis that assigns similar values to tokens likely to substitute for one another. Over this basis, embedding and detection functions are jointly optimized to preserve watermark signal through retokenization while limiting embedding distortion and detector variability on unwatermarked speech. On the Moshi full-duplex system, after eight consecutive passes of Mimi resynthesis, Redwing achieves 80.7% TPR at a calibrated 1% FPR, compared with 8.3% for KGW and at most 7.3% for WMAR. It also has the highest TPR after eight passes through three other neural codecs (77.5-93.0%), and the gains generalize to TTS models at a speech-quality cost close to that of KGW. These results show that retokenization is not merely a source of noise: its transition structure can be exploited as a design principle for robust token-level watermarking.

</details>


### [24] [Controlling Speaking Rate in Autoregressive TTS via Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33810)
*Francesco Verdini,Antonis Asonitis,Aref Farhadipour,Marzieh Razavi,Pierre-Edouard Honnet,Vijeta Avijeet,Juan Pablo Zuluaga Gomez*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重训练的自回归TTS语速控制方法：通过分析解码器块，发现语速轴、中性工作点与逐步强度尺度，在推理时将激活在该轴上的投影钳制为固定标量，从而实现对语速的稳定调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归TTS系统虽能合成自然语音，但训练完成后对语速几乎无法控制。现有加法式引导在极慢端会失效，因此需要一种在推理阶段即可调控语速、且稳定、保说话人身份、跨架构泛化的方法。

Method: 对解码器块进行激活分析，从合成的时拉伸/时压缩语音中学习语速方向，并确定中性工作点与逐步强度尺度；推理时将某单一解码器块激活沿该语速轴的投影钳制为固定标量。与标准加法式引导作对比。

Result: 钳制法在三个测试系统上均保持稳定，而加法式引导在极慢端失效；中等目标下更优规则因模型而异。该方法基本保持说话人身份，跨模型架构泛化，并在客观与人类评估中维持高自然度。此外，语速信息在多层可解码，但仅在中等深度窗口内可因果调控，并在公开Seed-TTS-Eval基准上验证有效。

Conclusion: 语速可在推理时通过钳制单一解码器块的语速轴激活进行调控，无需重训练；该方法稳定、自然、保说话人身份且跨架构通用，因果可控性集中于中等深度层。

Abstract: Autoregressive text-to-speech (TTS) systems synthesize natural speech but, once trained, offer little control over speaking rate. We show that speaking rate can be steered at inference time, without retraining, by clamping a single decoder block's activation along a discovered speed axis. A decoder-block analysis recovers the rate axis, a neutral operating point, and a per-step intensity scale; at inference, the activation's projection onto this axis is set to a fixed scalar. Learning this direction from synthetically time-stretched and time-compressed speech yields rate control that largely preserves speaker identity, generalizes across model architectures, and maintains high naturalness in objective and human evaluations. Unlike standard additive steering, which breaks at the slow extreme, clamping remains stable on all three systems tested; at moderate targets, the better rule depends on the model. Finally, we show that rate information is decodable across layers but causally steerable only within a mid-depth window, and demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on the public Seed-TTS-Eval benchmark.

</details>


### [25] [Uncovering shortcut learning in audio classifiers by discovering recurring concepts in temporal explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34030)
*Cecilia Bolaños,Luciana Ferrer,Magdalena Fuentes*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套流水线，通过挖掘音频分类器时间解释中的重复概念来揭示捷径学习，并在AudioSet Strong数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习数据集中事件间的相关性会导致捷径学习，模型基于相关事件的出现来预测目标事件。当这些相关性源于数据收集伪影（虚假相关）时，模型在实际应用中表现不佳。因此需要一种方法来发现并审计音频分类器中的潜在捷径。

Method: 提出一条流水线：首先隔离出对分类器决策起解释作用的音频片段；然后使用一组大型音频-语言模型（Large Audio-Language Models）对这些片段进行描述（caption）；接着用大型语言模型（Large Language Model）提取其中重复出现的概念；最终由人工审计这些概念以发现潜在的捷径学习。

Result: 在从AudioSet Strong整理的数据集上评估该框架，并控制了虚假相关存在与否的条件。结果表明该方法能可靠地揭示已学到的捷径，例如模型依赖"笑声"的存在来预测"掌声"。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过挖掘音频分类器时间解释中的重复概念，可以有效地审计并揭示音频分类器中的捷径学习，为实践中发现模型的虚假相关依赖提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Correlations between events in machine learning datasets may result in shortcut learning, where models learn to predict the target event based on the presence of a correlated event. When these correlations are spurious -- arising from data collection artifacts -- models are likely to perform poorly in practice. We propose a pipeline to uncover shortcut learning in audio classifiers by discovering recurring concepts in their temporal explanations. Specifically, we isolate audio segments that explain classifier decisions, caption them with an ensemble of Large Audio-Language Models, and use a Large Language Model to extract recurring concepts. The resulting concepts can be audited by humans to uncover potential shortcut learning. We evaluate our framework using datasets curated from AudioSet Strong, controlling for the presence or absence of spurious correlations. Results show that this approach reliably uncovers learned shortcuts, such as the model relying on the presence of "laughter" to predict "applause".

</details>


### [26] [Towards Interpretable Framework for Neural Audio Codecs via Sparse Autoencoders: Exploration toward Age, Gender, and Accent Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34052)
*Shih-Heng Wang,Tiantian Feng,Aditya Kommineni,Huang-Cheng Chou,Bowen Yi,Xuan Shi,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究在波形层面用稀疏自编码器（SAE）的引导（steering）方法，探查五种神经音频编解码器（NAC）中年龄、性别与口音等说话人特质信息的编码方式，发现所选维度可诱导目标方向的特质预测偏移，但伴随质量下降与跨编解码器/特质/方向的不稳定响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器广泛用于语音生成与音频-语言建模，但其如何编码说话人特质信息仍缺乏理解；已有工作仅在任务层面用SAE分析NAC中的口音信息，未覆盖波形层面及其他特质（年龄、性别、口音）。

Method: 将SAE分析从任务层面扩展到波形层面，针对年龄、性别与口音三类特质，利用SAE引导（steering）策略：识别与特质相关的稀疏激活维度，修改其激活值，并对重建语音进行评估，同时设置随机维度基线（在Mimi上）进行对照。

Result: 在五种NAC上，引导所选维度可诱导说话人特质预测产生目标方向的偏移；Mimi上的随机维度基线偏移更小，支持所选维度的相关性。但响应在不同编解码器、特质与引导方向间存在差异，且增强引导强度并未持续放大预期偏移；引导总体上提高了词错误率（WER）并降低了预测感知质量。

Conclusion: SAE能够捕获NAC中可引导的说话人特质信息，但引导带来的质量退化表明，需要更好地将特质相关信息与其他信息分离。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs (NACs) are widely used in speech generation and audio-language modeling, yet how they encode speaker-trait information remains poorly understood. Prior work applied sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to investigate accent information in NACs through task-level analysis. Here, we extend this analysis to the waveform level and to age, gender, and accent, using SAE steering to probe trait-related information in sparse activations. We identify trait-associated dimensions, modify their activations, and evaluate the resulting reconstructed speech. Across five NACs, steering the selected dimensions induces target-directed shifts in speaker-trait predictions. A random-dimension baseline on Mimi produces smaller shifts, supporting the relevance of the selected dimensions. However, responses vary across codecs, traits, and steering directions, and increasing steering strength does not consistently amplify the intended shifts. Steering also generally increases word error rates and lowers predicted perceptual quality. These findings suggest that SAEs capture speaker-trait information in steerable activations, while the accompanying quality degradation highlights the need to better separate trait-related information from other information.

</details>


### [27] [SAIL: Spatial Audio Intelligence with Large Language Models via Disentangled Acoustic-Spatial Encoding and Dual-Stream Q-Former](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34347)
*Zhengding Luo,Jinyang Wu,Haozhe Ma,Yanghao Zhou,Woon-Seng Gan,Wenwu Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出SAIL框架，通过分离声学与空间流并保持声源级对应关系，提升多声源空间音频理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间音频LLM多采用声学与空间特征的早期融合及与声源无关的token表示，难以在多声源场景中保持单个声音事件与其空间属性的对应关系。

Method: SAIL包含三部分：1) 解耦空间音频Transformer，将Mel频谱图与双耳相位差特征表示为独立的声学流与空间流；2) 声源判别性任务查询，为每个声源学习事件、方向和距离信息；3) 双流Q-Former，按声源槽组织声学与空间查询，将两流与LLM对齐。

Result: 与早期融合基线相比，SAIL在双声源声音事件检测、方向与距离估计及空间推理任务上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的、具有声源判别性的音频表示对多声源空间理解与推理至关重要。

Abstract: Spatial audio large language models (LLMs) enable embodied agents, wearable assistants, and immersive systems to recognize sound events, localize sources, and reason about their spatial relationships. However, existing spatial audio LLMs often rely on early fusion of acoustic and spatial features and source-agnostic token representations. These designs make it difficult to preserve the correspondence between individual sound events and their spatial attributes, particularly in multi-source scenes. To address this limitation, we propose SAIL, a Spatial Audio Intelligence framework with LLMs that preserves acoustic-spatial structure and source-level correspondence from audio encoding to LLM alignment. SAIL introduces a Disentangled Spatial Audio Transformer that represents Mel-spectrogram and interaural phase difference features as separate acoustic and spatial streams. Source-discriminative task queries further learn event, direction, and distance information for each source. A Dual-Stream Q-Former then aligns the two streams with the LLM using acoustic and spatial queries organized by source slots. Compared with the early-fusion baseline, SAIL achieves consistent improvements in dual-source sound event detection, direction and distance estimation, and spatial reasoning. These results demonstrate the importance of structured, source-discriminative audio representations for multi-source spatial understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [28] [Prox-Friendly Log-Magnitude Prior on Complex-Valued Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34445)
*Kazuki Matsumoto,Keidai Arai,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出EPILOG正则化器，通过对辅助变量进行正则化间接对复值信号的对数幅度施加先验，并推导其逐变量近端算子，用于语音去混响中的倒谱域稀疏促进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人耳听觉感知对幅度近似呈对数关系，因此对数变换在音频信号处理中至关重要。然而，将信号先验（如谐波结构）直接在log-magnitude域中融入由标准近端分裂算法求解的优化问题仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出EPILOG（Exponential Penalty for Imposing priors on LOG-magnitude）正则化器，通过正则化一个与log-magnitude相关联的辅助变量，间接对复值信号的对数幅度施加先验；进而推导其逐变量近端算子，并基于这些算子构建近端分裂算法。

Result: 语音去混响实验表明该正则化器有效，尤其在促进倒谱域稀疏性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: EPILOG为在log-magnitude域中融入信号先验提供了一种可行途径，通过辅助变量正则化与近端算子推导，实现了与标准近端分裂算法的兼容，并在去混响任务中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: The logarithmic transform is essential in audio signal processing since human auditory perception is approximately logarithmic with respect to magnitude. However, directly incorporating prior knowledge about signals (e.g., harmonic structure) in the log-magnitude domain into optimization problems solved by standard proximal splitting algorithms remains challenging. To address this issue, this paper proposes a novel regularizer termed EPILOG (Exponential Penalty for Imposing priors on LOG-magnitude). EPILOG indirectly imposes prior knowledge on the log-magnitude of a complex-valued signal through regularization of an auxiliary variable that is shown to be linked with the log-magnitude. Furthermore, we derive its variable-wise proximity operators and develop a proximal splitting algorithm using these operators. Experiments on speech dereverberation demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed regularizer, particularly in promoting cepstral-domain sparsity.

</details>


### [29] [SEmoEdit: Probing and Harnessing the Editability of Pre-trained Speech Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34648)
*Tianxin Xie,Pengfei Zhang,Kai Jiang,Zelin Zhao,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 SEmoEdit——首个免训练（training-free）语音情感编辑框架，通过将情感编辑建模为源情感与目标情感之间的动态速度传输（dynamic velocity transport），直接利用预训练 TTS 模型的生成动力学实现情感编辑，并发布 600 例的评测集 SEmoEditBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于训练的语音情感编辑方法通常需要大量任务特定训练且不稳定。作者希望探究：大规模 TTS 预训练模型的生成动力学能否被直接操控，从而实现免训练的情感编辑。

Method: 作者先构建受控测试集，沿生成轨迹系统诊断预训练 flow-matching 与混合 TTS 模型的可编辑性；基于发现提出 SEmoEdit，将情感编辑形式化为源情感与目标情感间的动态速度传输，统一情感替换、情感擦除与连续情感插值三种操作，无需参数更新与任务特定优化。

Result: 预训练 TTS 模型在情感上具有显著可编辑性，但该能力依赖架构与轨迹，且易被早期 flow-matching 步骤破坏；跨说话人情感迁移会额外携带情感之外的声学属性。SEmoEdit 在 SOTA 模型与骨干网络上效果显著、适用性广，优于基于训练与激活引导（activation-steering）的方法。

Conclusion: 预训练语音流蕴含丰富而潜在的情感编辑能力，为实际应用提供了有益指导；代码、基准与音频样例已开源。

Abstract: Existing training-based speech emotion editing methods often require substantial task-specific training and can be unstable. This motivates us to investigate whether the pretrained generative dynamics of large-scale text-to-speech (TTS) models can be directly manipulated for training-free emotion editing. To answer this question, we probe the editability of pretrained flow-matching and hybrid TTS models by constructing a controlled test set and systematically diagnosing editing effects along the generative trajectory. Our analysis reveals that pretrained TTS models are substantially editable in emotion, but such editability is architecture- and trajectory-dependent and can be disrupted by early flow-matching steps, while cross-speaker emotion transport carries additional acoustic attributes beyond emotion. To address these limitations, we propose SEmoEdit, the first training-free framework that formulates emotion editing as dynamic velocity transport between source and target emotions, enabling robust, flow-based speech emotion editing directly within pretrained TTS models. SEmoEdit unifies three core operations: emotion replacement, emotion erasure, and continuous emotion interpolation, requiring neither parameter updates nor task-specific optimization. To systematically evaluate these capabilities, we introduce SEmoEditBench, a dataset comprising 600 editing cases, and conduct extensive experiments across state-of-the-art (SOTA) models and backbones. Our results show that SEmoEdit is highly effective and broadly applicable, outperforming existing training-based and activation-steering methods. Ultimately, this work reveals that pretrained speech flows possess rich, latent emotion-editing capabilities, providing useful guidance for real applications. Code, benchmark, and Audio samples are available at https://github.com/imxtx/SEmoEdit.

</details>


### [30] [Unsupervised Speech Enhancement via Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34662)
*Diego Caviedes-Nozal,Liang Xu,Rasmus Kongsgaard Olsson,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出"输入条件漂移"（input-conditioned drifting）方法，用于非配对无监督语音增强。通过锚点编码器（anchor encoder）提供对输入特征的似然约束、关键编码器（key encoder）对检索帧重加权以条件化先验，从而在保留干净语料先验的同时将输出重新锚定到退化输入，无需标签或配对数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于漂移（drifting）的非配对语音增强方法仅优化干净语音的边缘先验（marginal prior），导致增强器逐渐丢失输入的语义内容与说话人身份，造成内容与音色退化。

Method: 提出输入条件漂移：1）锚点编码器（anchor encoder）通过拉向输入特征来提供缺失的似然；2）关键编码器（key encoder）通过重新加权检索帧来条件化先验；3）使用免训练（training-free）的编码器选择准则。整个过程不依赖标签或配对数据。

Result: 在 VoiceBank-DEMAND 上，词错误率（WER）降至 10.1%（未处理为 11.7%），说话人相似度从 0.490 恢复至 0.879；该方法部分迁移至 WSJ0-REVERB 的去混响任务：内容指标改善，但渲染质量未提升。

Conclusion: 输入条件漂移有效缓解了非配对无监督语音增强中内容与说话人身份流失的问题，在保持无监督、无配对训练的前提下显著提升内容保真度与说话人相似度，去混响任务仅部分受益。

Abstract: This paper addresses unsupervised speech enhancement in the unpaired setting using drifting methods, where training relies on separate collections of degraded and clean audio without corresponding pairs. While recent drifting approaches enable unpaired training, they do so at a heavy cost: because the objective optimizes only a marginal prior over clean speech, the enhancer gradually loses the input's linguistic content and speaker identity. To fix this, we introduce input-conditioned drifting. We preserve the pull of the clean corpus while re-tethering the output to the degraded input via two mechanisms: an anchor encoder supplies the missing likelihood by pulling toward the input's features, and a key encoder conditions the prior by re-weighting retrieved frames. Neither requires labels or paired data. Using a training-free encoder selection criterion, Word Error Rate on VoiceBank-DEMAND falls to 10.1% (unprocessed: 11.7%), speaker similarity recovers from 0.490 to 0.879, and the recipe transfers in part to dereverberation on WSJ0-REVERB: content improves, rendering quality does not.

</details>


### [31] [On Temporal Binding in Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34806)
*Paul Primus,Gerhard Widmer*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究用机械可解释性方法分析三个开源大音频语言模型，发现事件特定时间位置信息在中间模态融合层的“事件名称表示”中集中编码，呈低维弯曲的相对时间轨迹，并可因果性地影响粗略时间推理，但不影响精确的起始时间戳预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频录音的时间结构推理要求大音频语言模型把声音事件与其时间位置关联起来；理解其内部机制是诊断失败、定位需改进模型组件的前提。

Method: 采用机械可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）分析三个开源LALMs：定位时间信息在哪些层/表示中编码，刻画其几何结构（低维弯曲相对时间轨迹），并通过沿该轨迹对事件名称表示进行干预（steering）来检验因果作用。

Result: 三个模型中，事件特定位置均在中间模态融合层集中于事件名称表示；这些表示以低维弯曲的相对时间轨迹编码粗略事件位置；沿轨迹操控可系统性改变before/after判断；但同样的干预不能可靠改变预测的起始时间戳（onset timestamps）。

Conclusion: 该研究表明事件名称表示对粗略时间推理具有因果贡献，并揭示粗略时间推理与精确度量事件定位依赖不同的机制。

Abstract: Reasoning about temporal structure of audio recordings requires Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) to associate sound events with their temporal position. Understanding the underlying mechanisms is a first step toward diagnosing failures and identifying model components that may need improvement. Using mechanistic interpretability, we investigate how temporal information is represented and bound to sound events in three open-source LALMs. We find that across all three, event-specific location becomes concentrated in event name representations at intermediate modality integration layers. These representations encode coarse event position along a low-dimensional, curved relative time trajectory. Steering event name representations along this trajectory systematically shifts before/after beliefs, providing evidence that these representations contribute to coarse temporal reasoning. In contrast, the same interventions do not reliably shift predicted onset timestamps, suggesting that coarse temporal reasoning and precise metric event localization rely on distinct mechanisms.

</details>


### [32] [SincDPNet: Interpretable Raw-Waveform Bathroom Activity Recognition for Assistive Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34907)
*Debolina Chowdhury,Suman Samui,Sujoy Saha*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出浴室声学事件数据集（21,387个标注片段，覆盖5种环境）与紧凑原始波形分类器SincDPNet，并通过多目标贝叶斯优化在验证性能与模型规模间权衡，最佳模型14,040参数达80.2%准确率，最紧凑模型2,848参数达75.7%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浴室声学事件识别可支持环境辅助生活，适用于不宜持续视频监控的场景；但实际部署要求模型紧凑、可解释，并对录音环境变化具有鲁棒性。

Method: 构建七类浴室声学事件数据集，含21,387个标注片段、跨五种环境录制；提出SincDPNet，在原始波形上使用可学习sinc滤波器组（每个滤波器由两个频率参数控制，通带可直接以赫兹检视）后接深度可分离卷积主干；在重叠窗口划分前按录制会话与环境分离训练/验证/测试，以减少房间特定泄漏；采用多目标贝叶斯优化评估24种配置的验证性能—模型规模权衡。

Result: 最佳模型在留出环境上达到80.2%准确率、0.760 macro-F1，参数量14,040；紧凑N_f=25配置仅2,848参数，达到75.7%准确率、0.661 macro-F1、0.716 MCC。学习滤波器与混淆模式分析表明，频谱重叠导致水相关事件混淆，而Door与Walker/Crutch的误分类还反映其瞬态时间结构相似。

Conclusion: SincDPNet以原始波形输入、可解释sinc前端和小规模参数实现了具有竞争力的浴室声学事件识别性能，并提供可控的性能—规模折中；混淆分析显示频谱重叠与瞬态时间结构相似性是主要误差来源。

Abstract: Bathroom acoustic-event recognition can support ambient assisted living in settings where continuous video monitoring is undesirable. However, practical deployment requires models that are compact, interpretable, and robust to changes in the recording environment. This work introduces \dataset{}, a seven-class bathroom acoustic-event dataset containing 21{,}387 annotated clips recorded across five environments, and proposes SincDPNet, a compact raw-waveform classifier with a learnable sinc filter bank followed by a depthwise-separable convolutional body. Each sinc filter is controlled by two frequency parameters, allowing the learned passbands to be inspected directly in hertz while keeping the front end small. To reduce room-specific leakage, recording sessions and environments are separated before overlapping windows are assigned to the training, validation, and test partitions. We further use multi-objective Bayesian optimization as a design tool to examine the validation performance--model-size trade-off across 24 configurations. The selected designs span different operating points: the best-performing model achieves 80.2\% accuracy and 0.760 macro-F1 with 14{,}040 parameters, while the compact $N_f=25$ configuration uses only 2{,}848 parameters and achieves 75.7\% accuracy, 0.661 macro-F1, and 0.716 MCC on the held-out environment. Analysis of the learned filters and confusion patterns shows that spectral overlap contributes to confusion among water-related events, while the \textit{Door}/\textit{Walker/Crutch} errors also reflect similarities in their transient temporal structure.

</details>


### [33] [JazzSAMBA: A Synchronous and Asynchronous Multi-take Band Audio Dataset of Jazz Standards for Live Music Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34931)
*Phillip Long,Jacob Nguyen,Jace Hosto,Gage Hosto,Jett Takazawa,Fares Nofal,Sebastian Stade,Nithya Shikarpur,Julian McAuley,Cheng-Zhi Anna Huang,Stephen Brade,Aleksandra Teng Ma*
