  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温关联与宏观热力学量之间的关系此前仅有线性面积律，其隐含了与温度无关的松散上界，无法准确反映热退耦行为；需要更紧的标度关系以匹配宏观热力学预期。

Method: 对局域晶格哈密顿量的热态，针对任意二分 A|B 及临界逆温 β* 以下的 β，证明量子互信息满足以边界尺寸 |∂_AB| 为度量的 β² 面积律，其中 f̃(β) 在 β→0 时保持有限。

Result: 证明了 I_β(A,B) ≤ f̃(β)β²|∂_AB|；该二次标度使退耦后的非平衡自由能与界面总能量均正比于界面面积，并在高温下随 β 消失。

Conclusion: 高温下互信息与关联分别以二次和线性方式随逆温度被抑制，与宏观热力学量的热退耦预期一致，改进了高温关联与宏观热力学之间的标度关系。

Abstract: We prove a $β^{2}$ area law for the quantum mutual information of thermal states of local lattice Hamiltonians: for any bipartitioning $A|B$ and all inverse temperatures $β$ below a critical value $β^{*}$, we show $\mathcal{I}_β(A,B) \leqslant \tilde{f}(β)\, β^{2}\,|\partial_{AB}|$, where $|\partial_{AB}|$ denotes the size of the boundary region and $\tilde{f}(β)$ remains finite as $β\to 0$. This quadratic scaling improves the relationship between high-temperature correlations and macroscopic thermodynamics. While a linear area law implicitly permits a loose, temperature-independent upper bound on extractable work and binding energy, the quadratic scaling ensures that the nonequilibrium free energy available after decoupling regions and the total energy of the interface both are proportional to the area of the interface of $A|B$ and vanish as $β$ at high temperatures. These results show that the high-temperature mutual information and correlation are suppressed, respectively, quadratically and linearly in inverse temperature, which are consistent with the expected thermal decoupling in macroscopic thermodynamic quantities.

</details>

Abstract: Graph coloring is a well-known NP-complete problem with applications in scheduling, register allocation, frequency assignment, and network optimization. Quantum computing offers the potential for polynomial or even super-polynomial speedups in certain problem instances, yet practical quantum graph coloring methods must carefully balance qubit resources, circuit depth, and constraint enforcement. We proposed a solver agnostic quantum framework for $K$-coloring. Along with a novel encoding and efficient constraint implementation for its exact coloring exploiting Grover search and almost optimal coloring by the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm \emph{(QAOA)}, and \emph{Quantum Annealing}. Unlike using $O(NK)$ qubits as in \emph{SOTA}, we reduced the qubit requirements to $O(N \log_2 K)$, along with optimized comparator circuits enforcing adjacency constraints. Further symmetry-based graph reduction is incorporated as an optional preprocessing step to further reduce the instance size before quantum execution.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出BLS-FALQON算法，通过回溯线搜索减少测量开销，保持电路深度不变，总测量量比SO-FALQON降低37.7%。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 受回溯线搜索（BLS）理论启发，提出BLS-FALQON算法，通过BLS机制确定控制系数，避免SO-FALQON的额外二阶系数测量。

Conclusion: BLS-FALQON在保持与SO-FALQON相当电路深度的同时显著降低测量开销，且在真实量子硬件上有效。

Abstract: Feedback-based ALgorithm for Quantum OptimizatioN (FALQON) is a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for solving combinatorial optimization problems, which circumvents classical parameter optimization but requires a deep quantum circuit. To reduce circuit depth, Arai et al. proposed second-order FALQON (SO-FALQON), achieving the best depth reduction among existing approaches. However, SO-FALQON brings a 2.3 times per-step measurement overhead, as it needs to calculate an additional second-order control coefficient. In this paper, inspired by the Backtracking Line Search (BLS) theory, we propose another method called BLS-FALQON, which not only reduces circuit depth to a comparable extent, but also achieves fewer measurements than SO-FALQON. Numerical simulations on max-cut problem with 8 to 20 vertices demonstrate that BLS-FALQON reduces the total measurement count by 37.7% compared to SO-FALQON, while maintaining a comparable circuit depth. Furthermore, we conduct real quantum hardware experiments on the Tianyan-176 quantum computer, which uses the zuchongzhi2 superconducting quantum processor, confirming that BLS-FALQON remains effective under real quantum hardware conditions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有私密容量的强逆结论多依赖较弱的准则或受限于特殊信道，缺乏针对一般可退化信道的、基于单一联合误差准则的指数强逆。本文旨在为任意有限维可退化量子信道建立更普适、更精确的指数强逆定理，并统一处理关联与安全性的刻画。

Method: 采用单一误差准则：实际"消息—估计—环境"态与理想态（均匀分布、完全关联且与环境独立的理想态）之间的非保真度。核心工具是隐私检验的重叠界：两个共享维度 d_C 公共子系统的接收方布局，其重叠至多 d_C/M（M 为密钥规模），且与屏蔽维度无关。将该界与 arXiv:2608.01308 的有符号平均构造及到对称态的单滤波约化相结合；用高斯滤波把公共接收子系统替换为可控维度系统，借助量子边信息近似恢复原关联，该滤波保持精确交换对称性，并用均匀平滑熵估计约束任意关联信道输入下的输出维度。允许任意混合态编码器与任意集体解码器。

Result: 在严格高于信道最大相干信息的任意速率下，到理想态的优化保真度随信道使用次数指数衰减；当理想环境态固定为实际环境边缘态时，同一界仍成立。对反可退化信道，在同一联合准则下得到零速率处的指数强逆；由此推出量子擦除信道在其整个参数范围内私密容量的指数强逆。

Conclusion: 本文以单一非保真度准则统一证明了可退化量子信道私密容量的指数强逆，核心重叠界与高斯滤波约化技术较通用，可自然延伸到反可退化信道及量子擦除信道，为量子私密通信的强逆理论提供了更普适的工具与结论。

Abstract: We prove an exponential strong converse for the unassisted private capacity of every finite-dimensional degradable quantum channel. We use a single error criterion: the infidelity between the actual message-estimate-environment state and an ideal state consisting of uniformly distributed, perfectly correlated messages that are independent of the environment. At every rate strictly above the channel's maximum coherent information, the optimized fidelity to this ideal state decays exponentially in the number of channel uses. The same bound holds when the ideal environment state is fixed to the actual environment marginal. The proof applies to arbitrary mixed-state encoders and arbitrary collective decoders. Its main operator ingredient is an overlap bound for privacy tests: two receiver placements with a common subsystem of dimension $d_C$ have overlap at most $d_C/M$, where $M$ is the key size, independently of both shield dimensions. We combine this bound with the signed averaging construction presented in arXiv:2608.01308 and a single-filter reduction to symmetric states. A Gaussian filter replaces the common receiver subsystem by a system of controlled dimension from which the original correlations can be approximately recovered using quantum side information. The filter preserves exact exchange symmetry, and a uniform smooth-entropy estimate bounds its output dimension for arbitrary correlated channel inputs. We also obtain an exponential strong converse at zero for antidegradable channels under the same joint criterion and, consequently, for the private capacity of the quantum erasure channel throughout its parameter range.

</details>

Result: 带外束缚态驱动的非马尔可夫动力学导致储能出现单频振荡；在弱至中等耦合强度下经失谐控制可实现近单位充电效率，条件为电池到边界距离n小于充电器到边界距离m的三倍（n<3m），该约束源于镜面诱导反馈；而平均充电功率随n-m指数衰减。

Abstract: The waveguide-integrated architecture holds promise for scalable on-chip integration of a charger, a transmission channel, and a quantum battery in a modular, independently optimizable design. We investigate a minimal charging configuration consisting of two emitters coupled to a semi-infinite waveguide and identify the optimal charging mechanism, wherein non-Markovian dynamics driven by out-of-band bound states give rise to single-frequency oscillations of the stored energy. We show that near-unity charging efficiency is achievable at weak to moderate coupling strengths via detuning control, provided that the battery-to-boundary separation $n$ is less than three times the charger-to-boundary separation $m$ ($n<3m$)---a constraint imposed by the mirror-induced feedback---while the average charging power decays exponentially with the spatial separation $n-m$. These findings offer useful insights for monolithic solid-state quantum energy storage and transport.

### [92] [Beyond NISQ Assumptions: One-time Memory in the Classically Accessible Random-Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32603)
*Boyang Chen,Tianren Liu,Luojian Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在经典可访问随机预言机模型（CAROM）下构造了高效的一次性记忆（OTM）协议，仅用BB84态，通信量二次，并对最多 $2^{\ell/2-1}-1$ 次经典随机预言机查询的量子敌手达到关于 $n$ 指数小的模拟优势。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用经典可访问随机预言机模型（CAROM，如[BDF+11]和[AK22]），限制敌手只能对随机预言机进行经典查询；在该模型下构造高效模拟安全的一次性记忆（OTM）协议，协议仅使用BB84态。

Result: 对 $\lambda$ 比特消息和参数 $n=n(\lambda)$、$\ell=\ell(\lambda)$，构造使用 $n\ell$ 个量子比特和 $(n+2)\lambda$ 个经典比特（通信量二次）；对至多 $2^{\ell/2-1}-1$ 次经典随机预言机查询的量子敌手，其模拟优势至多 $(n+3)(3/4)^n$，即关于 $n$ 指数小。

Conclusion: CAROM假设对NISQ量子敌手有充分动机，且在早期容错量子计算机存在时仍可能合理；在此之下可实现高效模拟安全的一次性记忆，且模拟优势关于 $n$ 指数小。

Abstract: Quantum information enables many cryptographic primitives that are impossible in the classical world. A line of works has developed cryptographic protocol under the assumption that quantum adversaries are restricted to noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) computing power, enabling strong one-time functionalities. But the advent of early fault-tolerant quantum computers eras will allow deeper logical quantum circuits, calling into questions the applicability of these NISQ-based assumptions.
  In this work, we adapt the classically accessible random oracle model (CAROM) as in [BDF+11] and [AK22], in which adversaries are only allowed to classically query the random oracle. The restriction is well motivated for NISQ quantum adversaries and may remain plausible in the presence of early fault-tolerant quantum computers. Then, we show that an efficient simulation-secure one-time memory (OTM) is possible under CAROM. Our protocol uses only BB84 states and has quadratic communication: for a $λ$-bit message and integer-valued parameters $n=n(λ)$ and $\ell=\ell(λ)$, the construction uses $n\ell$ qubits and $(n+2)λ$ classical bits, and for any quantum adversary with at most $2^{\ell/2-1}-1$ classical queries to the random oracle, its simulation advantage is at most $(n+3)\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^n.$ Hence exponentially small simulation advantage in $n$.

</details>


### [93] [Fewer Qubits, Better Choices: Coupling-Aware Sub-QUBO Selection for Quantum-Assisted Traffic Zone Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32627)
*Qianwen Guo,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出，近端量子优化器在分解大规模QUBO问题时常用的“单变量翻转打分”选择规则在局部最优处失效，并提出基于二阶展开的选点方法，在交通网络实验中显著优于随机选择，且收益主要来自选择而非后端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端量子优化器只能处理约百个二值变量，需将大规模QUBO分解为硬件规模的子问题，同时固定其余变量。现有变量选择通常按单变量翻转带来的目标变化排序，但当当前解在单变量翻转下已最优时，该规则不再有信息量：所有得分都只是代价，真正改进来自被忽略的成对交互。因此需要更好的子问题变量选择方法。

Method: 论文分析单变量翻转打分规则的失效，并采用二阶展开，将变量选择转化为奖赏收集最密子图（prize-collecting densest-subgraph）选择问题；该问题可用贪心算法在变量数线性时间内求解。同时给出两个无需求解器的界，用于夹逼所选子集内可实现的改进。实验在芝加哥和费城交通网络上进行，并考察几何邻接与路网连通性、子问题规模、量子设备求解比例及编译约束。

Result: 在芝加哥和费城交通网络上，使用16变量子问题的选择规则优于使用64变量的随机选择，说明设备容量翻两番也无法弥补较弱的选择。将几何邻接替换为路网连通性后，优势扩大1.6–1.9倍。固定选择轨迹时，在120量子比特设备上求解子问题的比例从0变到1，最终目标值在六位有效数字内不变，表明收益来自选择而非后端。硬件可行性受耦合项限制：120变量问题在7,260项时无法编译，在5,118项时可成功编译；编译时间随项数近似二次增长并主导执行成本，达1,752秒，而量子处理仅469秒。

Conclusion: 在硬件可及规模下，量子优化分解的性能提升主要来自变量选择规则而非量子后端；基于二阶交互的选择更有效，但其硬件可行性受耦合项数量和编译开销限制。

Abstract: Near-term quantum optimizers accommodate roughly a hundred binary variables, requiring large Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization problems to be decomposed into hardware-sized subproblems while other variables remain fixed. Variables are typically selected by ranking the objective changes from individual flips. We show that this rule is uninformative once the incumbent is optimal under single-variable flips: every score represents a cost, while remaining improvements depend on overlooked pairwise interactions. A second-order expansion instead yields a prize-collecting densest-subgraph selection problem, solvable greedily in time linear in the number of variables, and two solver-free bounds bracketing the improvement achievable within a selected subset. On transportation networks from Chicago and Philadelphia, our rule with 16-variable subproblems outperforms random selection with 64, demonstrating that quadrupling device capacity cannot compensate for weaker selection. Replacing geometric adjacency with road-network connectivity widens the margin by a factor of 1.6-1.9. With the selection trace fixed, varying the fraction of subproblems solved on a 120-qubit device from zero to one leaves the final objective unchanged to six significant figures. At tested hardware-accessible sizes, gains therefore arise from selection rather than the backend. Hardware feasibility is limited by coupling terms: 120-variable problems fail to compile with 7,260 terms but succeed with 5,118. Compilation scales almost quadratically with term count and dominates execution cost, reaching 1,752 seconds versus 469 seconds of quantum processing.

</details>


### [94] [Exact Gaussian Entanglement Dynamics and Initial-State Control in Coupled Parametric Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32709)
*Roberto Bernal-Jaquez,Hector H. Hernandez-Hernandez,Alexander Schaum,Guillermo Chacon-Acosta*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性耦合谐振子在量子光学与连续变量量子信息中普遍存在，其纠缠产生的解析刻画、纠缠度量的选择以及初始态归属（可分离制备态 vs 已纠缠的耦合基态）常被混淆，需要一套归一化明确、判据与度量严格区分的分析框架。

Method: 采用固定简正模变换将双模系统解耦为两个参量振子，对每个振子用 Lewis-Riesenfeld 不变量与 Ermakov-Pinney 振幅精确求解，构造升降算符与 Lewis-Riesenfeld 相位；对不变量真空输入导出物理双模协方差矩阵的闭式表达，计算对数负度 E_N=½ arcosh(X/2)，其中无量纲不变量 X≥2 度量两简正模的相对相空间形变；对 Duan-Simon 判据做归一化显式的局部辛优化比较，并与正部分转置阈值对照；进一步用 SU(1,1) 表述将 2E_N 解释为 SU(1,1)/U(1) 圆盘上两条简正模压缩轨迹的双曲分离。

Result: 对不变真空输入得到物理协方差矩阵闭式解，对数负度仅依赖单一不变量 X，E_N=½ arcosh(X/2)；经局部辛优化后，Duan 判据与正部分转置在本纯态交换对称高斯族中共享同一纠缠阈值，但优化后的 Duan 量只是见证而非独立度量，满足 D_opt/D_sep = e^{-E_N}；严格区分真正可分离的乘积制备态与耦合哈密顿量已纠缠的基态，仅前者用于声称纠缠产生；正弦刚度调制下可分离输入在远离共振处关联有界、在参量共振附近持续增长；数值显示初始宽度与啁啾构成两个独立且实验可及的纠缠控制参数；SU(1,1) 表述表明纠缠同时由相对压缩幅度与相对压缩角决定，并给出幅度近似相同时仅靠角度即可产生强纠缠的区域。

Conclusion: 该工作为时变双线性耦合谐振子的纠缠动力学提供了精确、归一化自洽的解析描述：单一不变量 X 完全决定对数负度，Duan 判据在该纯态对称高斯族中与 PPT 阈值一致但仅为见证，SU(1,1)/U(1) 几何揭示压缩角度是独立于幅度的纠缠来源，且初始宽度与啁啾可作为实验可控的纠缠调控手段。

Abstract: We study two bilinearly coupled harmonic oscillators driven by a common time-dependent spring. A fixed normal-mode transformation reduces the pair to two parametric oscillators, each solved exactly by its Lewis-Riesenfeld invariant and Ermakov-Pinney amplitude, including the ladder-operator construction and the Lewis-Riesenfeld phase. For invariant-vacuum inputs the physical two-mode covariance matrix is obtained in closed form, and the logarithmic negativity collapses to $E_{N}=\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{arcosh}(X/2)$, where a single dimensionless invariant $X \geq 2$ measures the relative phase-space deformation of the two normal modes. We give a normalization-explicit account of the Duan--Simon comparison: after local symplectic optimization the Duan test and the positive partial transpose share the entanglement threshold for this pure exchange-symmetric Gaussian family, but the optimized Duan quantity is a witness, not an independent measure, with $D_{\rm opt}/D_{\rm sep}=e^{-E_N}$. We carefully separate a genuinely separable product preparation from the already-entangled ground state of the coupled Hamiltonian, and use only the former for claims of entanglement generation. Under sinusoidal stiffness modulation a separable input shows bounded correlations away from resonance and sustained growth near parametric resonance, and we demonstrate numerically that the initial width and chirp act as two independent, experimentally accessible controls of the entanglement at a chosen target time. An $SU(1,1)$ formulation identifies $2 E_{N}$ with the hyperbolic separation of the two normal-mode squeezing trajectories on the $SU(1,1)/U(1)$ disk and shows that entanglement is governed by both the relative squeezing magnitude and the relative squeezing angle; ; we exhibit regimes in which nearly equal magnitudes still produce strong entanglement through the angle alone.

</details>


### [95] [Generation of arbitrary quantum states in cavity-atom systems with a three-level ancilla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32845)
*Da-Wei Luo,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将 Law & Eberly 方案推广，用三能级原子作为辅助，通过每步并行移动两个激发，以更少演化步骤（减少一半）确定性地生成任意量子态，并给出控制场解析解及量子速度极限分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 Law & Eberly 方案生成任意量子态所需的演化步骤较多，控制场时序与幅度的误差容易逐步累积，效率与稳健性有提升空间。

Method: 以三能级原子为辅助系统，设计每步控制同时并行转移两个激发的协议，推导控制场强度与作用时间的完整解析表达式，并结合量子速度极限进行定量分析。

Result: 所需演化步数减半；系统动力学面临的时序与幅度误差累积概率降低；给出了控制场强度与时序的解析解及量子速度极限框架下的定量结果。

Conclusion: 所提协议可更高效地生成任意量子态，在减少步骤的同时提升了抗误差能力，为基于辅助原子的量子态制备提供了改进方案。

Abstract: We generalized the seminal work of Law & Eberly, and propose a protocol to generate arbitrary quantum states with a three-level atom as an ancilla in a more efficient manner. This protocol moves two excitations in parallel in each control step, thus reducing the number of evolution steps by half. Full analytical expressions for the control field strengths and timings are derived. The system dynamics is exposed to less chance of error accumulation in the timing and amplitudes of the control fields, and quantitative study in the context of the quantum speed limit is also considered.

</details>


### [96] [Quantum Machine Learning for Cybersecurity Applications: Simulation and Hardware Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32911)
*Zirui Zhu,Zisheng Chen,Xiangyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合架构（紧凑MLP压缩特征 + 少量量子比特的QSVM/VQC量子头），在资源受限条件下对标经典模型，并在NSL-KDD、Ling-Spam任务及IBM量子真机上验证其竞争性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的参数量与算力预算下，经典威胁检测流水线在接近决策边界的样本上性能下降；现有工作未能充分证明量子组件在资源受限场景下能否提升端到端威胁检测能力。

Method: 构建混合架构：先由紧凑多层感知机层压缩数据信息，再将处理后的特征路由至少量量子比特的量子头（QSVM与VQC实现）。在仿真平台上，以相近参数量预算与经典模型对比；在NSL-KDD网络入侵检测与Ling-Spam垃圾邮件过滤两个任务上评测；将最优4比特QSVM部署到IBM Quantum设备并进行噪声感知执行（在较小子集上评估）；另对某QSVM模型开展对抗攻击测试鲁棒性。

Result: 浅层量子头表现与经典模型相当；在困难的近似边界样本上，能适度降低漏报攻击与误报。硬件验证结果与仿真行为较为接近，剩余性能差距主要由器件噪声而非模型设计导致。对抗攻击结果用于评估QSVM鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 即使在小规模、含噪声的量子设备上，精心设计的量子组件仍可作为具竞争力、预算友好的组件，应用于实际网络威胁检测。

Abstract: Under tight feature and compute budgets, classical threat detection pipelines often degrade on near-decision-boundary events. Small quantum processors are now available, but existing work inadequately shows whether quantum components improve end-to-end threat detection under the above resource constrained conditions. This paper tries to address this gap with a hybrid architecture that uses a compact multilayer perceptron layer to compress the information in data and then routes the processed features to a few qubit quantum heads implemented in quantum support vector machine (QSVM) and variational quantum circuit (VQC) models. On a simulation platform, we benchmark these hybrid models against classical models with comparable parameter budgets on two representative cybersecurity tasks, network intrusion detection on NSL-KDD dataset and spam filtering on Ling-Spam dataset. To validate their precision on real quantum hardware, we deploy the best 4-qubit QSVM model on an IBM Quantum device with noise-aware execution, evaluated on a smaller sub-dataset. In the results, shallow quantum heads consistently match, and on difficult near-boundary cases modestly reduce missed attacks and false alarms compared to classical models using the same features. Hardware validation results track the simulation behavior closely enough that the remaining gap is dominated by device noise rather than model design. Furthermore, we conduct adversarial attacks to test the robustness of one QSVM model. Taken together, the study shows that even on small, noisy devices, carefully engineered quantum components may function as competitive, budget-aware components in practical cyber threat detection applications.

</details>


### [97] [The wiring sets a phase-blind quantum memory's gap, the weight caps its coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32943)
*Ruini Qian,Zhaobin Lyu,Zelong Yin,Jingjing Hu,Dengfeng Li,Shuoming An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究工程耗散下量子记忆的相位衰减与"gap time"的关系，发现当每次跳跃重置一个基态时，连接方式（wiring）可设定gap时间，而相位保持其速率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程耗散能纠正错误，但记忆的相位会衰减；记忆中"gap"所测量的物理量尚无定论，因此需要厘清gap时间与相位衰减速率之间的关联。

Method: 理论分析：设定每次跳跃重置一个基态，考察不同wiring对gap时间与相位速率的影响；在dephasing条件下比较gap时间与相位的变化范围；分析跨两个及以上qubit的contrast衰减，并提出双分支泵（two-branch pump）方案与双时钟测试（two-clock test）。

Result: 在dephasing下，gap时间跨wiring可变化两个数量级，而相位速率不变；在该类情形中，跨两个及以上qubit的contrast比任意距离的裸qubit衰减更快，无任何泵功率可替代；其上限为跨越qubit数的二倍；双分支泵在距离三处可突破该上限；双时钟测试可在已发表数据上运行。

Conclusion: gap时间由wiring设定而非由相位速率决定，二者可解耦；contrast衰减存在与跨越qubit数相关的上限，双分支泵可突破该限制，所提双时钟测试具备在现有数据上验证的可行性。

Abstract: Engineered dissipation corrects errors, yet a memory's phase decays. What the memory's gap measures is not settled. Here we show that when each jump resets one basis state, the wiring can set the gap time while the phase keeps its rate. Under dephasing the gap time spans two orders across wirings; the phase does not. In this class, a contrast on two or more qubits fades faster than a bare qubit at any distance; no such pump power substitutes. The ceiling is two over the qubits the contrast spans. A two-branch pump can escape it at distance three; a two-clock test runs on published data.

</details>


### [98] [Toroidal-Core Certificates for PPT-Squared Diagonal Orthogonal Covariant Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32979)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对DOC类量子信道，给出了PPT-squared猜想的一个充分条件：通过将相干数据拆分为两个关联矩阵并进行凸混合，导出显式TCP核心，从而得到使两PPT信道复合为EB的逐元素不等式判据，并在四维构造了非EB但两两复合皆EB的信道族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT-squared猜想——两个PPT量子信道的复合是否必为纠缠破缺（EB）——在一般情形仍未解决；在DOC类中，首个未解决的确定性维度是四维。作者希望在该类中给出可验证的充分判据，推动理解复合何时变为EB。

Method: 对任意有限维DOC映射推导充分条件：将量子相干数据分离为两个关联矩阵，分别与单位阵混合直到落入秩一关联矩阵的凸包，再转化为显式的三点完全正（TCP）核心；剩余部分为纯经典贡献，当满足简单逐元素混合不等式时即为TCP。对M_d上的PPT DOC映射Φ_{A,B,C}与Φ_{D,E,F}，条件为逐元素界(AD)_{ij}≥2⌊√d⌋√(A_{ii}A_{jj}D_{ii}D_{jj})；四维时普适系数为4。此外构造双随机PPT信道连续族，并给出秩敏感的精细化分析。

Result: 所得逐元素不等式保证Φ_{A,B,C}∘Φ_{D,E,F}为EB；四维普适系数为4。构造的连续双随机PPT信道族中，所有a≠1的成员均非EB，但族内任意两两复合均被证明为EB。秩敏感精细化显示低相干秩可改善普适系数。

Conclusion: 该判据是充分而非必要的，与因子宽度和半定层级方法互补；结果给出了DOC类中PPT-squared的显式可验证证书，并揭示相干秩对EB性的影响。

Abstract: The positive-partial-transpose-squared (PPT-squared) conjecture asks whether the composition of two positive-partial-transpose (PPT) quantum channels must be entanglement breaking (EB). The problem remains open in general and, within the diagonal orthogonal covariant (DOC) class, the first unresolved deterministic dimension is four. A sufficient condition for PPT-squared composition is derived for DOC maps in arbitrary finite dimension. The proof isolates the quantum coherence data into two correlation matrices, mixes each with the identity until it lies in the convex hull of rank-one correlation matrices, and converts the resulting objects into an explicit triplewise completely positive (TCP) core. The remaining contribution is purely classical and is TCP whenever a simple entrywise mixing inequality is satisfied. For PPT DOC maps $Φ_{A,B,C}$ and $Φ_{D,E,F}$ on $M_d$, the entrywise bound $(AD)_{ij}\ge 2\lfloor\sqrt{d}\rfloor\sqrt{A_{ii}A_{jj}D_{ii}D_{jj}}$ for all $i,j$ guarantees that $Φ_{A,B,C}\circΦ_{D,E,F}$ is EB. In dimension four the universal coefficient becomes four. A continuous family of bistochastic PPT channels is then constructed; every member with $a\ne1$ is not EB, while every pairwise composition within the family is proved to be EB. A rank-sensitive refinement shows explicitly how low coherence rank can improve the universal coefficient. The resulting certificate is sufficient rather than necessary and is complementary to factor-width and semidefinite-hierarchy approaches.

</details>


### [99] [Loss-Resilient Quantum Networking with Dicke Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33094)
*Bowen Wang,Hongyu Yang,Jiacheng Sun,Wanping Zhou,Jizhou Wu,Yunlong Xiao,Dian Wu,Jian Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作首次实验制备保真度0.922(11)的八光子半激发Dicke态，并以此实现开目标量子隐形传态与量子远程克隆两种网络任务，在光子损耗、探测不完美及部分节点信息缺失下仍超越经典极限与GHZ基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠可为量子网络提供经典不可替代的能力，但其在物理与信息访问逐步受限条件下的演化规律尚不清楚，缺乏在真实损耗环境下保留功能性的网络架构与统一操作框架。

Method: 实验制备八光子半激发Dicke态（保真度0.922(11)），并基于该态实施两项网络任务：开目标隐形传态（将未知态从一节点传至七个远端节点中的任意一个）与量子远程克隆（分发七个近似克隆）；同时考察不完美光子探测、传输损耗及部分参与节点信息缺失等条件，并与GHZ基准对比。

Result: 开目标隐形传态保真度达0.926(7)–0.941(6)；七个克隆的保真度为0.713(3)，接近最优值5/7；协议在探测不完美、传输损耗和信息缺失下仍高于经典极限，并优于对应GHZ基准；随参与节点减少，传态保真度呈阶梯式层级下降，其两端分别对应开目标隐形传态与最优远程克隆。

Conclusion: Dicke纠缠可作为抗损耗量子网络的可行架构，将开目标隐形传态与量子远程克隆统一于单一操作框架，为在现实条件下保持有效功能的量子技术开辟路径。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement can endow a quantum network with capabilities unavailable classically, yet their evolution under progressively reduced physical and informational access remains largely unexplored. Here we realize the first eight-photon half-excitation Dicke state, with a fidelity of 0.922(11), and use it to implement two distinct network tasks: open-destination teleportation and quantum telecloning. An unknown state can be teleported from one agent to any of seven remote agents with fidelities of 0.926(7)-0.941(6), while seven approximate clones are distributed with a fidelity of 0.713(3), close to the optimal value of 5/7. The protocol remains above the classical limit under imperfect photon detection, transmission loss and missing information from participating agents, and outperforms the corresponding GHZ benchmarks. As fewer agents contribute, the teleportation fidelity falls through a stepwise hierarchy whose two extremes recover open-destination teleportation and optimal telecloning, connecting both tasks within a single operational framework. These results establish Dicke entanglement as a promising architecture for loss-resilient quantum networks, opening a route towards quantum technologies that retain useful functionality under realistic operating conditions.

</details>


### [100] [The Breakdown of Classical Minicrypt Equivalences in the Quantum-Computation Classical-Communication Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33098)
*Boyang Chen,Yiming Wang,Ziyi Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造两个预言机世界，证明经典Minicrypt图景在QCCC模型下崩溃：存在可高效验证的单向谜题与QCCC一次性签名，但分别不存在单向函数与比特承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密码学中单向函数、伪随机数生成器、承诺与签名等基本原语刻画了同一密码世界（Minicrypt）。作者希望探究这一图景在量子计算-经典通信（QCCC）设定下能保留到什么程度。

Method: 采用预言机分离方法，构造两个存在高效可验证单向谜题（EV-OWPuzz）的预言机世界，并借助已知等价关系引入QCCC一次性签名。证明中发展了分析随机相位态的技术，包括集中定理与LOCC退耦定理。

Result: 在第一个预言机世界中，即使允许量子伪确定求值，单向函数也不存在；在第二个世界中，QCCC比特承诺不存在。因此，经典Minicrypt中从签名到单向函数与比特承诺的归约在QCCC设定下没有完全黑盒对应物。

Conclusion: 经典Minicrypt图景在QCCC模型中通过预言机分离被打破，表明签名与单向函数、比特承诺之间的经典归约无法在QCCC设定下以黑盒方式保持；所发展的随机相位态分析技术可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: Classically, it is well-known that several fundamental cryptographic primitives, including one-way functions, pseudorandom number generators, commitments, and signatures, characterize the same cryptographic world, which is known as "Minicrypt". In this paper, we investigate to what extent this picture persists in the quantum-computation classical-communication (QCCC) setting, where parties may perform quantum local computation, but communicate through only classical messages. We demonstrate that the classical Minicrypt landscape breaks down in the QCCC model by oracle separations. We construct two oracle worlds where efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles (EV-OWPuzz) exist. By a known equivalence, QCCC one-time signatures also exist in both worlds. In the first, one-way functions do not exist, even if we allow quantum pseudodeterministic evaluation. In the second, QCCC bit commitments do not exist. Thus, the classical Minicrypt reductions from signatures to one-way functions and bit commitments have no fully black-box counterparts in the QCCC setting. Our proofs develop techniques for analyzing the random phase states, including a concentration theorem and an LOCC decoupling theorem, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum Monte Carlo Tree Search with Fixed Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33132)
*Mingjie Hu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子计算如何改进固定置信度下的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），提出量子MCTS算法（QMCTS），建立了实例相关的查询复杂度下界与匹配上界，并在仿真与真实量子硬件上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MCTS在计算密集型决策问题中评估随机叶节点值代价高昂，而量子计算的最新进展为降低此类查询复杂度提供了新机遇，因此有必要系统研究量子加速在固定置信度MCTS中的潜力与极限。

Method: 首先在量子oracle模型下形式化MCTS问题；进而提出QMCTS算法，将搜索树上的阈值式淘汰（threshold-based elimination）与量子蒙特卡洛估计相结合以降低随机叶值评估成本；同时开展下界分析，引入新的量子相位测试（quantum phase-testing）结果，并据此推导上界。

Result: 建立了实例相关的查询复杂度下界，并给出QMCTS对应的上界，实现理论上的上下界匹配；通过仿真实验验证了理论结果，并在真实量子硬件上初步验证了QMCTS的可行性。

Conclusion: QMCTS在固定置信度设置下可有效降低MCTS的查询复杂度，其下界分析所提出的量子相位测试结果具有超越MCTS的潜在应用价值，且算法在真实量子设备上具备可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing are opening new opportunities for computationally intensive decision problems. This paper studies how quantum computing can improve Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) in the fixed-confidence setting, where the goal is to identify a near-optimal move in a given game tree with high probability while minimizing query complexity. We first formulate MCTS under a quantum oracle model. We then develop a quantum MCTS algorithm (QMCTS) that combines threshold-based elimination on the search tree with quantum Monte Carlo estimation to reduce the cost of evaluating stochastic leaf values. We establish an instance-dependent lower bound on the query complexity and derive a corresponding upper bound for QMCTS. The lower-bound analysis introduces a new quantum phase-testing result that may also be useful beyond MCTS. We validate the theoretical results through simulation experiments and further demonstrate the feasibility of QMCTS on real quantum hardware.

</details>


### [102] [Nash Equilibrium from Quantum-Field Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33188)
*Hao Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将真空纠缠收获嵌入量子输入博弈：两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器从(3+1)维Minkowski无质量标量场收获纠缠，裁判用独立问题量子比特与相位校准的局域Bell测量换算为收益。微扰领头阶的平均收益正比于带符号纠缠裕度√(H²+Q²)−P，并给出1+2n₊个颤抖手完美均衡；固定协议下五个解析可证设置实现7→3→1→7→1的均衡数序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有纠缠收获研究多关注探测器间纠缠量与场关联本身，而这类量子场关联能否改变稳定的策略行为尚不清楚。作者希望建立"量子输入博弈"框架，把真空纠缠收获的场关联直接映射为博弈收益，以检验不同场关联机制能否产生不同的稳定策略结构。

Method: 构造量子输入博弈：两探测器在(3+1)维Minkowski时空中从自由无质量标量场收获真空纠缠；裁判引入独立问题量子比特并做相位校准的局域Bell测量，将量子态转化为经典收益。在微扰领头阶计算平均收益，提炼出由Hadamard关联H、Pauli-Jordan因果项Q和局域激发噪声P构成的带符号纠缠裕度。在收益非简并条件下分析有退出选项、以及早/晚交互时机的颤抖手完美均衡计数。

Result: 领头阶平均收益正比于√(H²+Q²)−P。在收益非简并处，允许退出及早/晚交互选择时存在1+2n₊个颤抖手完美均衡，n₊为正收益时机均衡数。对某一固定协议，五个解析可证设置给出均衡数序列7→3→1→7→1，分别对应类空区、第一对易子瓣、中心载波相消、第二瓣以及波前之后的Huygens区。

Conclusion: 在该固定量子输入验证协议内，不同场关联区域会生成不同的稳定策略结构，说明纠缠收获的场关联能够系统性影响博弈的均衡模式，为量子场论关联与博弈论稳定性之间建立了可解析检验的桥梁。

Abstract: Entanglement harvesting transfers quantum field correlations to localized probes, yet whether these correlations can alter stable strategic behavior has remained unclear. We formulate a quantum-input game in which two Unruh-DeWitt detectors harvest vacuum entanglement from a free massless scalar field in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. A referee converts this state into payoffs using independent question qubits and phase-calibrated local Bell measurements. To leading perturbative order, the mean reward is proportional to the signed entanglement margin $\sqrt{\mathcal{H}^2+\mathcal{Q}^2}-\mathcal{P}$, where $\mathcal{H}$ is the Hadamard correlation contribution, $\mathcal{Q}$ is the Pauli-Jordan causal contribution, and $\mathcal{P}$ is the local excitation noise. Away from payoff degeneracies, allowing each player to exit or to choose an early or late interaction yields $1+2n_+$ trembling-hand perfect equilibria, where $n_+$ counts the positive-reward timing equilibria. For one fixed protocol, five analytically certified settings realize the sequence $7\to3\to1\to7\to1$. They represent the spacelike region, the first commutator lobe, central carrier cancellation, the second lobe, and the post-wavefront Huygens regime. Thus, within this fixed quantum-input verification protocol, distinct field-correlation regimes generate different stable strategic structures.

</details>


### [103] [Occupation-selective photon-pair interactions in a flux-pumped SQUID resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33228)
*L. N. Ferreira,D. Z. Rossatto,A. S. M. de Castro,G. D. de Moraes Neto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在磁通泵浦的约瑟夫森电路中识别出一种“占据数条件化的成对相互作用”，并建立了相应的有效理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁通泵浦约瑟夫森电路是参数化光子产生与多模玻色操控的重要平台，但两个简谐模式参与同一被驱动SQUID坐标时出现的新型相互作用尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 从完整约瑟夫森余弦项出发，推导含时Schrieffer–Wolff与Magnus两种互补有效描述，揭示直接与虚过程；并与未展开双模电路哈密顿量的数值收敛Floquet处理进行基准对比。

Result: 发现一个简并成对过程：信号模中成对产生/湮灭两个光子，而第二简谐模占据数不变；该控制模同时改变成对共振频率与成对跃迁强度，二者物理起源不同（对角非线性移共振，非线性矩阵元使避免交叉间隙依赖控制模占据）。由此产生二维成对谱，可实现Fock晶格中的选择性跃迁；控制模处于相干叠加时产生信号–控制模纠缠及依赖控制模的非高斯态；可观测层面比较表明跃迁强度的变化不能仅由占据依赖的共振移动解释。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一种玻色子控制的参数化相互作用，即一个简谐模式的状态同时控制另一模式中非线性过程的谱位置与耦合强度。

Abstract: Flux-pumped Josephson circuits provide a versatile platform for parametric photon generation and multimode bosonic control. We identify an occupation-conditioned pair interaction that arises when two harmonic normal modes participate in the same driven SQUID coordinate. We consider a degenerate pair process in which two photons are created or annihilated in a signal mode while the occupation of a second harmonic mode remains unchanged. Although it does not exchange excitations, this controller mode modifies both the resonance frequency and the strength of the pair transition. The two effects have different physical origins: diagonal Josephson nonlinearities shift the pair resonance, whereas the nonlinear Josephson matrix element makes the avoided-crossing gap itself depend on the controller occupation. Starting from the full Josephson cosine, we develop complementary time-dependent Schrieffer--Wolff and Magnus descriptions that expose the direct and virtual processes responsible for this interaction, and benchmark the resulting effective theory against a numerically converged Floquet treatment of the unexpanded two-mode circuit Hamiltonian. The occupation dependence produces a two-dimensional pair spectrum that enables selective transitions within the bosonic Fock lattice. When the controller is prepared in a coherent superposition, the same interaction converts this conditional dynamics into signal--controller entanglement and controller-dependent non-Gaussian state generation. Observable-level comparisons further show that the variation of the pair-transition strength cannot be reproduced by occupation-dependent resonance shifts alone. These results establish a boson-controlled parametric interaction in which the state of one harmonic mode controls both the spectral position and the coupling strength of a nonlinear process in another.

</details>


### [104] [In-plane optimal qubit operation with control fidelities exceeding 99 %](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33229)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Zhenzhen Kong,Rong-Long Ma,Hao-Tian Jiang,Ao-Ran Li,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Tao Wu,Rui Zheng,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在平面锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，通过优化面内磁场方向最小化电荷噪声敏感度，使Hahn回波相干时间提升5倍、控制误差降低近10倍，门保真度最高达99.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空穴自旋量子比特因全电操控适合大规模量子计算，但价带空穴的丰富物理机制导致各向异性，既是挑战也是延长相干时间、提升操控保真度的机会。

Method: 在平面锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，研究其在面内磁场下对电荷噪声的各向异性磁化率；将纵向自旋电敏感度与相干性及控制性能相关联，寻找电荷噪声敏感度最小的最优工作点；采用Hahn回波与门集层析（GST）进行表征。

Result: 优化磁场方向可降低自旋电敏感度，使Hahn回波相干时间提升5倍，控制不保真度抑制近10倍；最优工作点下GST测得最大门保真度99.82%。

Conclusion: 通过磁场方向优化可显著提升空穴自旋量子比特的相干性与操控保真度，增强其在可扩展量子信息处理中的应用前景。

Abstract: Hole spin qubits based on semiconductor quantum dots are promising for building future large-scale quantum computers owing to their all-electrical manipulation. However, abundant physical mechanisms of valence band holes lead to anisotropic qubit properties. There is an opportunity to prolong the coherence time and achieve high-fidelity qubit manipulations. Here, we report a single-hole spin qubit in a planar germanium quantum dot and investigate its anisotropic susceptibility to charge noise under an in-plane magnetic field. By correlating the longitudinal spin-electric susceptibility with qubit coherence and control performance, we identify an optimal operating point where the sensitivity to charge noise is minimized. We find that optimizing the magnetic-field orientation reduces the spin-electric susceptibility, resulting in a five-fold enhancement of the Hahn-echo coherence time and a nearly tenfold suppression of control infidelity. At the optimal operating point, gate set tomography demonstrates the maximum gate fidelity of 99.82 %. Our finding enhances the prospects of hole spin qubits for scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [105] [High-fidelity geometric quantum gates exceeding 99.9% in germanium quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33251)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Rong-Long Ma,Zhenzhen Kong,Ao-Ran Li,Chengxian Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yang Liu,Hao-Tian Jiang,Zhi-Tao Wu,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Guang-Can Guo,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，通过门集层析发现非共振噪声限制保真度，引入几何量子计算后各类门保真度普遍超99%，且在微波失谐下仍保持>99%，展现噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求高保真度且鲁棒的量子比特操控。作者在锗量子点单空穴自旋比特上评估控制保真度时，发现门集层析序列中连续I门期间的非共振噪声严重制约比特性能，因此希望寻找噪声鲁棒的操控方案。

Method: 在锗量子点中制备单空穴自旋量子比特，使用门集层析（GST）表征I、X/2、Y/2门的控制保真度；针对发现的问题，引入几何量子计算（GQC）实现抗噪声的比特操控，并在较宽Rabi频率范围及微波频率失谐条件下测试几何门的保真度。

Result: 常规门集层析测得I、X/2、Y/2的最大控制保真度分别为97.48%、99.81%、99.88%。几何门控制在宽Rabi频率范围内保真度均高于99%，最高超过99.9%；当微波频率失谐±2.5 MHz（X/2、Y/2门）和±1.2 MHz（I门）时，几何门保真度仍超过99%。

Conclusion: 几何量子计算能够有效克服非共振噪声导致的保真度下降，在半导体量子计算中具有可重复实现高保真比特操控的潜力。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity and robust qubit manipulations is a crucial requirement for realizing faulttolerant quantum computation. Here, we demonstrate a single-hole spin qubit in a germanium quantum dot and characterize its control fidelity using gate set tomography. The maximum control fidelities reach 97.48%, 99.81%, 99.88% for the I, X/2 and Y /2 gate, respectively. These results reveal that off-resonance noise during consecutive I gates in gate set tomography sequences severely limits qubit performance. Therefore, we introduce geometric quantum computation to realize noiseresilient qubit manipulation. The geometric gate control fidelities remain above 99% across a wide range of Rabi frequencies. The maximum fidelity surpasses 99.9%. Furthermore, the fidelities of geometric X/2 and Y /2 (I) gates exceed 99% even when detuning the microwave frequency by +-2.5 MHz (+-1.2 MHz), highlighting the noise-resilient feature. These results demonstrate that geometric quantum computation is a potential method for achieving high-fidelity qubit manipulation reproducibly in semiconductor quantum computation.

</details>


### [106] [NxM-Version Programming for Quantum Software: High-Level Components across Frameworks and Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33255)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Higor Amario de Souza,Anthony Accioly,Valter Vieira de Camargo,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Quanifi：将 Qiskit、Cirq、Qrisp、pyQuil、PennyLane 等高级量子编程框架封装为 Apache NiFi 数据流画布上的组件，以 OpenQASM 2.0 交换电路，实现 N×M 程序执行（N 个实现 × M 个后端引擎），并在真实硬件上发现三个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件工程仍需开发者直接使用量子门与量子比特编程，并掌握各框架（Qiskit、Cirq、Qrisp、pyQuil、PennyLane）特有的知识，且每个框架通过各自的工具链连接量子硬件，导致跨框架与跨引擎的复用和验证困难。

Method: 将各框架的高层例程封装为 Apache NiFi 数据流画布上的组件，组件间以 OpenQASM 2.0 格式交换电路，使一个框架构建的电路可由另一个框架执行；在此基础上提出 N×M 程序执行：在单个 NiFi 流中，将 N 个算法实现分别在 M 个引擎（模拟器或真实硬件）上运行，并对每个结果标注其实现与引擎来源，从而将结果不一致定位到具体实现、具体引擎或某一实现-引擎对。

Result: 在来自 IBM、IQM 和 Quantum Inspire 的三台量子计算机上，用三个框架构建的 Grover 算法进行实验；并在通过两条路径访问的同一台 IQM 设备上，对三个加法器构建器组成的矩阵进行实验。N×M 执行发现三个真实缺陷：Quantum Inspire 适配器中错误的门集声明、Tuna-17 上与取反的 R_x 角度一致的执行行为，以及 Open Quantum 导致的电路被修改；后两者在未报错的情况下返回错误答案。

Conclusion: 所有三个缺陷均已上报，Open Quantum 的提供商确认并修复了其 QASM 解析缺陷；表明基于 OpenQASM 交换与 N×M 执行的跨框架、跨引擎方法能够有效暴露量子软件栈中静默产生错误结果的真实缺陷。

Abstract: Quantum computing has in recent years evolved from a purely theoretical field to an active area in both academia and industry. As a result, Quantum Software Engineering has emerged as an area that aims to organize the process of building, testing, and running quantum software. However, writing quantum software still requires programming with quantum gates and qubits, as well as knowledge specific to quantum software frameworks such as Qiskit, Cirq, Qrisp, pyQuil and PennyLane, each of which reaches quantum hardware through its own toolchain. We present Quanifi, which packages the high-level routines of these frameworks as components on the Apache NiFi dataflow canvas. The components exchange circuits as OpenQASM 2.0, so a circuit built by one framework can be executed by another. This allows for two types of redundancy: several frameworks implement the same algorithm, and several engines (simulators or real hardware) execute the same circuit. We describe N$\times$M program execution, which crosses the two: a single NiFi flow runs each of the $N$ implementations on each of the $M$ engines, and because every result is labelled with its implementation and its engine, a disagreement can be linked to an implementation, to an engine, or to a single implementation-engine pair. We ran Grover's algorithm built by three frameworks on three quantum computers from IBM, IQM and Quantum Inspire, and a matrix of three adder builders on one IQM device reached through two routes. N$\times$M execution helped us discover three real defects: an incorrect gate-set declaration in the Quantum Inspire adapter, execution consistent with negated $R_x$ angles on Tuna-17, and circuit modification through Open Quantum. The latter two returned wrong answers without reporting an error. We reported all three defects and Open Quantum's provider confirmed and fixed its QASM parsing defect.

</details>


### [107] [Preparation-controlled relaxation in an overdamped RLC circuit: A pedagogical route to the spectral Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33277)
*Matheus H. dos Santos,Cler T. Garcez,Jeveson C. da Silva,G. D. de Moraes Neto,Norton G. de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以过阻尼RLC电路为经典类比，研究"初态能量更高者能否更快冷却"的谱Mpemba效应，并从确定性、随机、开放系统到量子动力学逐层推广该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初始能量本身不足以决定弛豫路径，作者希望揭示能量序反转（Mpemba类效应）的一般机制，即初态在慢、快衰减模式上的激发权重如何决定冷却速率。

Method: 以过阻尼RLC电路为实验平台，推导模式制备条件与交叉判据，用示波器重构电荷、电流、能量验证；引入白噪声驱动、Ornstein-Uhlenbeck平衡处理、Fokker-Planck生成元，并桥接到热阻尼量子谐振子与阈下参量振荡器的Liouvillian描述。

Result: 经典电路中初态高能量态可穿过低能量态；反转在白噪声驱动下仍存在；OU处理区分均值信息与协方差信息，中心Gibbs制备保持能量序，而位移或各向异性Gaussian态可编码慢/快扇区；热阻尼量子谐振子平均裸能超额按单指数衰减，禁止有限平均占据下的能量序反转；阈下参量振荡器恢复慢/快正交扇区，交叉可由位移与协方差控制。

Conclusion: 观测到的弛豫联合取决于生成元谱、制备所布居的模式以及观测量可见的模式三个要素。

Abstract: Can a system with more stored energy cool faster than one with less? An overdamped resistor--inductor--capacitor (RLC) circuit offers a classical, experimentally accessible spectral Mpemba analogue: both states lose energy monotonically, yet the initially higher-energy state can cross below the lower-energy one. The reason is that initial energy alone does not determine the relaxation path; initial charge and current set how strongly the slow and fast decay modes are excited. We develop this mechanism from deterministic to stochastic and open-system descriptions. For the classical circuit, we derive the modal preparation conditions and crossing criterion, explain geometrically why equal-energy states can relax differently, and confirm the effect experimentally by reconstructing charge, current, and energy from oscilloscope traces. The inversion survives when the circuit is driven by externally injected white noise. An equilibrium Ornstein--Uhlenbeck treatment separates information carried by the ensemble mean from that stored in its covariance: centered Gibbs preparations preserve mean-energy ordering, while displaced or anisotropic Gaussian states can encode distinct slow and fast sectors. A Fokker--Planck generator then bridges to Markovian quantum dynamics. For a thermally damped quantum harmonic oscillator, the mean bare-energy excess decays with a single exponential factor, forbidding energy-order inversion for finite mean occupation. A below-threshold parametric oscillator restores slow and fast quadrature sectors, allowing crossings controlled by displacement and covariance. The Liouvillian formulation shows that observed relaxation depends jointly on the generator spectrum, the modes populated by preparation, and the modes visible to the observable.

</details>


### [108] [Temperature--Hamiltonian Ambiguity in Strong-Coupling Quantum Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33389)
*Jinshuang Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 强耦合下约化平衡态存在"温度—哈密顿量"歧义：约化态只确定无量纲Gibbs生成元，无法唯一确定局域哈密顿量与温度。作者提出操作化方案，用独立于Gibbs参数化的物理信息确定局域哈密顿量，再据此定义唯一局域温度，并通过两个精确可解模型予以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合使子系统偏离其裸哈密顿量的Gibbs态。此时约化平衡态虽与某Gibbs形式相容，但存在多种等价表示（同一状态可由不同的局域哈密顿量与温度组合给出），因此"局域温度"和"局域哈密顿量"的归属并不唯一。作者旨在澄清并解决这一歧义，为弱耦合之外的局域热力学提供操作化基础。

Method: 提出所谓"温度—哈密顿量歧义"的形式化：约化平衡态固定无量纲Gibbs生成元，却不足以唯一确定进入Gibbs表示的局域哈密顿量与温度。提出操作化解决方案——局域哈密顿量由独立于稳态Gibbs参数化的物理信息（如动力学）确定；当精确约化态与该独立哈密顿量生成的Gibbs族相容时，即给出唯一的局域温度。用两个精确可解模型演示：1）二能级系统—谐振子模型，通过基于温度重整化（相对指定局域哈密顿量）与均力哈密顿量层级重整化（在浴温下）的状态等价描述显式呈现歧义；2）精确玻色模型给出互补情形，其中延迟动力学独立确定重整化的局域激发尺度，从而先固定局域哈密顿量，再由稳态布居确定温度。

Result: 二能级—谐振子模型表明，同一约化平衡态可分别用温度重整化（固定局域哈密顿量、改变温度）和均力哈密顿量层级重整化（固定浴温、改变哈密顿量结构）两种状态等价描述来刻画，从而将歧义显式化。精确玻色模型则显示，延迟动力学可独立固定一个重整化的局域激发尺度，先确定局域哈密顿量，随后由稳态布居唯一确定局域温度，构成互补的解法路径。

Conclusion: 区分"状态表示"与"热力学归属"：约化态本身不足以指定热力学量，需要额外的物理信息来选定局域哈密顿量。由此提出一个超越弱耦合的操作化局域热力学框架，使局域温度在强耦合下仍能被唯一、可操作地定义。

Abstract: Strong coupling generally drives a subsystem away from the Gibbs state of its bare Hamiltonian at the bath temperature. We formulate a temperature--Hamiltonian ambiguity: the reduced equilibrium state fixes the dimensionless Gibbs generator, but does not by itself uniquely determine the local Hamiltonian and temperature entering a Gibbs representation. We propose an operational resolution in which the local Hamiltonian is identified from physical information independent of the stationary Gibbs parametrization. Whenever the exact reduced state is compatible with the Gibbs family generated by that independently identified Hamiltonian, it then determines a unique local temperature. An exactly solvable two-level-system--oscillator model makes the ambiguity explicit through state-equivalent descriptions based on temperature renormalization relative to a specified local Hamiltonian and Hamiltonian-of-mean-force level renormalization at the bath temperature. An exact bosonic model provides the complementary case in which retarded dynamics independently fixes a renormalized local excitation scale and thereby specifies the local Hamiltonian before the stationary populations determine the temperature. These results distinguish state representation from thermodynamic assignment and provide an operational framework for local thermodynamics beyond weak coupling.

</details>


### [109] [Local Many Worlds in Spacetime: Deutsch-Hayden Descriptors and Local Wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33453)
*Mordecai Waegell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并论证：在满足局域性、空间可分离且排除超决定论与逆因果的前提下，量子力学必然是"局域多世界"理论。作者区分局域薛定谔图景与局域海森堡图景，并证明任意N量子比特系统的局域波函数均可由Deutsch-Hayden（DH）描述符直接构造，从而兼具两种图景的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔定理表明，局域、空间可分离且无超决定论或逆因果的物理理论必然是"多局域世界"理论。局域多世界形式体系不含非可分离的全局波函数，仅有保持概率流守恒的局域幺正演化，且无波函数坍缩。局域海森堡图景通过DH描述符紧凑地编码信息，但其形式晦涩、难以理解，因而需要一种方法将两种图景联系起来。

Method: 作者首先阐述局域薛定谔图景：每个时空事件拥有自己的局域波函数，仅由过去光锥内的局域波函数与局域幺正演化算子构建，与类空间隔的局域波函数完全断开。同时以海森堡图景为每个系统赋予DH描述符，各自在局域幺正操作下演化。本文进一步证明：对于任意N量子比特系统，其局域波函数总是可以直接由DH描述符构造出来。

Result: 证明了对任意N量子比特系统，局域波函数可由对应的DH描述符直接重建。局域波函数与DH描述符均记录了过去相互作用的历史信息，二者包含相同信息；波函数提供直观清晰的物理叙述，而描述符则以更紧凑但较晦涩的形式表达同一内容。

Conclusion: 通过建立局域波函数与DH描述符之间的直接构造关系，本文将局域薛定谔图景的直观性与局域海森堡图景的紧凑性结合起来，使此前相对难以理解的局域海森堡图景更易被接受和使用。

Abstract: Bell's theorem establishes that a local and spatially separable physical theory with no superdeterminism or retrocausality must be a theory of many local worlds. The local many worlds formalism of quantum mechanics is a transparent separable physical theory in spacetime with a Lorentz covariant causal structure. Unlike the Everett many worlds interpretation, there is no nonseparable universal wavefunction in configuration space, but like Everett, there is only local unitary evolution which conserves probability (fluid) current, and no wavefunction collapse. Each event in spacetime has its own local wavefunction which is built up using only the local wavefunctions and local unitary evolution operators within its past light cone, and which is entirely disconnected from spacelike separated local wavefunctions. We call this the local Schrödinger picture. Local many worlds can also be described in the Heisenberg picture by ascribing Deutsch-Hayden (DH) descriptors to each individual system, each of which evolves under local unitary operations. Both the local wavefunctions and DH descriptors contain records of past interactions, and while the local wavefunctions provide a clear and intuitive physical narrative, the descriptors contain all of the same information in a much more compact form. Unfortunately, the compact form is also somewhat cryptic, which has made the local Heisenberg picture relatively inaccessible. The purpose of this article is to show that the local wavefunctions for any $N$-qubit system can always be constructed directly from the DH descriptors, allowing us to combine the benefits of both pictures.

</details>


### [110] [How many photons can a molecule scatter before its coherence is lost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33475)
*M. R. Tarbutt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了分子量子比特在保守光阱中因散射囚禁激光光子而产生的退相干率，并将其用分子极化率的矢量与张量部分α₁、α₂表示；该速率在α₁、α₂均较小时被显著抑制，并针对CaF分子确定了相应的波长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子量子比特置于保守光阱中时，会散射囚禁激光的光子，从而损失相干性，这是限制分子量子比特性能的关键问题，需要对其退相干率进行定量刻画。

Method: 将退相干率分解为两部分：一部分等于两个量子比特态的平均拉曼散射率，另一部分正比于两者瑞利散射振幅之差的平方；并进一步用分子极化率的矢量部分α₁和张量部分α₂来表达结果，进而分析抑制条件。

Result: 分子的退相干率通常远大于原子，但当α₁和α₂同时较小时该速率会被抑制；论文为CaF分子识别出了满足这一条件的波长。

Conclusion: 分子量子比特的光子散射退相干可通过选择使矢量与张量极化率均较小的波长来有效抑制，这为分子量子比特在光阱中的相干性优化提供了波长选择的依据。

Abstract: Molecular qubits held in conservative optical traps may lose coherence when they scatter photons from the trapping lasers. The rate for this process has two parts, one equal to the mean Raman scattering rate of the two qubit states, and the other proportional to the square of the difference in their Rayleigh scattering amplitudes. This paper calculates the decoherence rate, expressing results in terms of the vector and tensor parts of the molecular polarizability, $α_1$ and $α_2$. While the decoherence rate is generally much larger for molecules than for atoms, it is suppressed at wavelengths where $α_1$ and $α_2$ are both small. The wavelengths where this occurs are identified for CaF molecules.

</details>


### [111] [The Fully Depolarizing Noise Conjecture for Entangled Physical States: A Twenty-Year Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33508)
*Gil Kalai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重访作者2006年提出的"纠缠物理量子比特关联误差"猜想：有效噪声信道中含联合完全去极化分量，其速率与双比特门误差相当，构成对量子容错的结构性障碍。该猜想仍未解决，但现有实验平台已可检验，作者同时讨论噪声敏感性与计算复杂度、量子优势的统计分析等相关方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该猜想若成立，将超越标准噪声模型，对可扩展量子容错构成重大挑战。随着实验量子计算进步，该问题已进入可实证检验的阶段，值得重新审视并推动经验验证。

Method: 本文为综述与反思性论文：重新表述猜想的理论内涵（关联误差、联合完全去极化分量及其速率量级），并结合量子噪声敏感性、计算复杂度以及量子优势实验声明的统计分析等相关研究路径进行讨论。

Result: 猜想本身仍为开放问题，尚无定论；但作者指出当前实验设备已具备对其进行经验检验的能力。此外，论文梳理了噪声敏感性与复杂度在含噪中等规模量子系统中的角色，以及量子优势实验统计分析的进展。

Conclusion: 关联误差猜想是对量子容错的关键潜在障碍，亟需实验检验；作者将其置于更广泛的量子计算批判性研究框架内，并在致敬Yuri Gurevich的文集中回顾了个人与科学经历的关联。

Abstract: In this paper I revisit my 2006 conjecture on correlated errors in entangled physical qubits, originally proposed as a potential obstruction to quantum fault tolerance. The conjecture asserts that, in any physical implementation of a quantum computer, the effective noise channel acting on entangled physical qubits contains a joint fully depolarizing component, with a rate comparable to that of two-qubit gate errors. This hypothesized structural constraint goes beyond standard noise models and, if valid, would pose a significant challenge to scalable quantum fault tolerance.
  The conjecture remains open, but recent advances in experimental quantum computing bring it within reach of empirical testing on current devices. I also discuss two related directions in my critical study of quantum computation: the role of noise sensitivity and computational complexity in noisy intermediate-scale quantum systems, and the statistical analysis of experimental claims of quantum advantage. Finally, since this paper is written for a volume honoring Yuri Gurevich, I include some reflections on the ways in which my scientific and personal trajectory became intertwined with Yuri's.

</details>


### [112] [Engineering Defect-Phonon Interactions Through Heterophase-Interfaces in Silicon Carbide Membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33545)
*Kirlie Iulius Figuera Michal,Jin Hee Lee,Keiju Sato,Takuji Maekawa,Je-Hyung Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过远程外延构建3C/4H SiC异相界面，调控缺陷-声子相互作用，实现室温下Vsi缺陷零声子跃迁的显著增强（线宽4.71 meV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带隙半导体中的点缺陷可提供固态自旋与光子量子比特，且能在室温工作；但本征电子-声子耦合导致声子边带发射宽、零声子线（ZPL）弱，限制了高效自旋-光子界面与可扩展的光子介导相互作用。

Method: 基于远程外延（remote epitaxy）技术引入受控异相界面（heterophase interface），将通常被视为需消除缺陷的堆垛层错转化为晶体异质性工程手段，在单相4H-SiC膜与3C/4H异相界面SiC中对比研究Vsi缺陷的光学性质。

Result: 相较于单相4H-SiC膜，3C/4H异相界面SiC中的Vsi零声子光学跃迁大幅增强，室温下零声子线线宽显著变窄，仅为4.71 meV。

Conclusion: 异相界面工程可有效调控缺陷-声子相互作用，为在单晶本征性质之外设计缺陷量子发射体提供新自由度，是获得室温明亮量子发射体与增强零声子跃迁的有效途径。

Abstract: Point defects in wide-bandgap semiconductors offer spin and photonic qubits in a solid-state platform, making them important building blocks for quantum information processing, communication, and sensing. While these systems have the strong advantage of room-temperature operation, intrinsic electron-phonon interaction induces broad phonon-sideband emission and weak zero-phonon-line transitions, limiting efficient spin-photon interfaces and scalable photon-mediated interaction. Here, we introduce a controlled heterophase interface based on the remote epitaxy technique as a crystal heterogeneity-engineering strategy. While stacking faults are treated as imperfections to be eliminated, our results instead show that crystal-phase interfaces can provide an additional degree of freedom to engineer defect-phonon interaction beyond the intrinsic properties of a single crystal. In comparison with single-phase 4H-silicon carbide (SiC) membranes, Vsi in 3C/4H heterointerface SiC exhibits a drastic enhancement of zero-phonon optical transitions, with a substantially narrower zero-phonon linewidth of 4.71 meV even at room temperature. These results establish heterophase-interface engineering as an effective route for tailoring defect-phonon interactions and realizing bright room-temperature quantum emitters with enhanced zero-phonon optical transitions.

</details>


### [113] [Terminal-Register Certification for Finite-Measurement Learning of Multiscale Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33567)
*Bhvain Makwana,Kashyap Patel,Manjunath Joshi,Jaideep Mulherkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维纯态的逆二元MERA学习，论证了序列与终端测量的等价性及归一化后选择的扰动放大效应，引入噪声感知的有限采样证书，并在横场Ising基态上以6千万训练测量/次达到0.99以上保真度，同时对n=16下的架构公平性进行对照研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化量子态学习不仅依赖表达力强的拟设，还需要在有限测量与不完美实现下仍然有意义的可操作证书。现有工作缺乏对逆MERA学习过程中测量调度等价性、后选择放大效应以及有限采样误差预算的严格刻画，也缺少对架构公平性的系统评估。

Method: 采用逆二元多尺度纠缠重整化拟设（MERA）学习一维纯态：粗粒化过程中被移除的量子比特被相干控制并汇聚到终端寄存器统一测量。推导理想序列测量与终端测量在匹配因果操作下给出相同完整比特串分布的结论，并分析归一化后选择使扰动按前缀接受率反比放大的规律。提出噪声感知定理，为有限采样证书引入逐个体校准的总变差实现预算。在开边界横场Ising基态上以冻结8量子比特调度评估协议，并进行1080个电路噪声单元与6480行置信覆盖的固定电路鲁棒性验证。进一步在n=16下开展三项研究：120轮精确梯度多起点诊断、因果锥完备且参数匹配的局域电路对照实验。

Result: 冻结8量子比特调度、每次运行5.6亿次模拟训练测量，在全部60次留出运行中保真度均超过0.99，平均保真度0.996886。1080个电路噪声单元与6480行置信覆盖的鲁棒性验证未出现违反可靠性的情形。n=16的120轮精确梯度多起点诊断中，MERA在58/60配对重启中保真度更高，60/60中长程误差更低，但无一次运行满足预设平稳准则。因果锥完备、参数匹配的局域电路总门暴露度高2.62倍，却在保真度、长程误差、能量和熵的30次配对比较中全部落败。

Conclusion: 逆MERA学习在有限测量与噪声下可获得高保真度并具备可靠的证书框架；测量调度等价性与后选择放大规律为协议设计提供了理论依据。在n=16的架构对照中，MERA相对局域电路在保真度、长程误差、能量与熵上均占优，尽管更高门暴露度并未转化为更优性能，凸显了架构结构而非参数或门数量对学习质量的决定性作用。

Abstract: Structured quantum-state learning not only depends on an expressive ansatz but also on an operational certificate that stays meaningful with finite measurements and imperfect implementation. We study pure one dimensional states learning by an inverse binary multiscale entanglement renormalization ansatz (MERA). In the learning procedure, the qubits removed during coarse graining are controlled coherently and measured together at the terminal register. We confirm that an ideal sequential and terminal measurement schedule delivers the same complete bit string distribution under matched causal operations, while normalized postselection can amplify perturbations inversely with prefix acceptance. A noise aware theorem introduces an individual calibrated total variation implementation budget to the finite shot certificate. The protocol is estimated on an open boundary transverse field Ising ground state. A frozen 8-qubit schedule using $560$ million simulated training measurements per run achieves fidelity above $0.99$ in all $60$ held-out runs, with a mean fidelity of $0.996886$. 1080 circuit-noise cells and 6480 confidence-coverage rows are covered by fixed-circuit robustness validation without a locked soundness violation. We then address architectural fairness at $n=16$ using three new studies. In a 120-run exact-gradient multistart diagnostic, MERA has higher fidelity in 58/60 paired restarts and lower long-range error in 60/60, although no run met the prespecified stationarity criterion. Finally, a causal cone-complete, parameter matched local circuit achieves $2.62\times$ greater aggregate gate exposure yet loses all 30 paired comparisons in fidelity, long-range error, energy, and entropy.

</details>


### [114] [Interactions Protect Periodically Driven Time Crystals Against Dephasing but Destabilise Multipolar-Driven Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33574)
*Karthikeya Machiraju,Kaustav Bhowmick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于无序踢动Ising模型（N=12,16自旋），通过精确对角化研究相互作用强度与配位数如何影响周期及随机多极驱动下时间晶体序在退相位下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨周期驱动时间晶体序在退相位噪声下的保护机制：相互作用强度与配位结构（而非总相互作用强度）是否决定相干时间的稳定性。

Method: 对含非理想π脉冲与逐格点退相位的无序踢动Ising模型（N=12、16）做精确对角化；对比周期驱动、n=0,1,2阶随机多极驱动与Thue-Morse序列；并在相同总相互作用强度zJ下比较网格与环状几何以分离耦合强度与配位数。

Result: 周期驱动下相干时间约按1/γ标度，强耦合降低退相位作用的横向倾角；n≥1多极驱动中环上耦合加倍使相干时间缩短至0.54(n=1)、0.37(n=2)、0.44(Thue-Morse)，而随机符号(n=0)不受影响；无序扫描支持"翻转框架"图像（相互作用破坏脉冲误差的块抵消）；相同zJ下网格比环寿命长出2.15(N=12)与2.78(N=16)倍，且三种相干时间定义下结论一致。

Conclusion: 更强的相互作用可保护周期驱动时间晶体序但会破坏随机多极驱动下的序，且配位结构超越总相互作用强度、也超越翻转框架图像而起作用。

Abstract: We show that stronger interactions protect periodically driven time-crystal order against dephasing but destabilise order under random multipolar drives, and that coordination matters beyond the total interaction strength. We studied a disordered kicked Ising model of $N=12$ and $16$ spins with imperfect $π$ pulses and per-site dephasing by exact diagonalisation, comparing a periodic drive, random multipolar drives of order $n=0,1,2$ and the Thue-Morse sequence, and separated coupling strength from coordination by comparing a grid with a ring at equal total interaction strength $zJ$. For the periodic drive the coherence time scales approximately as $1/γ$, and stronger coupling reduces the transverse tilt on which dephasing acts. For multipolar drives of order $n\ge1$, doubling the coupling on a ring shortens the coherence time to $0.54$ ($n=1$), $0.37$ ($n=2$) and $0.44$ (Thue-Morse) at $N=16$, while random signs ($n=0$) are unaffected. A disorder scan supports a toggling-frame picture in which interactions spoil the block cancellation of the pulse error. At equal $zJ$ the grid outlives the ring by $2.15$ ($N=12$) and $2.78$ ($N=16$) for the periodic drive despite an identical transverse tilt, a coordination effect beyond this picture. All ratios hold under three definitions of the coherence time.

</details>


### [115] [Simultaneously Query-Optimal Quantum Linear-System Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33686)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Chenghua Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子线性系统算法可同时达到矩阵查询复杂度 O(κlog(1/ε)) 与右端项查询复杂度 O(κ/s)，并给出同实例最坏情形下的联合下界，从而解决了这两个最优界能否同时实现的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性系统算法通过块编码预言机 U_A 访问系数矩阵、通过态制备预言机 U_b 访问右端项。已知二者的最优查询复杂度分别为 O(κlog(1/ε)) 和 O(κ/s)（其中 s=α‖A^{-1}b‖），但能否用单一算法同时达到这两个界一直未决，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 在给定 s 的常数因子估计的前提下，设计一种量子线性系统算法，将态制备与精度细化分离：先完成与精度无关的态制备，再单独提升精度，从而使矩阵查询次数依赖目标精度 ε，而右端项查询次数不依赖 ε。同时构造同实例最坏情形用于下界证明。

Result: 所提算法以误差 ε 制备归一化解，同时使用 O(κlog(1/ε)) 次 U_A 查询和 O(κ/s) 次 U_b 查询。下界方面，对任意量子线性系统算法与任意解范数尺度，存在单个 Hermitian 实例，使该算法同时需要 Ω(κlog(1/ε)) 次矩阵查询和 Ω(κ/s) 次态制备查询。

Conclusion: 两类预言机复杂度可被同时优化，所得界在联合意义下最优，原先的公开问题得到肯定回答。

Abstract: Quantum linear-system algorithms access the coefficient matrix through a block-encoding oracle $U_A$ and the right-hand side through a state-preparation oracle $U_b$. The optimal query complexities of these two oracles are known separately: $O(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries to $U_A$ and $O(κ/s)$ queries to $U_b$, where $s=α\|A^{-1}b\|$. Whether a single algorithm can achieve both bounds simultaneously has remained open.
  We resolve this question affirmatively. Given a constant-factor estimate of $s$, we give a quantum linear-system algorithm that prepares the normalized solution to error $\varepsilon$ using simultaneously $ O\left(κ\log\frac1\varepsilon\right)$ queries to $U_A$ and $O\left(κ/{s}\right)$ queries to $U_b$. The algorithm separates state preparation from precision refinement, so only the matrix-query count depends on the target accuracy.
  We also prove a matching same-instance worst-case lower bound: for every quantum linear-system algorithm and every solution-norm scale, there exists a single Hermitian instance on which that algorithm requires both $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix queries and $Ω(κ/s)$ state-preparation queries. Thus the two oracle complexities can be optimized simultaneously, and the resulting bounds are jointly optimal.

</details>


### [116] [On Embedding Design in Quantum Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33700)
*Ban Q. Tran,Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Susan Mengel,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出统一嵌入框架，将嵌入视为塑造变分电路特征表示的函数变换，并据此提出两种量子物理信息神经网络嵌入方法：LQNN-TE-QPINN（以辅助量子电路生成数据相关特征并与坐标相加）与AdaFreq-QPINN（将arccos-Chebyshev编码的频率缩放因子设为可训练参数）。在一维与二维Burgers方程上，二者显著优于直接编码与固定解析频率编码的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理信息神经网络（QPINN）通过物理残差训练参数化量子电路求解PDE，但坐标到量子态的嵌入映射作用尚未被充分理解，缺乏统一的理论刻画与系统性评估。

Method: 提出统一嵌入框架，把嵌入形式化为塑造变分电路可用特征表示的函数变换。在该框架下设计两种嵌入：LQNN-TE-QPINN用辅助量子电路生成数据相关特征，并将其与输入坐标相加（而非此前公式中的相乘）；AdaFreq-QPINN将arccos-Chebyshev编码的频率缩放因子参数化为可训练变量。在Burgers方程上，将二者与直接编码、固定解析频率编码及混合架构进行对比评估。

Result: 一维Burgers方程中，LQNN-TE-QPINN以720个可训练参数取得相对L2误差0.0916，优于直接编码QPINN的0.4501，以及参数约多11倍的局部自适应经典PINN的0.1233；AdaFreq-QPINN仅增加4个参数即取得解析嵌入中的最低误差。二维情形下，LQNN-TE-QPINN取得最低训练目标，但解误差与数据重上传QPINN未明显区分。在模拟硬件噪声下，LQNN-TE-QPINN保持最低绝对导数误差，但相对其无噪基线退化最明显。

Conclusion: 嵌入设计显著影响QPINN的逼近与优化行为，统一嵌入框架为量子PDE求解器的嵌入选择提供了理论依据与实证指导。

Abstract: Quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) solve partial differential equations (PDEs) by training parameterized quantum circuits against physics-based residuals, yet the role of the embedding that maps coordinates into quantum states remains insufficiently understood. In this research, we introduce a unified embedding framework that formulates embedding as a functional transformation shaping the feature representation available to the variational circuit, and we propose two embeddings within it. The Linear Quantum Neural Network Trainable Embedding QPINN (LQNN-TE-QPINN) generates data-dependent features with an auxiliary quantum circuit and adds them to the input coordinates, instead of multiplying them as in our previous formulation. The adaptive-frequency QPINN (AdaFreq-QPINN) promotes the frequency-scaling factors of an arccos-Chebyshev encoding to trainable parameters. We evaluate both against direct and fixed analytical frequency encodings and alternative hybrid architectures on one- and two-dimensional Burgers equations. In one dimension, LQNN-TE-QPINN achieves a relative $L_2$ error of 0.0916 with 720 trainable parameters, compared with 0.4501 for direct-encoding QPINN and 0.1233 for a locally adaptive classical physics-informed neural network with approximately eleven times more parameters, while AdaFreq-QPINN attains the lowest error among the analytical embeddings with only four additional parameters. In two dimensions, LQNN-TE-QPINN attains the lowest training objective, although its solution error is not clearly separated from that of a data re-uploading QPINN. Under simulated hardware noise, LQNN-TE-QPINN retains the lowest absolute derivative error while degrading most relative to its noiseless baseline. These results demonstrate that embedding design substantially influences the approximation and optimization behavior of QPINNs.

</details>


### [117] [Autonomous phase discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33802)
*Shiyu Zhou,Yuxuan Zhang,Sebastian Wetzel,Roger Melko,Xiu-Zhe Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将可微编程与无监督学习结合，构建全自主系统，沿自适应轨迹搜索基态数据以发现量子相，在无预设相标签或目标相的情况下显著优于等预算随机采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相研究依赖对称性、拓扑等数学工具与物理直觉，但缺乏在未知组织原理的哈密顿量空间中进行通用探索的方法。

Method: 结合可微编程与无监督学习，构建全自主搜索系统；沿自适应轨迹而非固定参数网格评估基态数据；使用三种不同求解器实现，并在等基态评估预算下与随机采样对比。

Result: 在含多达200个不同相的广义团簇链上，同预算下多发现至多25个相，并在30倍预算下与随机采样持平；在50参数Chern绝缘体上，到达简谐构造未获得的区域，同时覆盖采样找到的全部区域。

Conclusion: 梯度驱动的哈密顿量空间自主探索，是在无相标签、无指定目标相条件下发现量子相的可行途径。

Abstract: Understanding quantum phases of matter has long relied on physicists' intuition and mathematical tools such as symmetry and topology. Remarkably successful as these approaches have been, they provide no universal way to explore a Hamiltonian space whose organizing principle is not known in advance. In this work, we introduce a fully autonomous system combining differentiable programming and unsupervised learning for quantum phase discovery. The search evaluates ground-state data along an adaptive trajectory rather than on a predetermined parameter grid. We demonstrate the system with three different solvers and benchmark it against random sampling at equal ground-state-evaluation budgets. On a generalized cluster chain hosting up to $200$ distinct phases, the search finds up to $25$ more phases at the same budget, and matches random sampling given thirty times its budget. On a $50$-parameter Chern insulator, it reaches sectors not obtained by the simple harmonic constructions considered here, in a family whose inverse problem remains open, while recovering all sectors found by sampling. Our results establish autonomous, gradient-driven exploration of Hamiltonian space as a practical route to discovering quantum phases without phase labels or a prescribed target phase.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum data processing equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33815)
*Tai-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子数据处理不等式提升为精确等式，通过复平面条带上的边界与表面Poisson积分表示，把相对熵损失分解为Petz恢复项与相对模流失配边界项，并给出BS损失的类似分解及最优平方根标度的恢复界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子数据处理不等式仅给出相对熵在量子信道下单调不增的定性结论，缺乏对损失量的精确定量刻画。作者希望将这一基本不等式精确化，揭示相对熵损失的物理机制，并改进以往恢复界对小特征值的逆幂依赖。

Method: 在复平面条带上构造边界与表面Poisson积分表示，将相对熵损失分解为两个独立非负机制：刻画态可重构性的操作型Petz恢复分量与度量相对模动力学失配的边界项；进一步将损失表示为原态与其平均Petz恢复态之间的测量相对熵加非负变分余项；对Belavkin–Staszewski损失给出类似积分表示，分解为负对数Uhlmann保真度与平均重构加非负变分余项。

Result: 得到了相对熵损失与BS损失的精确等式分解；建立了任意指定模参数（含标准未旋转Petz映射）下的恢复界，相对熵损失达到最优平方根标度；前置因子依赖于不同特征值计数或对数谱量，改进了以往界中小特征值的逆幂依赖。

Conclusion: 量子数据处理不等式可被精确化为等式，相对熵损失具有可操作的Petz恢复与相对模动力学失配两部分结构；所得恢复界在标度与谱依赖上优于既有结果。

Abstract: The quantum data processing inequality asserts that quantum relative entropy is monotonically non-increasing under quantum channels. Here, we promote this fundamental inequality to an exact equality via boundary and surface Poisson integral representations across a complex strip. The relative-entropy loss is resolved into two independent, nonnegative physical mechanisms: an operational Petz recovery component governing state reconstructibility, and a boundary term measuring the mismatch of relative modular dynamics. We further express the loss as the measured relative entropy between the original state and its averaged Petz recovery state plus a nonnegative variational remainder. For the Belavkin--Staszewski (BS) loss, analogous integral representations yield a decomposition into negative log Uhlmann fidelity with an averaged reconstruction plus a nonnegative variational remainder. Furthermore, we establish recovery bounds at any prescribed modular parameter, including the canonical unrotated Petz map, with optimal square-root scaling in the relative entropy loss. The prefactors depend on distinct-eigenvalue counts or logarithmic spectral quantities, improving upon the inverse-power dependence on small eigenvalues in previous bounds.

</details>


### [119] [Dynamic Sculpting of Photon Statistics in a Raman-Coupled Cavity QED System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33837)
*Amir Rahmani,Alireza Dehghani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通过量子干涉主动在时间上调控光子统计特性的方案，基于拉曼耦合的腔-原子系统结合参量放大，通过时间调制失谐实现聚束与反聚束的动态切换，并在连续波下获得时间门控的反聚束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子统计的控制对经典与量子光子学应用至关重要，但稳态光子统计已被广泛研究，如何在时间上动态操纵这些统计特性仍具挑战性，因此需要一种主动调控聚束与反聚束之间转换的机制。

Method: 采用拉曼耦合的腔-原子系统并引入参量放大，通过时间调制中间态失谐来加深光子阻塞或增强光子聚束；同时调制腔失谐与中间态失谐以产生静态条件或单独调制任一失谐无法实现的反聚束。

Result: 时间调制中间态失谐可分别加深光子阻塞或增强光子聚束；同时调制腔和中间态失谐产生反聚束；由于调制具有周期性，反聚束以与调制频率决定的重复率在连续波操作下规律性重现，实现时间门控的光子反聚束。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种基于拉曼辅助量子干涉的通用的动态调控光子关联机制，可实现时间门控光子反聚束，为量子光子学应用提供灵活手段。

Abstract: Controlling photon statistics is pivotal to many applications in classical and quantum photonics. While steady-state photon statistics have been widely studied, dynamically manipulating these statistical properties in time remains elusive. Herein, we propose a scheme to actively control photon statistics between bunching and antibunching regimes through quantum interference. Our approach relies on a Raman-coupled cavity-atom system with parametric amplification, where time modulating the detuning of the intermediate state allows us to either deepen the photon blockade or enhance photon bunching. Simultaneous modulation of the cavity and intermediate-state detunings produces antibunching that cannot be achieved under static conditions or by modulating either detuning alone. Because the modulation is periodic, the antibunching reappears at regular time intervals, enabling time-gated photon antibunching under continuous-wave operation at a repetition rate determined by the modulation frequency. These results show a versatile mechanism based on Raman-assisted quantum interference for dynamically engineering photon correlations.

</details>


### [120] [Nonlinear engineering of Hong-Ou-Mandel interference with structured light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33862)
*Simon Darveau,Bienvenu Ndagano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过调控泵浦场的宇称与空间模式（HG/LG），可实现对HOM干涉中聚束-反聚束转换行为（形状、频率）的工程化控制，并展示了随模式阶数增强的灵敏度以及正比于OAM的整数倍角分辨率增强，为HOM增强的旋转传感提供可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进入Hong-Ou-Mandel（HOM）干涉仪的光子对，其聚束或反聚束取决于其在被测自由度上量子态的对称性，从而产生相长或相消干涉。对自发参量下转换产生的光子对，该对称性此前多通过线性变换与投影来调控；本文希望采用另一种途径——直接裁剪泵浦场（泵浦宇称与空间模式）来调控这一对称性，并探索其带来的转换特性与传感潜力。

Method: 理论分析与实验实现相结合。采用编码在Hermite-Gaussian（HG）与Laguerre-Gaussian（LG）基下的旋转结构泵浦模式，研究泵浦场宇称对干涉转换过程的影响，并与NOON态类比分析角分辨率增强。

Result: 在HG情形下，从相长到相消干涉的转换呈现出该模式族独有的丰富结构，且随模式阶数提高，灵敏度增强；在LG情形下，如同NOON态，角分辨率获得整数倍增强，增强因子正比于轨道角动量（OAM）含量。

Conclusion: 泵浦场宇称会影响HOM干涉中从相长到相消转换的形状与频率；利用结构化旋转泵浦模式可提升灵敏度和角分辨率，从而为基于HOM干涉的旋转传感开辟了可能性。

Abstract: A pair of photons entering a Hong-Ou-Mandel interferometer will bunch or anti-bunch depending on the symmetry of their state in the measured degree of freedom, resulting in constructive or destructive interference. For photon pairs generated through spontaneous parametric down-conversion, this symmetry can be engineered through linear transformations and projections applied to the photon pairs, or by tailoring the pump field. Here, we adopt the latter approach to demonstrate that the pump's parity affects the shape and frequency of the transition from constructive to destructive interference. We demonstrate this concept theoretically and experimentally using a rotating structured pump mode encoded in the Hermite-Gaussian and Laguerre-Gaussian bases. In the former case, we show that the transition exhibits a rich structure unique to the mode family, resulting in enhanced sensitivity as the mode order increases. In the latter case, we demonstrate, as with NOON states, an integer enhancement in angular resolution by a factor proportional to the orbital angular momentum content, thereby opening the possibility of Hong-Ou-Mandel-enhanced rotation sensing.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum-Computing Self-Consistent Kohn-Sham DFT: Plane-Wave-Orthonormalized Orbitals with a Dual-Basis Quantum Eigensolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33934)
*Lazaro Calderin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种面向量子计算机的第一量子化、全电子、全势Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论方法，结合经典优化的量子线路本征求解器与PWOO基组及对偶基收缩策略，在H$_8$链上验证了其精度与噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算机上实现全电子、全势的Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，需要能处理自洽场（SCF）本征问题的高效量子本征求解方案，同时需在有限量子比特与噪声条件下保持与经典计算相当的精度。

Method: 采用第一量子化、全电子、全势KS-DFT框架，配合经典优化的量子线路本征求解器求解任意正交归一基下的SCF本征问题。构造PWOO基（平面波与对其中及彼此正交归一化的原子轨道结合）。本征求解器采用对偶基形式：经全基初始化步骤后，每步SCF在含K个函数的收缩基（$\lceil\log_2 K\rceil$个量子比特）中求解，当基外残差超阈值时在全基中更新。以H$_8$链（SVWN泛函，K=8）进行态矢模拟及含噪声模拟验证，并在IonQ Forte-1上开展实验。

Result: H$_8$链（SVWN，K=8）的态矢模拟SCF结果与经典计算在32768个平面波及至cc-pVTZ的PWOO基下符合至0.02 mHa以内；基组收缩使PWOO能量变化至多5.7 μHa。含模拟散粒噪声时，STO-3G PWOO SCF收敛至接近无噪声不动点。在IonQ Forte-1上，三量子比特寄存器读出在单标准差内复现无噪声PWOO-AO能量差。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在量子计算机上实现高精度全电子KS-DFT的可行路径，对偶基量子本征求解与PWOO基组结合可在有限量子比特数下达到经典级别精度，并具备对散粒噪声与真实硬件噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present a first-quantization, all-electron, full-potential Kohn-Sham density functional theory method for quantum computers, based on a classically optimized quantum-circuit eigensolver for the self-consistent field (SCF) eigenproblems in any orthonormal basis. The plane-wave-orthonormalized-orbital (PWOO) basis combines plane waves with atomic orbitals (AOs) orthonormalized against them and one another. The eigensolver has a dual-basis form: after full-basis bootstrap steps, it solves each SCF step in a contracted basis of $K$ functions ($\lceil\log_2 K\rceil$ qubits), updated in the full basis when the residual outside it exceeds a threshold. For an H$_8$ chain (SVWN, $K=8$), the state-vector-emulated SCF agrees with classical calculations within 0.02 mHa for 32768 plane waves and PWOO bases up to cc-pVTZ; contraction changes these PWOO energies by at most 5.7 $μ$Ha. With emulated shot noise, the STO-3G PWOO SCF converges near the noise-free fixed point. On IonQ Forte-1, three-qubit-register readout reproduces the noise-free PWOO-AO energy difference within one standard deviation.

</details>


### [122] [Hardness of Approximating Quantum Code Distance Beyond $\sqrt{N}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33956)
*Upendra Kapshikar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究稳定子码最小距离近似问题的计算复杂度，将对数加法近似间隙从O(√N)收紧到线性αN（在NP⊄coRP假设下不可行），并开启细粒度复杂度研究，证明在SETH与非一致Gap-ETH下不存在快速算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最小距离问题即使线性加法间隙也是NP难近似；而量子码此前仅有Kapshikar-Kundu与Grigorescu-Jha-Samperton的归约，仅达到块长N的O(√N)加法间隙，两者之间存在明显差距，作者旨在弥合该差距并进一步刻画精确的复杂度阈值。

Method: 通过复杂度归约与假设性论证：一是构造从NP问题到稳定子码距离近似（加法间隙αN）的随机化归约；二是开展细粒度复杂度分析，针对逻辑量子比特数κ与块长N分别建立基于SETH和Gap-ETH的条件性下界。

Result: 证明除非NP⊆coRP，否则不存在随机算法能将距离近似到常数α>0的加法间隙αN；细粒度方面，对任意ε>0，除非SETH失效，否则不存在2^((1-ε)κ)·poly(N)时间的随机算法计算κ个逻辑量子比特码的距离；且在块长、逻辑量子比特数与间隙同时线性的实例上，除非非一致Gap-ETH失效，否则不存在2^{o(N)}时间的随机算法近似到线性加法间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了稳定子码最小距离近似的复杂度：将对数间隙推进到线性加法间隙并与经典情形对齐，同时在细粒度层面给出了基于SETH与Gap-ETH的严格下界，表明这一问题在参数化与指数时间假设下均具有本质计算难度。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of approximating the minimum distance of a stabilizer code. Classically, the minimum distance problem is NP-hard to approximate even to within an additive gap linear in the block length. For quantum codes, however, the reductions of Kapshikar and Kundu and of Grigorescu, Jha and Samperton reach only an additive $O(\sqrt{N})$ gap in the block length $N$. We close this gap and, in a separate direction, initiate the fine-grained study of the problem.
  We show that no randomized algorithm approximates the distance to within an additive $αN$, for a constant $α> 0$, unless $\mathsf{NP} \subseteq \mathsf{coRP}$. In the fine-grained setting, for every $\varepsilon > 0$, no randomized $2^{(1-\varepsilon)κ}\,\mathrm{poly}(N)$-time algorithm computes the distance of a code with $κ$ logical qubits unless SETH falls; and no randomized $2^{o(N)}$-time algorithm approximates the distance to within a linear additive gap, on instances where block length, number of logical qubits, and gap are simultaneously linear, unless non-uniform Gap-ETH falls.

</details>


### [123] [Swapping Quantum Annealing Errors into a Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33975)
*Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出腔量子退火方案：将退火中因小能隙产生的错误通过腔模转换为光子，从而加速退火并实现结果认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火在小能隙Δ处会变慢，且该处产生的错误无法被检测。

Method: 引入腔量子退火：在退火中途耦合频率为O(1)≫Δ的腔模，把错误交换为光子；在p-spin模型中验证。预置光子可反向运行交换过程以制备激发态。

Result: 退火时间缩短至无腔情形所需时间的近平方根；通过光子计数认证结果，高产光子运行以O(1)产额将基态保真度提升至99.99%以上，且增益随系统规模指数增长。

Conclusion: 腔可增强基于量子比特的计算，为量子退火实现高保真量子态制备开辟道路。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising form of quantum computation, but it slows down at a small energy gap $Δ$ during the anneal, and the errors it makes there remain undetected. We introduce cavity quantum annealing, in which a cavity mode of frequency $O(1)\ggΔ$, coupled mid-schedule, swaps these errors into photons. In the $p$-spin model, this reduces the annealing time to nearly the square root of that required without the cavity. Moreover, counting the photons certifies the result: high-photon runs, with an $O(1)$ yield, reach a ground-state fidelity above $99.99\%$ orders of magnitude sooner, a gain that grows exponentially with system size. A pre-loaded photon instead runs the swap in reverse, preparing an excited state. More broadly, our work shows that cavities can enhance computations performed with qubits, paving the way toward high-fidelity quantum state preparation by quantum annealing.

</details>


### [124] [QBX: A Compiler for 2-local Qubit Hamiltonian Simulation on Quantum Chiplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33997)
*Zikun Li,Zhuoming Chen,Zhihao Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QBX，首个面向量子芯粒（chiplet）架构的2-local量子比特哈密顿量模拟编译器，通过分层优化与highway机制显著降低跨芯粒通信开销并聚合Pauli串为多目标受控门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-local量子比特哈密顿量模拟是量子计算的基础任务，应用广泛。现有通用芯粒编译器仅针对门级程序，无法利用哈密顿量模拟的高层语义优化；现有哈密顿量模拟专用编译器面向单体架构，扩展性有限且无法适应芯粒异构性，导致电路输出次优。

Method: QBX采用可扩展的分层方法，减少跨芯粒通信的数量与距离；集成面向芯粒的highway机制以实现高效长距离量子比特通信，降低跨芯粒交互成本；并将2-local Pauli串高效聚合为多目标受控门，结合highway进行深度优化。

Result: 评估表明，QBX在电路深度和操作数量上显著优于通用编译器与领域专用编译器，同时可扩展至更大规模的2-local哈密顿量模拟。

Conclusion: QBX是首个针对芯粒架构的2-local哈密顿量模拟专用编译器，通过高层语义驱动的分层优化与highway通信机制，在关键电路指标上超越现有方案并具备良好可扩展性。

Abstract: 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation, a fundamental task in quantum computing, is widely applied in various applications. This paper presents QBX, the first quantum compiler designed for 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation on quantum chiplet architectures. Existing general-purpose quantum compilers for chiplet architectures target quantum programs at the gate level and miss optimizations requiring high-level semantics of Hamiltonian simulation. Conversely, existing domain-specific compilers for Hamiltonian simulation are designed for monolithic architectures, and their limited scalability and inability to adapt to the heterogeneity of chiplet architectures lead to sub-optimal circuit outputs. QBX implements a scalable hierarchical approach to reduce both the amount and distance of cross-chiplet communications. It integrates the highway mechanism, a chiplet-oriented solution for efficient long-range qubit communication, to diminish cross-chiplet interaction costs. Furthermore, QBX efficiently aggregates 2-local Pauli strings into multi-target controlled gates, harnessing highways for deep optimization. Our evaluations show that QBX markedly outperforms both general-purpose and domain-specific compilers in circuit depth and operation count, while scaling to much larger 2-local Hamiltonian simulations.

</details>


### [125] [Nakayama's reduction of quantum topos and Bayesian quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34200)
*Atsuhide Mori*
