Abstract: Time-multiplexed (TDM) photonic reservoirs are usually judged by their scores on a few datasets, which say little about a chip's overall performance, and designed by enumerating candidate circuits and simulating each one, which is inefficient and gives no guarantee of finding a good structure. For a time-unrolled passive linear network with data-modulated gates or sources and a ridge readout, we derive an exact bridge from the task to the prediction risk in three steps. (i) Task features: the finite-shot risk depends on the time series only through finitely many statistics of its training windows and their correlations with the target, selected by the encoding and the optical paths. (ii) Encoding: every output moment is a polynomial in the features of the data-modulated sites, with degree and support bounded by tuples of optical paths. Gate encoding reads the characteristic function and creates interactions across time lags; squeezing encoding reads the moment-generating function and displacement encoding low-order moments, and with quadrature receivers they yield only additive and linear models, respectively, for every topology. (iii) Readout: for homodyne, heterodyne and photon-number receivers the risk closes exactly, and its dependence on the number of shots is an explicit sum over signal-to-noise modes; threshold clicks admit finite-dictionary approximations with certified error. Parts of the network that share no light carry independent states, which a receiver combines only through measurement events that join them. Checks on seven datasets confirm every exact statement to machine precision. The three steps thus yield a useful space of candidate TDM architectures, pruned by exact statements rather than by trial simulation, which can in future guide the selection of TDM chips for target requirements.

</details>


### [164] [Gauge freedom and efficient algorithms for Lindbladian learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35265)
*Steven T Flammia,Savar D Sinha,Yu Tong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在存在SPAM噪声时学习局部Lindbladian的问题，刻画了因规范变换导致的本质不可学习障碍，并为所有通用规范不变量分量给出SPAM鲁棒的高效学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作虽提出了在理想访问假设下可扩展的Lindbladian学习算法，但SPAM噪声会使不同的Lindbladian在实验上不可区分，从而使部分分量根本不可学习。因此需要一个对SPAM障碍的精确刻画，以及在此条件下仍可学习的算法框架。

Method: 针对有界度局部Lindbladian，识别出一族与可信单量子比特控制对易的保局域性规范变换，并据此对Lindbladian分量进行分类。对一般依赖规范的分量，构造Lindbladian实例证明其不确定性；对通用规范不变量分量，设计仅使用可信单量子比特操作、无需辅助比特与纠缠控制的SPAM鲁棒学习算法。

Result: 对依赖规范的分量，即使在完全正性约束下仍可具有与系统规模无关的Ω(1)不确定性。所提算法在常数局域性和度数下，使用O~(ε^{-2}log(N/δ))次实验及总演化时间即可学习所有通用规范不变量分量；保证允许全局SPAM随N增长而任意偏离理想情形，仅需非消失的局部可见度。另给出物理正性约束下额外Hamiltonian系数边界诱导规范不变的充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文给出了SPAM导致的Lindbladian学习障碍的锐利刻画，并提供了规范感知的SPAM消除算法工具包，可实现开放量子系统的可扩展刻画；额外参数的一般可学习性仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a local Lindbladian in the presence of state-preparation-and-measurement (SPAM) noise. Although recent work has developed scalable learning algorithms under idealized access assumptions, SPAM can make distinct Lindbladians experimentally indistinguishable, thus making part of the Lindbladian fundamentally unlearnable. We give a sharp characterization of this obstruction for bounded-degree local Lindbladians. We identify a family of locality-preserving gauge transformations that commutes with trusted single-qubit control, and use it to classify Lindbladian components. For the generically gauge dependent components, we construct Lindbladians to show that they can have $Ω(1)$ uncertainty independent of system size, even after imposing complete positivity.
  We complement this characterization with SPAM-robust algorithms for learning every universally gauge-invariant component. Our algorithms use only trusted single-qubit operations, require neither ancillas nor entangling control, and use $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\left(ε^{-2}\log(N/δ)\right)$ experiments and total evolution time for constant locality and degree. Our guarantees allow global SPAM to become arbitrarily far from ideal as $N$ grows, requiring only nonvanishing local visibility. We also establish sufficient conditions for boundary-induced gauge invariance of additional Hamiltonian coefficients under physical positivity constraints, though learning these additional parameters in general remains an open problem. More broadly, our gauge-aware framework and SPAM-cancellation techniques offer a practical toolkit for the scalable characterization of open quantum systems.

</details>


### [165] [Generalized LIMDDs: Succinctness and Canonicity for Decision Diagrams Modulo a Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35271)
*Arend-Jan Quist,Alexis de Colnet,Thomas Reps,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将决策图视为定长字上函数的最小自动机，用群作用粗化Myhill–Nerode商，研究由$C^{\leq k}R_q$（含/不含$X$）生成的群族，证明其相对Pauli-LIMDD具有指数级简洁性提升并完全确定简洁性阶，给出五规则归约系统与多项式时间规范形算法，同时指出超出（反）对角群时最小规范形计算具有非局部性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RODD（约简有序决策图）可作为定长字上函数的最小自动机，其节点即残差、约简即Myhill–Nerode商。作者希望研究对该商按群作用进一步粗化的情形：将同一$G$-轨道内的残差节点合并并在边上记录群元素。对$\{0,1\}^n\to\mathbb{C}$与Pauli群$G$，这正对应Pauli-LIMDD。动机是探索更一般的群族能否带来更强的压缩能力，并厘清其可判定性、变换性与规范形性质。

Method: 固定群$G$作用于残差，按$G$-轨道合并节点并在边上记录群元素。取$G$来自由$C^{\leq k}R_q$（阶为$q$的相位旋转，至多$k$个控制量子比特）生成的双参数群族，分别考虑含与不含比特翻转$X$的版本。以超图态作为分离对象，分析不同群成员相对Pauli-LIMDD的简洁性；建立五规则归约系统以刻画唯一规范形；分析五个查询与八个变换的可处理性；研究最小规模规范形的计算复杂度与非局部性。

Result: 该族相对Pauli-LIMDD具有指数级简洁性提升，且其简洁性阶被完全确定。超图态总可被该族某成员高效表示，并作为分离实例。五个查询与八个变换的可处理性在该族内保持不变，且与Pauli-LIMDD表现一致。五规则归约系统对族内每个成员给出唯一规范形，该规范形可在LIMDD规模的多项式时间内计算。若粗化超出（反）对角群，最小规模规范形的计算是非局部的，可能需要重建整个图。

Conclusion: 通过以群作用粗化RODD的Myhill–Nerode商，可统一刻画Pauli-LIMDD及其推广。由$C^{\leq k}R_q$（含/不含$X$）生成的双参数群族在保持与Pauli-LIMDD相同的查询/变换可处理性的同时，带来指数级简洁性增益，并具备多项式时间可计算且唯一的规范形。但一旦粗化超出（反）对角群，最小规范形问题变为非局部，可能需重建整图，标志着该压缩范式的边界。

Abstract: A reduced ordered decision diagram is the minimal automaton of a function on words of fixed length: its nodes are the residuals, and reduction is the Myhill--Nerode quotient. We study what happens when that quotient is coarsened by a group. Fix a group $G$ acting on residuals, merge two nodes when their residuals lie in one $G$-orbit, and record the group element on the edge. For functions $\{0,1\}^n\to\mathbb{C}$ and $G$ the Pauli group this is the Local Invertible Map Decision Diagram. We take $G$ from the two-parameter family generated by $C^{\leq k}R_q$, the phase rotation of order $q$ with up to $k$ control qubits, with and without the bit flip $X$. We show that this gives exponential succinctness improvements compared to Pauli-LIMDD, and we determine the succinctness order of the family completely. The separating objects are hypergraph states, which can always be efficiently represented by some member of the family. We settle the tractability of five queries and eight transformations, which is invariant across the family, and shows the same behavior as Pauli-LIMDD. We give a five-rule reduction system whose normal forms are unique for every member of the family, and we show that this canonical form is computable in polynomial time in the size of the LIMDD. We show that, when coarsening beyond the (anti-)diagonal groups, the calculation of a minimal sized normal form turns out to be non-local and it might to rebuild the whole diagram.

</details>


### [166] [Circuit-level benchmarks of GKP-concatenated qLDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35282)
*Yuan Yao,Ruipeng Xing,Jianshuo Gao,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在统一仿真框架下比较BB码与tricycle码作为GKP外层码的性能，从码容量噪声推进到含噪综合征提取与调度分辨的位移传播。结果表明模拟信息辅助的BP-OSD在三类噪声下均优于硬判决译码，全电路模型下BB序列阈值约为0.212、tricycle约为0.142。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GKP码的可扩展容错量子纠错需要有限速率外层码以在真实电路噪声下利用模拟GKP信息，但qLDPC外层码作为电路级GKP级联候选尚未被系统比较。

Method: 采用统一仿真框架，对BB码与tricycle码进行基准测试，噪声模型从码容量噪声逐步推进到重复含噪综合征提取，再到调度分辨的位移传播；译码方面比较模拟信息辅助的BP-OSD与硬判决译码。

Result: 在三种噪声模型下，模拟信息辅助的BP-OSD均给出比硬判决译码更高的有限尺寸交叉估计。全电路模型中，所选BB序列的阈值σ_th约为0.212，对应压缩需求约10.46 dB；tricycle序列约为0.142，对应约13.94 dB。

Conclusion: 模拟GKP信息在电路级噪声下仍能改善译码；在本研究所用共同仿真假设下，所选BB序列的有限尺寸交叉估计高于所选tricycle序列。

Abstract: Scalable fault-tolerant quantum error correction based on Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes requires finite-rate outer codes that can exploit analog GKP information under realistic circuit noise. However, quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) outer codes have not been systematically compared as candidates for circuit-level GKP concatenation. Here, we benchmark BB and tricycle codes using a unified simulation framework that progresses from code-capacity noise to repeated noisy syndrome extraction and schedule-resolved displacement propagation. Across all three noise models, analog-informed belief-propagation with ordered-statistics decoding (BP-OSD) yields higher finite-size crossing estimates than hard-decision decoding. In the full circuit model, the analog-informed crossings are \(σ_{\mathrm{th}}\simeq0.212\) for the selected BB sequence and \(0.142\) for the tricycle sequence, corresponding to squeezing requirements of approximately \(10.46\,\mathrm{dB}\) and \(13.94\,\mathrm{dB}\), respectively. These results show that analog GKP information continues to improve decoding under circuit-level noise. Under the common simulation assumptions used here, the selected BB sequence also yields a higher finite-size crossing estimate than the selected tricycle sequence.

</details>


### [167] [Distributional Variants of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35327)
*Uma Girish,Kunal Mittal,Barak Nehoran,Ran Raz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子查询算法经典模拟猜想（在均匀分布下）在偏置乘积分布与等重量切片分布下均等价于原猜想，并进一步证明Aaronson-Ambainis多项式影响变量猜想在这些分布变体下也等价于原始版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂度理论中一个长期猜想认为：在均匀输入分布下，量子查询算法可被经典查询算法以多项式开销模拟，即量子算法平均接受概率可由经典算法逼近。该猜想关系到判定问题上的指数级量子优势是否必须依赖额外结构。作者希望理解该猜想是否依赖于均匀分布这一特定选择，因此研究其他自然输入分布下类似命题与原猜想的关系。

Method: 作者考虑两类分布：一是偏置乘积分布 μ_p，输入位为固定偏置 p 的独立伯努利变量；二是切片上的均匀分布 ν_p，即在汉明重量为 ⌊pn⌋ 的串集合上均匀分布。作者证明，对于每个固定 p∈(0,1)，μ_p 分布下量子查询算法具有多项式开销经典模拟当且仅当均匀分布下同样成立；并对 ν_p 分布证明类似的等价性。此外，作者在布尔超立方体上有界低阶多项式框架下，提出Aaronson-Ambainis猜想的偏置乘积分布与切片分布变体，并证明其均等价于原始Aaronson-Ambainis猜想。

Result: 对所有固定 p∈(0,1)，μ_p 分布下的量子查询算法经典模拟命题与均匀分布下原猜想等价；ν_p 分布（等重量切片均匀分布）下亦与均匀分布情形等价。相应地，Aaronson-Ambainis猜想在偏置乘积分布和切片均匀分布下的所有变体均等价于原始猜想。

Conclusion: 该均匀分布下的量子查询算法经典模拟猜想并不依赖于均匀分布这一特定选择：在偏置乘积分布和等重量切片分布下，其类似命题与原猜想等价。同时，Aaronson-Ambainis猜想在这些自然分布变体下也保持等价性，表明该猜想及其多项式表述具有较强的健壮性。

Abstract: A longstanding conjecture in quantum complexity theory asserts that, under the uniform input distribution, quantum query algorithms can be polynomially simulated by classical query algorithms. More precisely, the acceptance probability of any quantum query algorithm can be approximated, on average over uniformly random inputs, by a classical query algorithm, with only polynomial query overhead. The conjecture is central to understanding whether exponential quantum advantages for decision problems necessarily rely on additional structure.
  We study analogues of this conjecture under other natural input distributions and prove that they are all equivalent to the original uniform-distribution conjecture. We first consider the product distribution $μ_p$, where the input bits are independent Bernoulli variables with fixed bias $p$. We show that for every fixed $p \in (0, 1)$, quantum query algorithms under the $μ_p$ distribution admit polynomial-overhead classical simulations if and only if the same holds under the uniform distribution. Second, we consider the distribution $ν_p$ that is uniform over the slice of strings with Hamming weight $\lfloor pn \rfloor$ and prove a similar equivalence for the $ν_p$ distribution and the uniform distribution.
  The Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture is a stronger statement that implies the above-mentioned conjecture and is formulated in terms of bounded low-degree polynomials on the Boolean hypercube. It asserts that under the uniform distribution, any such polynomial with nonnegligible variance must have an influential variable. We formulate analogues of this conjecture, where the underlying distribution is a biased product distribution or a uniform distribution over a slice, and prove that all these variants are equivalent to the original Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture.

</details>


### [168] [Zero Knowledge Proofs in Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35339)
*Tuhin Paul,Srijani Das,Manasi Patra,Ramij Rahaman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子态在操作约束下不可区分性的零知识验证框架，构建了实现信息论安全的多方量子网络协议，突破了传统ZKP对计算假设和双方场景的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零知识证明构造依赖计算假设，且主要局限于双方（bipartite）场景，其信息论实现及网络场景基本未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用局部操作施加的根本限制，基于量子态在操作约束下的不可区分性，建立零知识验证框架；据此为多方量子网络构造显式协议。

Result: 证明了验证方在保持正确性验证能力的同时，受固有约束无法提取底层状态的信息；所构造协议实现信息论安全，任何合作方子集即使拥有无限联合计算能力也无法获得超出命题有效性之外的知识。

Conclusion: 该工作为双方及网络场景下的信息论零知识证明提供了可行路径，将零知识验证推广到多方量子网络并实现无条件安全。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) enable the verification of a statement without revealing any information beyond its validity and constitute a fundamental primitive in cryptography and information theory. However, existing constructions rely on computational assumptions and are predominantly confined to bipartite settings, leaving their information-theoretic realization in bipartite or network scenarios largely unexplored. Here we develop a framework for zero-knowledge verification based on the indistinguishability of quantum states under operational constraints. Exploiting the fundamental limitations imposed by local operations, we show that a verifier is inherently restricted from extracting information about the underlying state while retaining the ability to verify correctness. We construct explicit protocols for multiparty quantum networks that achieve information-theoretic security, ensuring that no subset of collaborating parties can gain knowledge beyond the validity of the statement, independent of their joint computational power.

</details>


### [169] [Heuristic lower bounds on real Grothendieck constants of finite and infinite order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35399)
*Erika Bene,Tamás Vértesi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用旋转不变三次核构造候选下界，推测实Grothendieck常数满足 $K_{\mathrm G}\geq9\pi/16$，并在维度4–24给出改进的有限维下界及纠缠维度见证候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进实Grothendieck常数 $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ 及其无穷维极限 $K_{\mathrm G}$ 的已知下界（如 $6\pi/11$），并探索其与Bell不等式、维度见证的联系。

Method: 采用旋转不变的三次核：在连续构造中，确定使半球二值策略在三次边界扰动下失稳的显式阈值，并辅以数值证据；同时在最高24维中，由单位向量构造有限对称系数矩阵，并使用大规模see-saw优化验证。

Result: 若阈值处半球策略全局最优的猜想成立，则 $K_{\mathrm G}\geq9\pi/16\simeq1.767146$，优于严格下界 $6\pi/11\simeq1.713596$。数值优化支持连续预测，并在 $d=4$ 至 $24$ 给出改进的 $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ 候选下界（以二值最优精确为前提）。部分矩阵作为二分关联Bell泛函时给出有限维维度见证候选；若相应二值上界成立，则每个参与方至少需要五维局域Hilbert空间。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了实Grothendieck常数的候选改进下界及有限维维度见证，但其结论依赖于数值支持的猜想（半球策略全局最优、二值最优精确及二值上界成立）。

Abstract: We propose candidate lower bounds on the real Grothendieck constants $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ and their infinite-dimensional limit $K_{\mathrm G}$ using rotationally invariant cubic kernels. For the continuous construction, we identify an explicit threshold above which hemispherical binary strategies are unstable under cubic boundary deformations. Supported by numerical evidence, we conjecture that hemispherical strategies are globally optimal at this threshold. This conjecture would imply $K_{\mathrm G}\geq9π/16\simeq1.767146$, improving the rigorous lower bound $6π/11\simeq1.713596$. We also construct finite symmetric coefficient matrices from unit vectors in dimensions up to $24$. Large-scale see-saw optimization supports the continuous predictions and suggests improved lower bounds on $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ in the studied dimensions from $4$ to $24$, conditional on the conjectured binary optima being exact. Selected matrices also yield finite-setting candidate dimension witnesses when interpreted as bipartite correlation Bell functionals. If the corresponding binary upper bounds hold, these witnesses would certify a local Hilbert-space dimension of at least five for each party.

</details>


### [170] [Dilation theorem for continuum quantum stochastic processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35403)
*Jonáš Fuksa,Clara Wassner,Jens Eisert,Gregory A. L. White*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出连续过程张量（cPT）框架下的膨胀定理，证明所有连续过程张量均可由"系统-环境"在有限哈密顿量耦合下的闭合描述任意逼近，从而为连续区间上的非马尔可夫动力学建立了严格的膨胀理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开放系统中，膨胀（dilation）是连接随机动力学与更大空间上确定性薛定谔演化的桥梁。然而，已有膨胀理论仅涵盖态、信道与量子梳等离散或有限情形，对于连续区间上完全一般的非马尔可夫动力学仍存在显著空白。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在配套工作引入的连续过程张量（cPT）框架下（该框架将过程表示为L²函数的多组分玻色Fock空间中的向量，可容纳量子系统在区间上的全部多时刻统计），本文构造并证明对应的膨胀定理：给出系统与环境通过有界哈密顿量耦合的显式闭合描述，证明任意cPT都可由其任意逼近。

Result: 所有cPT均可由系统与环境有界哈密顿量耦合的显式闭合描述任意逼近；此类正则表示构成全空间中的稠密子空间，便于处理；据此得到适用于连续情形的Choi对偶版本，并实现对连续控制（continuum controls）的严格处理。

Conclusion: 该膨胀定理不仅具有物理吸引力，还证明了cPT框架的完备性；正则表示实际支撑了整个形式体系，为连续区间上非马尔可夫动力学的严格数学处理与连续控制提供了基础。

Abstract: Connecting mathematical formalism in open quantum systems to its underlying physics necessitates the notion of a dilation, a way to bridge stochastic dynamics with deterministic Schrödinger evolution on a larger space. Although dilations are well known in the literature for states, channels, and quantum combs, there is a substantial gap when it comes to the fully general setting of non-Markovian dynamics on a continuous interval. In an accompanying work, we introduce a continuous process tensor (cPT) framework wherein these processes are represented by vectors in multi-species bosonic Fock spaces of $L^2$ functions. This accommodates all multi-time statistics of a quantum system on an interval. Here, we present a corresponding dilation theorem and show that all cPTs can be approximated arbitrarily well by an explicit closed description with system and environment coupled by a bounded Hamiltonian. As well as its physical appeal and demonstrating completeness of the framework, we show that such regular representations actually underpin the formalism, defining a dense subspace of all cPTs that is easy to handle. This leads to a continuum-suitable version of Choi duality, and permits the rigorous treatment of continuum controls.

</details>


### [171] [Fixed-Time Gaussian State Transfer via Collective Dissipation in a Fully Static Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35446)
*Austen Couvertier,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖集体耗散的完全静态高斯态传输方案，无需相干输运或含时控制，由共享环境锁定单一传输时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续变量通信通常假设需要相干输运或主动控制来实现信息传输，本文旨在检验：仅凭集体耗散是否足以支撑固定时间的高斯态传输。

Method: 通过集体与共享环境耦合的动力学，采用亮态-暗态分解，得到解析传输时间 t* = π/ga 及实数干涉因子，从而消除动力学相位累积；进一步分析系综中振幅不对称与相位级失谐的影响，并引入有限Ornstein-Uhlenbeck记忆（零阶O算子展开）考察非马尔可夫效应。

Result: 通道性能对振幅级不对称稳健（表现为均匀速率修饰）；相位级失谐破坏固定时间机制，导致动力学相位缠绕与复现行为。有限记忆仅带来速率与相位重整化，不引入新动力学路径；快记忆极限下环境关联被快速抑制，通道对环境失谐不敏感并恢复马尔可夫极限。

Conclusion: 集体耗散本身即可支撑固定时间的高斯态传输，挑战了连续变量通信必须依赖相干输运或主动控制的假设，并将耗散确立为信息传输的独立机制。

Abstract: We establish a fully static, dissipation-only primitive for Gaussian state transfer in which collective coupling to a shared environment fixes a single transfer time without coherent transport or time-dependent control. The dynamics close in a bright--dark decomposition, yielding an analytic transfer time $t^{*} = π/ g_{a}$ and a real interference factor that eliminates dynamical phase accumulation, so that all phase sensitivity arises solely from the Gaussian fidelity metric. This defines a fixed-time Gaussian communication channel in which dissipation mediates information transfer without active control. Ensemble analysis shows that channel performance remains robust to amplitude-level asymmetries, which act as uniform-rate dressing, whereas phase-level system detuning disrupts the fixed-time mechanism via dynamical phase winding and produces revival behavior. Incorporating finite Ornstein--Uhlenbeck memory via a zeroth-order O-operator expansion yields only rate and phase renormalization without introducing new dynamical pathways. In the fast-memory regime, environmental correlations are rapidly suppressed, rendering the channel insensitive to environmental detuning and restoring the Markovian limit. These results show that collective dissipation alone is sufficient to support fixed-time Gaussian state transfer, challenging the assumption that coherent transport or active control are required for continuous-variable communication.

</details>


### [172] [Efficient classical algorithm for estimating linear statistics of Boson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35447)
*Benoit Seron,Hugo Thomas,Eduardo Araujo,Alex Arkhipov,Changhun Oh,Leonardo Novo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色采样输出光子占据数线性函数（线性统计量）的粗粒化复杂度，给出了在加性误差内近似该类统计量的高效经典算法，统一了分子振动光谱模拟与探测器分箱粗粒化分布等结果，并据此经典攻破了一类单向函数，同时将非线性统计量的经典可模拟性归约为含单层相互作用线性光学电路的跃迁振幅计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样是展示量子计算优势的重要候选方案，但尚不清楚大规模玻色采样器能否实现有用的计算应用。其障碍在于输出空间随规模指数增长，且玻色采样分布具有反集中性质，因此需要粗粒化输出分布才能估计物理可观测量。作者希望厘清何种粗粒化统计量可被经典高效模拟，从而界定玻色采样的实际计算能力边界。

Method: 作者聚焦于基于输出光子占据数线性函数的特定粗粒化方式，定义为线性统计量。针对不同输入态（如Fock态与压缩态），构造了在加性误差内近似该线性统计量的高效经典算法。

Result: 所提算法能够在加性误差范围内高效经典近似玻色采样的线性统计量；由此将近期关于分子振动光谱的量子启发经典模拟算法、以及基于探测器分箱的粗粒化分布经典近似纳入统一框架；并证明可用该算法经典评估一个基于玻色采样提出的单向函数，而其他密码学应用方案则可逃逸该经典模拟技巧。

Conclusion: 线性统计量的玻色采样粗粒化在特定条件下可被经典高效模拟，削弱了部分相关密码学方案的安全性；非线性统计量的经典可模拟性仍为开放问题，且与含单层相互作用的线性光学电路跃迁振幅计算问题相关联。

Abstract: Boson Sampling is a prominent candidate for the demonstration of quantum computational advantage but it remains unclear whether a large-scale boson sampler can find useful computational applications. The challenge is that to use a boson sampler to estimate, for example, a physical observable, it is necessary to coarse grain the outcome distribution, owing to the exponential size of the outcome space and the anti-concentration properties of the Boson Sampling distribution. In this work, we analyse the complexity of a specific type of coarse-graining of Boson Sampling distributions based on linear functions of the output photon occupation numbers, which we refer to as linear statistics. We present an efficient classical algorithm for approximating linear statistics of boson samplers within additive error for different kinds of input states, such as Fock states and squeezed states. This allows us to unify in the same framework recent results on efficient quantum-inspired classical algorithms for simulating molecular vibronic spectra, or efficient classical approximations of coarse-grained distributions based on detector binning. Additionally, we show how our algorithm can be used to classically evaluate a proposed one-way function based on Boson Sampling, while other proposals for cryptographic applications escape our classical simulation techniques. We leave open the question of classical simulability of non-linear statistics and connect it to the problem of computing transition amplitudes of linear-optical circuits with one layer of interactions.

</details>


### [173] [Improving the Stability of the Hierarchical Equations of Motion for Open Quantum Systems with Strong Coupling to Structured Bosonic Baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35484)
*Salvatore Gatto,Samuel L. Rudge,Bokang Hou,Eran Rabani,Michael Thoss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用辅助Fock空间图像与非幺正相似变换，将HEOM中导致数值不稳定的不平衡"升阶项"转化为升降阶平衡组合，从而显著扩展了可模拟的系统-热库耦合强度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEOM（分层运动方程）是模拟开放量子系统动力学的强有力方法，但在玻色子环境中，层级不可避免的有限截断会引发根本性的数值不稳定性。作者希望深入分析并解决这一长期存在的数值问题。

Method: 作者首先采用HEOM生成元的辅助Fock空间图像（auxiliary Fock-space picture），识别出由非正规代数结构（non-normal algebraic structure）引起强瞬态放大的"不平衡升阶项"；随后施加一个非幺正相似变换（non-unitary similarity transformation），将这些不平衡项改写为升阶与降阶的平衡组合，从而抑制其数值影响。先在具有简单Brownian振子谱密度的自旋-玻色子模型上验证，再推广到结构化谱密度。

Result: 该方法在两种谱密度情形下均有效：变换后的HEOM方案能够模拟比原始方法大得多的系统-热库耦合强度，同时保持数值稳定。

Conclusion: 通过在辅助Fock空间中识别并平衡HEOM的非正规升阶项，非幺正相似变换可有效抑制截断引起的数值不稳定，显著拓展HEOM在强耦合玻色子环境中的适用参数范围。

Abstract: The hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) approach is one of the most powerful methods avail- able to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems. Although the HEOM approach has been shown to be stable and efficient in many parameter regimes and physical scenarios, it is also well known that the unavoidable finite truncation of the hierarchy can lead to fundamental numeri- cal instabilities for bosonic environments. In this work, we apply recently developed techniques to analyze these issues. We use an auxiliary Fock-space picture of the hierarchy to first identify the unbalanced hierarchy-raising terms responsible for the strong transient amplification associated with the non-normal algebraic structure of the HEOM generator. We then apply a non-unitary similarity transformation that converts these terms into balanced combinations of hierarchy-raising and hierarchy-lowering terms, thereby suppressing their numerical impact. We first demonstrate the efficacy of this approach via a spin-boson model with a simple Brownian oscillator spectral den- sity and subsequently extend the analysis to a structured spectral density, showing for both cases that the transformed HEOM approach allows simulations of much larger system-bath coupling than would otherwise be possible.

</details>


### [174] [Temporal trade-offs in high-dimensional entanglement: a comprehensive noise model for optimal time-bin QKD protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35488)
*Alexandra E. Bergmayr-Mann,Gláucia Murta,Marcus Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高维时间-bin纠缠在QKD中的维度-速率权衡问题，建立含损耗、暗计数、时间抖动等的通用噪声模型，在单个Franson干涉仪、渐近密钥率与共享纠缠度指标下给出高维编码优于qubit编码的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时间-bin纠缠易实现、鲁棒且可能提供比简单qubit协议更高的密钥率，但受限于时钟分辨率，时间编码面临"发送更多低维光子还是少量高维光子"的权衡。该问题依赖协议全上下文（产生率、损耗、暗计数、时间抖动等），难以回答，因此需要系统建模分析。

Method: 针对单个Franson干涉仪，以渐近密钥率和共享纠缠量作为性能指标，构建包含产生率、损耗、暗计数、时间抖动等所有相关参数的灵活噪声模型，并据此比较高维编码与qubit编码的性能。

Result: 分析表明确实存在高维编码仍胜过任何qubit版本的参数区域，典型情形是光子对到达率受限时，例如由损耗或泵浦功率受限所导致。

Conclusion: 高维时间-bin编码在配对到达率受限的条件下具有优势，并非在所有情形下都劣于qubit方案；所提噪声模型可用于指导高维QKD协议的参数选择。

Abstract: High-dimensional time-bin entanglement is known for having high potential for quantum key distribution (QKD) applications, being easily implementable, robust and may offer better keyrates than simple qubit protocols. The temporal encoding, combined with limited clock resolution, however, implies a trade-off: Is it better to send more low-dimensionally encoded photons or few high-dimensional ones? Answering this question presents a hard challenge as it depends on the entire context of the protocol, including the production rates, losses, dark counts, timing jitters and many more. We answer this question for single Franson based interferometers and asymptotic key rate and shared entanglement as main figures of merit. We present a flexible noise model incorporating all relevant parameters and find that there are indeed regions where high-dimensional encoding still outperforms any qubit based version when the pair arrival rate is limited, for instance through loss or limited pump power.

</details>


### [175] [Fourier-Geometric Circuit Design for Gate and Entanglement Placement in Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35489)
*Seungcheol Oh,Chaemoon Im,Daeyeun Kim,Soohyun Park,Vaneet Aggarwal,Mohsen Heidari,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于编码生成元伴随作用的PQC傅里叶系数可达性设计准则，将算子空间分解为按频率索引的二维不变平面，给出使目标傅里叶系数可达的门排布规则，并通过消融、PDEBench回归与Maxwell场建模验证其有效性及噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PQC输出为有限傅里叶级数，频率由数据编码门固定，而傅里叶系数取决于可训练门与纠缠门的排布方式。现有研究虽给出刻画门结构影响系数的度量，但未转化为明确的设计准则，无法说明应如何排布门才能使目标系数可达。

Method: 利用编码生成元的伴随作用（adjoint action），将算子空间分解为按频率索引的二维不变平面，证明每个傅里叶系数恰为有效态与可观测量在该频率平面上双线性投影之和。对Pauli编码，高频平面由混合多比特Pauli串张成，因此目标系数仅在局部旋转与纠缠层使有效态和可观测量同时获得该平面支撑时才可贡献输出。对Pauli读出与可交换二比特纠缠门，给出在给定相互作用图上使目标傅里叶系数可达的局部旋转与纠缠层放置规则。采用单层编码进行验证。

Result: 通过放置消融实验、PDEBench回归任务和物理信息Maxwell场建模验证了所提设计规则的有效性，并进一步证明其对中等程度的模拟门噪声与阻尼具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作将门结构对傅里叶系数的分析转化为显式的电路设计准则，为目标傅里叶系数可达性提供了可操作的排布规则，并在单层编码下验证了其有效性与抗噪性。

Abstract: The output of a parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) can be expressed as a finite Fourier series whose accessible frequencies are fixed by the data-encoding gates. While the encoder determines which frequencies can appear, the corresponding Fourier coefficients depend on how the trainable and entangling gates are arranged. Although existing studies provide metrics for characterizing how gate structure affects Fourier coefficients, they do not translate these analyses into an explicit design criterion specifying how gates should be arranged to make a target coefficient reachable. In this paper, we provide such a criterion. Using the adjoint action of the encoding generator, we decompose operator space into two-dimensional invariant planes indexed by frequency and show that each Fourier coefficient is exactly a sum of bilinear projections of the effective state and observable onto the planes at that frequency. Because, for Pauli encodings, high-frequency planes are spanned by mixed multi-qubit Pauli strings, a target coefficient can contribute to the output only when local rotations and entangling layers are arranged so that both the effective state and observable acquire support on one of its planes. For Pauli readouts and commuting two-qubit entanglers, this yields a circuit design rule that specifies, for a given interaction graph, a placement of local rotations and entangling layers that makes a target Fourier coefficient reachable. Using a single encoding layer, we validate the proposed design through a placement ablation, regression tasks from PDEBench and physics-informed Maxwell field modeling, and further demonstrate its robustness to moderate simulated gate noise and damping.

</details>


### [176] [Electron decoherence in cylindrical holes and circular apertures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35498)
*Cruz I. Velasco,F. Javier García de Abajo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了快电子在两种典型几何结构中的退相干定量理论，揭示了相干性损失对相位敏感电子显微术分辨率的根本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由电子相干性在电子离开环境中的可区分激发时会丧失，这一退相干过程从根本上限制了相位敏感电子显微术的分辨率与对比度，因此需定量刻画其机制。

Method: 结合电磁格林张量方法与涨落-耗散定理，并完整保留推迟效应，研究圆柱孔（金属中钻孔）与薄膜圆孔（理想导体）两种几何；圆孔采用柱面波模式展开求解。

Result: 圆柱孔得到闭合形式、按方位角分辨的表达式，分离了路径位置、温度与电导率依赖关系；高电导与高温极限下化为与温度线性、且与电导率和电子速度无关的普适式。圆孔能量损失概率低频按1/ω发散，而退相干概率保持有限并在孔径与电磁波长可比时达最大；孔径诱导退相干一般弱于圆柱孔，但大孔径高温下例外。

Conclusion: 对大孔而言，退相干可主导聚焦电子探针的空间展宽，必须纳入相干电子束仪器设计中。

Abstract: The coherence of free electrons sets fundamental limits on the resolution and contrast of phase-sensitive electron microscopy because coherence is lost whenever the electron leaves a distinguishing excitation in its environment. We develop a quantitative theory of fast-electron decoherence in two canonical geometries: a cylindrical hole drilled through a realistic metal and a circular aperture in a thin perfectly conducting film. By combining an electromagnetic Green-tensor formulation with the fluctuation--dissipation theorem while fully retaining retardation, we obtain the decoherence probability and elastic phase as functions of the electron trajectory, temperature, and material response. For the cylindrical hole, we obtain a closed-form, azimuthally resolved expression that separates the dependences on path position, temperature, and conductivity. In the high-conductivity and high-temperature limits, this result reduces to a universal expression that is linear in temperature and independent of both conductivity and electron velocity. For the circular aperture, which we solve using a cylindrical-wave modal expansion, the energy-loss probability diverges as $1/ω$ at low frequency, whereas the decoherence probability remains finite and reaches a maximum when the aperture radius is comparable to the relevant electromagnetic wavelength. Because the interaction is spatially localized, aperture-induced decoherence is generally weaker than that produced by a cylindrical hole, except for sufficiently large apertures at high temperature. Finally, we show that, for sufficiently large holes, decoherence can dominate the spatial broadening of a focused electron probe and must therefore be incorporated into the design of coherent electron-beam instruments.

</details>


### [177] [How fast can a parent estimate the value of their children? A quantum algorithm for stochastic games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35511)
*Marien Raat,Merlin Incerti-Medici,James R. Wootton,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Daniel Bultrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种求解随机二人博弈（expectiminimax树）的量子算法，通过结合去随机化多层蒙特卡洛估计与相干二分搜索，在查询复杂度上同时实现了对分支因子的二次加速和对精度的二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典博弈搜索树（expectiminimax树）同时包含对抗层与随机层嵌套结构。已有量子算法分别对对抗树的"分支因子二次加速"和单层期望的"精度二次加速"有结果，但二者嵌套时能否同时保持加速尚不明确，本文旨在解决这一嵌套场景下的量子查询复杂度问题。

Method: 对随机层（chance layers）使用去随机化的多层蒙特卡洛估计器，对对抗层使用相干二分搜索；通过引入均方根误差与一致误差保证之间的转换规则，并将其与量子均值估计进行嵌套组合，构造出适用于D=2m层的递归量子算法。算法框架可推广至一般条件：父节点值是其子节点值的Lipschitz函数，且随机顶点处的值对其子节点线性，但只有确定性步骤具有亚线性查询复杂度的量子子程序时加速才得以保持。

Result: 算法以均方根误差ε估计博弈值，查询叶子值次数为 O(K^D · deg^{D/4} · (N/ε) · D^{4D} · log(deg N/ε)^{4D})，其中D=2m，K为绝对常数。若将深度D视为常数，则复杂度为 Õ(deg^{m/2} ε^{-1})，相较经典算法的 Õ(deg^{m} ε^{-2})，同时实现了分支因子的二次加速与精度的二次加速。

Conclusion: 对抗树的二次分支因子加速与单层期望的二次精度加速在嵌套结构中可以同时保留；所提算法针对随机博弈给出，但适用于更一般的Lipschitz父节点与线性随机顶点条件，加速保持的前提是确定性步骤存在亚线性查询复杂度的量子子程序。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for stochastic $2$-player games. Their game trees are the expectiminimax trees of classical game search: $m$ adversarial layers alternate with $m$ chance layers, every node has $deg$ children, and the leaf values lie in an interval of length $N$. The algorithm estimates the value of the game to root mean square error $ε$ with \[ O\!\left( K^{D}\, deg^{\frac{D}{4}} \, \frac{N}ε \, D^{4D} \log\left( \frac{deg N}ε \right)^{4D} \right), \qquad D = 2m \] queries to the leaf values, where $K$ is an absolute constant. If we treat the depth $D$ as a constant as is sometimes done in the literature, the bound is $\widetilde O(deg^{m/2}ε^{-1})$ against the classical $\widetilde O(deg^{m}ε^{-2})$. So the quadratic speedup in the branching factor known for adversarial trees and the quadratic speedup in the accuracy known for a single expectation both survive when we nest one inside the other.
  We use a derandomised multilevel Monte Carlo estimator for chance layers and a coherent binary search for adversarial layers. The composition is done by introducing a conversion between root-mean-square and uniform error guarantees, and a composition to nest it with quantum mean estimation. We state the algorithm for stochastic games, but it is valid for the following conditions: the value of a parent is a Lipschitz function of the values of its children and the value at a chance vertex is linear in its children, however the speedup is preserved only if the deterministic step has a quantum subroutine with sublinear query complexity.

</details>


### [178] [Induced Coherence in Quantum and Classical Interferometry: Origin and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35523)
*Balakrishnan Viswanathan,Ravi Kamal Pandey,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Sibasish Ghosh,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子诱导相干干涉仪中验证了涉及相干性、可预测性与纠缠的三元关系（triality relation），并揭示路径不可区分性对干涉与纠缠的互补调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杨氏双缝干涉中的波粒二象性满足Englert-Greenberger-Yasin不等式，近期研究表明其服从一个包含相干性、可预测性与纠缠的普适三元关系。作者希望探究该三元关系能否在诱导相干干涉仪中持续成立，并进一步理解相干性与纠缠的来源及调控机制。

Method: 以量子诱导相干干涉仪为研究平台，显式推导并演示三元关系的持续性；对比经典构型以检验其是否退化为已知的二象性不等式；分析路径不可区分性（源的内在不可区分性）对干涉与纠缠的影响，并探讨相干性与纠缠的起源及调控手段。

Result: 三元关系在量子诱导相干干涉仪中依然成立；经典构型下该关系退化为经典的二象性不等式。路径不可区分性在本方案中对干涉和纠缠均起到互补的调控作用，研究同时阐明了经典与量子诱导相干干涉仪中相干性的本质特征。

Conclusion: 诱导相干干涉仪可作为检验波粒二象性三元关系的有效平台，路径不可区分性是统一刻画干涉与纠缠互补性的关键参量；经典与量子版本中相干性的性质存在本质差异。

Abstract: The wave-particle duality that manifests in the Young's double-slit interferometry satisfying the well known Englert-Greenberger-Yasin inequality has recently been shown to obey a universal triality relation involving coherence, predictability and rather surprisingly entanglement. In the present work, we explicitly demonstrate the persistence of the triality relation in the quantum induced coherence interferometer and the classical configuration recovers the well known duality inequality. Furthermore, we explore the origin of coherence and entanglement, in the induced coherence interferometer, and means to control them. We show that path indistinguishability (or intrinsic indistinguishability of the sources) in our setup plays a complementary role in influencing both interference and entanglement. We bring out manifestly the nature of coherence in both classical and quantum versions of induced coherence interferometers.

</details>


### [179] [Analysis of asymmetric errors in NISQ experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35535)
*Gil Kalai,Tomer Shoham,Carsten Voelkmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文延续对NISQ实验（尤其是Google 2019"量子霸权"实验）的分析，将此前针对对称读出误差的傅里叶+统计方法拓展到非对称读出误差，并结合实验与模拟数据检验门误差（振幅阻尼）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作分别发展了对称读出误差的统计工具（arXiv:2404.00935），但非对称读出误差尚未系统研究；同时Fefferman等（arXiv:2306.16659）提出振幅阻尼门误差会导致样本中零的比例增大，需用真实实验数据进行验证。

Method: 提出并应用统计工具分析非对称读出误差；以傅里叶方法结合统计工具为框架；对Google实验数据（量子比特数12≤n≤53）进行零/一比例分析，并补充n=12,14的IBM与Google模拟器仿真。

Result: 在Google实验数据的整个量子比特范围（12≤n≤53）内，零与一的比例仅反映所报告读出误差的非对称性，并未观察到Fefferman等预测的振幅阻尼门误差导致的零比例增大效应。

Conclusion: 非对称读出误差足以解释Google实验中零/一比例的观测结果；门误差（振幅阻尼）效应在该实验数据中不显著，需通过仿真进一步区分读出误差与门误差的影响。

Abstract: This paper continues our analysis of NISQ experiments and especially the Google 2019 ``quantum supremacy" experiment. In arXiv:2008.05177 we proposed statistical tools to analyze symmetric and asymmetric readout errors, and the symmetric case was extensively developed and studied in arXiv:2404.00935, where we used Fourier methods combined with statistical tools.
  Here we extend and complement our earlier study and analyze asymmetric readout errors for data from experiments and simulations.
  Our study was further motivated by a paper by Fefferman et al. arXiv:2306.16659, who showed that the effect of amplitude damping gate errors is to have larger fractions of zeroes in the samples produced by the quantum computer. We show that this effect of gate errors is not observed in the Google experimental data, where, for the entire range of the number of qubits, $12 \le n \le 53$, the fraction of zeroes and ones reflects only the asymmetry of reported readout errors, and report on simulations with $n=12,14$ using IBM's and Google's simulators.

</details>


### [180] [Sublinear Copies Suffice for Fidelity Estimation with Pauli Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35547)
*Jayadev Acharya,Abhilash Dharmavarapu,Yuhan Liu,Nengkun Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种估计已知目标态与未知实验制备态之间量子保真度的协议，在精度ε下仅需亚线性数量的Pauli基测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子保真度是衡量量子态制备质量的核心指标，但传统估计方法通常需要近乎完整的状态层析，测量次数随维度d剧烈增长，成本高昂。如何在保证精度ε的前提下大幅降低测量开销，是该领域的核心挑战。

Method: 设计一种估计量子保真度的测量协议，利用Pauli基测量（Pauli basis measurements）对已知目标态与未知实验态进行测量采样，通过控制测量次数与统计估计手段实现保真度精度ε的估计。

Result: 测量次数达到亚线性量级 o(d^0.9908/ε^2)，即在维度d较大时显著优于传统全层析所需的测量规模，且指数0.9908<1表明复杂度对维度d呈亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 该协议以亚线性测量复杂度实现了高精度量子保真度估计，为量子态验证与基准测试提供了测量效率更优的可行方案。

Abstract: We present a protocol that estimates the quantum fidelity, up to precision $\varepsilon$, between a known target state and unknown lab-prepared state with sublinear, $o(d^{0.9908}/\varepsilon^2)$, number of Pauli basis measurements.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum Query Complexity Beyond the Worst Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35580)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Yanlin Chen,Amin Shiraz Gilani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开创了量子平滑分析的系统研究，证明平滑量子查询复杂度可远小于经典查询复杂度，并给出对称布尔函数的近似紧刻画及若干字符串问题的量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典平滑分析能解释算法在超越最坏情况下的实际性能，但量子对应框架尚未被系统研究；作者希望借此揭示平滑输入下可能出现的更大量子加速。

Method: 在平滑分析框架下研究量子查询复杂度：构造总函数比较平滑量子与经典查询复杂度；对对称布尔函数刻画平滑随机与量子查询复杂度；研究模式匹配、编辑距离等字符串问题。核心技术为近似计数两个非重复字符串间碰撞数的近紧量子算法。

Result: (1) 存在某总函数，其平滑量子查询复杂度比经典查询复杂度指数级更小；(2) 给出对称布尔函数平滑随机与量子查询复杂度的近紧刻画，统一了最坏情形与平均情形结果；(3) 对模式匹配、编辑距离等字符串问题在多种参数区间实现多项式到超多项式的量子加速，并改进碰撞计数算法。

Conclusion: 平滑分析能够揭示比最坏情形更大的量子加速，为在更现实输入上实现量子优势提供了新路径。

Abstract: Smoothed analysis is a central framework in classical algorithms for explaining the performance of algorithms beyond the worst case, often explaining why algorithms perform well in practice. We initiate a systematic study of its quantum counterpart and show the following results. $(1)$ We show that there is a total function whose smoothed quantum query complexity is exponentially smaller than its classical query complexity. $(2)$ We give near-tight characterizations of smoothed randomized and quantum query complexities for symmetric Boolean functions, unifying the worst-case complexity results of [Beals et al, FOCS'98] and average-case complexity results of [Ambainis and de Wolf, STACS'00]. $(3)$ We study string problems such as pattern matching and edit distance and, in various regimes, give polynomial to superpolynomial quantum speedups. Our main technical ingredients include a near-tight quantum algorithm for $\varepsilon$-approximating the number of collisions between two non-repetitive strings, improving the result of Le Gall and Ng [QIC'22]. Together, our results show that smoothing can reveal larger quantum speedups than worst-case analysis suggests, opening a path towards quantum advantage on more realistic inputs.

</details>


### [182] [QC-Stark: A Multi-Task Benchmark Revealing Capability Dissociations in LLMs Evaluated on Quantum Computing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35581)
*Pranav Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QC-Stark是一个涵盖11项量子计算任务、用于评估大语言模型的基准，发现总体排名掩盖了任务间性能的显著差异，且部分任务排名与总体排名不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大语言模型的评估多依赖总体排名，可能掩盖模型在不同具体任务上的性能差异，缺乏针对量子计算领域多任务、可自动验证的系统性评测基准。

Method: 构建涵盖电路构建、调试、编译、纠错与模拟等11项量子计算任务的基准；开展2750次评估（10个模型 × 11任务 × 5难度级 × 5随机种子）；采用2参数项目反应理论（IRT）模型验证测量质量，并进行提示敏感性分析以检验排名稳健性；所有任务通过执行自动验证。

Result: 总体排名与任务级排名的Spearman相关系数在11项任务中有4项统计不显著，说明总体排名掩盖了任务间的显著差异；IRT模型验证了测量质量，提示敏感性分析表明排名在不同提示条件下保持稳健；所有任务均可自动验证，无需人工评估。

Conclusion: 总体排名不足以全面反映大语言模型在量子计算任务上的真实能力，需关注任务级细粒度评估；QC-Stark提供了可自动验证的多任务评测框架，代码与数据已公开于Huggingface。

Abstract: We introduce QC-Stark, a benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on 11 quantum computing (QC) tasks, spanning circuit construction, debugging, compilation, error correction, and simulation. Across 2,750 evaluations (10 models $\times$ 11 tasks x 5 difficulty levels x 5 seeds), we find that overall rankings mask substantial per-task variation. The Spearman correlation between overall and per-task rankings is statistically insignificant for 4 out of the 11 tasks included in this benchmark. A 2-parameter Item Response Theory (IRT) model validates measurement quality, and prompt sensitivity analysis confirms ranking robustness across prompt conditions. All tasks are auto-verifiable via execution, thus not requiring any manual evaluation. We make the code and data publicly available on Huggingface.

</details>


### [183] [Exponential lower bounds for low-degree strategies in position-based quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35592)
*Isabel M. Moreno-Cuadrado,David Perez-Garcia,Carlos Palazuelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非局域量子计算（NLQC）中实现对角±1幺正算符族的资源下界问题。在首轮策略对ε依赖度为低阶多项式的假设下，证明至少存在某个U_ε的实现需要指数级资源exp(Ω(n^{2δ}/(log n)^2))，显著推进了此前仅为次线性的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC是否需要对数维度以外指数级额外量子资源是一个核心开放问题，其指数下界对基于位置的量子密码安全性至关重要，并与通用量子模拟器最优性、全息量子引力等问题相关联。然而现有最优下界仅为n的次线性，亟需新的证明技术与更强的下界。

Method: 考虑由布尔超立方体ε∈{±1}^{n^2}索引的对角±1幺正族U_ε，满足U_ε|i⟩_A|j⟩_B=ε_{ij}|i⟩_A|j⟩_B。假设所有ε均可在NLQC中以常数精度实现，且首轮通信策略对ε的依赖是度为O(n^{1/4-δ})的多项式，进而利用特定Banach空间的几何性质（type常数）并结合取值于该空间的布尔函数随机估计进行证明。

Result: 在上述多项式依赖假设下，至少存在一个U_ε其NLQC实现所需资源规模为exp(Ω(n^{2δ}/(log n)^2))，即获得了指数级下界，突破了此前仅次线性的限制。

Conclusion: 通过Banach空间type常数与布尔函数随机估计相结合的新方法，论文在特定假设下首次给出了NLQC实现对角幺正族的指数级资源下界，为NLQC下界研究及位置基量子密码安全性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) consists on the implementation of a bipartite unitary $U_{AB}$ by two cooperating distant players by means of a two-round protocol with a single intermediate round of simultaneous communication. It is a major open question to know whether there exists a unitary $U_{AB}$ whose implementation in NLQC requires extra quantum systems with dimensions ${\rm exp}(n^{Ω(1)})$, where $n$ is the dimension of systems $A$ and $B$. This type of exponential lower bound is essential for security of position-based quantum cryptography, since the capabilities of the adversaries are precisely described by NLQC. It has also been connected to many other problems, such as the optimality of universal quantum simulators, or the expected properties of holographic quantum gravity. Currently the best lower bounds are only sublinear in $n$.
  In this paper we consider the family of diagonal ${\pm 1}$ valued unitaries $U_{\varepsilon}$, indexed by an element of the Boolean hypercube $\varepsilon \in \{\pm 1\}^{n^2}$, i.e. $U_{\varepsilon}|i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B =\varepsilon_{ij} |i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B$. We show that if $U_{\varepsilon}$ can be implemented in NLQC with constant accuracy for all $\varepsilon$, and the dependency on $\varepsilon$ in the first round of the associated strategy is a polynomial of degree $O(n^{\frac{1}{4}-δ})$, then the implementation of at least one $U_{\varepsilon}$ in NLQC requires resources scaling as $\exp\left(Ω\left(\frac{n^{2δ}}{(\log n)^2}\right)\right)$. The proof is based on geometric properties of particular Banach spaces, namely their type constants, together with random estimates of Boolean functions valued on them.

</details>


### [184] [Low-Weight Canonical Logical Bases from Pair-Partition Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35601)
*Koki Okada,Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过为二元循环置换矩阵对分割（CPM-PP）校验分配四元系数，构造并规范化逻辑代表元，从而为量子比特CSS码建立完整的规范逻辑基，并给出了多个具体二元码的参数与基权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子比特CSS码提供一个完整且规范的逻辑基构造方法，使逻辑空间可由规范配对的对偶代表元张成，并在特定条件下保持二元权重。

Method: 对二元循环置换矩阵对分割（CPM-PP）校验赋予四元系数；构造并规范化逻辑代表元，再展开为二元矩阵；当两个校验系统在不相交列集上具有可逆子矩阵时，利用单个配对多项式的逆对余因子代表元进行规范配对。

Result: 所得代表元对张成整个逻辑空间；当配对多项式为系数为1的循环移位单项式时，规范化保持代表元的二元权重；给出一般块维度和CPM大小的构造及示例，并报告了七个二元码的参数、校验秩和基权重。代表例参数为[[320,80,14]]、[[448,112,18]]、[[2048,512,24]]，三者X侧和Z侧的最大校验权重均为10，规范逻辑代表元在X侧和Z侧的二元权重分别为25、27、27。

Conclusion: 该构造为量子比特CSS码提供了完整的规范逻辑基，并在特定配对多项式条件下保持二元权重；所给代表例展示了在X侧和Z侧具有受控权重的较大码字参数。

Abstract: We construct complete canonical logical bases for qubit CSS codes by assigning quaternary coefficients to binary circulant permutation matrix pair-partition (CPM-PP) checks, constructing and normalizing logical representatives, and expanding the result into binary matrices. When the two check systems have invertible submatrices on disjoint column sets, cofactor representatives are canonically paired by the inverse of a single pairing polynomial. The resulting pairs span the entire logical space. When the pairing polynomial is a cyclic-shift monomial with coefficient one, normalization preserves the binary weights of the representatives. We state the construction for general block dimensions and CPM size, work through a corresponding example, and report the parameters, check ranks, and basis weights of seven binary codes. Representative examples have parameters $[[320,80,14]]$, $[[448,112,18]]$, and $[[2048,512,24]]$. All three have maximum check weight 10 on both the X and Z sides. Their canonical logical representatives have binary weights 25, 27, and 27, respectively, on both the X and Z sides.

</details>


### [185] [Succinct Arguments for QMA from Collapsing Hash Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35633)
*James Bartusek,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：仅假设塌陷哈希函数存在，即可构造QMA的简洁论证（succinct arguments），这是首个仅依赖非结构化"Minicrypt"假设的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的QMA简洁论证方案通常依赖结构化假设（如公钥加密），而这类假设强于Minicrypt假设。作者希望将基础降低到未知能推出公钥加密的非结构化假设（如塌陷哈希函数/单向函数），从而获得更弱、更可信的密码学前提。

Method: 核心技术贡献是"量子简洁爪态生成（claw-state generation）"协议：仅用经典通信，就能把少量量子关联放大为任意大量的爪态关联。相比[Zhang, STOC 2021]，该协议轮复杂度更低、可在标准模型下证明、且更简洁。由此得到基于单向函数的量子简洁盲委托（blind delegation）协议，再接入[Bartusek, Liu, Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026]的通信压缩编译器，最终构造出QMA的简洁论证。

Result: 在仅假设塌陷哈希函数（非结构化Minicrypt假设）下，成功构造出QMA的简洁论证方案；同时给出了改进的爪态生成协议，具备更优轮复杂度、标准模型证明和更简洁的结构，并得到基于单向函数的量子简洁盲量子计算委托协议。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将QMA简洁论证的安全性建立在仅Minicrypt级别的非结构化假设上，显著弱化了前提假设，并通过对爪态生成的改进和通信压缩编译器的组合，为量子密码学中的简洁证明提供了更简洁、更标准化的构造路径。

Abstract: We prove the existence of succinct arguments for QMA, assuming only the existence of collapsing hash functions. This is the first scheme that relies only on unstructured ``Minicrypt'' assumptions, which are not known to imply public-key encryption.
  Our main technical contribution is a quantum-succinct \emph{claw-state generation} protocol that allows us to bootstrap a small number of quantum correlations into an arbitrarily large number of claw-state correlations, using classical communication only. This improves upon the work of [Zhang, STOC 2021], having better round complexity, a proof in the standard model, and being overall much simpler. This yields a quantum-succinct blind delegation of quantum computation protocol from one-way functions, which we plug into the communication-compression compiler of [Bartusek, Liu, and Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026] to obtain succinct arguments for QMA.

</details>


### [186] [Learning sparse quantum states from single-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35648)
*Su-un Lee,Liang Jiang,Kunal Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏量子态的学习问题，证明只需单量子比特测量即可高效学习此类态，无需纠缠门，且与当前量子设备兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏量子态（密度矩阵在未知乘积基下至多s个非零元素）虽具有紧凑的经典描述，但可携带长程纠缠，导致无法仅凭局部约化密度矩阵重构。此前方法需使用大量纠缠门来提取信息，代价高且与现有设备不兼容。

Method: 提出仅使用单量子比特测量的学习算法。针对不同稀疏度情形设计策略：常数s时给出多项式样本与多项式经典计算复杂度算法；s随n多项式增长时，保证多项式样本复杂度；当未知基为已知固定有限单量子比特基集合（如泡利算符本征基）的乘积时，经典复杂度降至准多项式。

Result: 常数稀疏度s下，算法以多项式样本复杂度和多项式经典计算复杂度从单量子比特测量中学习稀疏态。s随n多项式增长时，仍保持多项式样本复杂度，经典计算复杂度一般无法保证高效，但在特定乘积基假设下为准多项式。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了在无纠缠门条件下对具有长程纠缠的稀疏态的高效学习，单量子比特测量要求使算法与当前量子设备兼容。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a sparse quantum state, an $n$-qubit quantum state whose density matrix has at most $s$ nonzero matrix entries in an unknown product basis. While such states admit compact classical descriptions, they can carry long-range entanglement that prevents reconstruction from local reduced density matrices alone. Therefore, previous learning approaches addressed such long-range-entangled states using many entangling gates to extract the necessary information. In this work, we show that sparse states can nevertheless be efficiently learned using only single-qubit measurements. Specifically, when the sparsity $s$ is constant, our algorithm can learn sparse states from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity and classical computational complexity. When $s$ grows polynomially with $n$, sparse states can still be learned from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity, although efficient classical computation is not guaranteed in general. In this regime, however, the classical computational complexity becomes quasipolynomial when the state is sparse in an unknown basis that is a product of a known fixed finite set of single-qubit bases (e.g., eigenbases of Pauli operators). These results establish efficient learning of sparse states with long-range entanglement without entangling gates, and the single-qubit measurement requirements make our algorithms compatible with current quantum devices.

</details>


### [187] [Optimal performance in a chaotic Floquet quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35655)
*Lakshmija N K,Uma Divakaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究由N个量子比特构成、均匀相互作用的量子电池：以周期性横向脉冲充电并在相互作用方向引入无序。无序使动力学逸出置换对称子空间，从而提升储能；大无序极限下可用随机矩阵理论精确计算储能，并伴随混沌充电相。无序增强时能量涨落趋于可忽略而储能最大，且存储阶段在退相干噪声下仍保持高可提取功（ergotropy≈储能），效率趋近1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找既能使量子电池储能最大化、又能使能量涨落最小化并保持高可提取性的充电机制；探究无序与周期性驱动在量子多体电池中的协同作用。

Method: 模型：N个量子比特均匀相互作用；初态为基态。充电方式：横向周期性kick驱动 + 相互作用方向无序。分析动力学是否保留置换对称性；在大无序极限下用随机矩阵理论（RMT）计算储能。储能阶段加入dephasing噪声，计算ergotropy（可提取功）与储能之比。

Result: 无序使系统离开置换对称子空间，储能高于无 disordered 情形；大无序极限下出现混沌充电相并可由RMT精确给出储能；能量涨落随无序增强而减小并在大无序极限趋于可忽略，同时储能最大；在dephasing噪声下，大无序时ergotropy接近储能，提取效率趋近1。

Conclusion: 无序+周期性驱动的组合可同时实现高储能、低涨落与近乎完全的功提取，是优化量子电池性能的有效途径；RMT为强无序充电相提供定量描述。

Abstract: We study a quantum battery consisting of $N$ qubits interacting uniformly with each other. Starting from its ground state, the battery is charged by introducing periodic kicks in the transverse direction along with adding disorder in the interaction direction. Although the battery Hamiltonian along with the initial state preserves the permutation symmetry, the dynamics in presence of disorder during the charging phase takes the system out of the permutation symmetric subspace, resulting to a larger amount of stored energy as compared to that without disorder. In the limit of large disorder, this interplay brings in a possibility of a chaotic charging phase when stored energy can be exactly calculated using random matrix theory. More importantly, we also find that the energy fluctuations decrease and become negligible in the limit of large disorder, which is also when the energy stored is maximum, a condition highly desirable for the optimum functioning of the battery. We complete the battery cycle by considering the storage phase in the presence of dephasing noise. We find that for larger disorder strengths, the ergotropy remains close to the stored energy, indicating nearly complete energy extractability with an efficiency approaching unity.

</details>


### [188] [The power of oracle access: Optimal sample and query complexity of the abelian state hidden subgroup problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35656)
*Yuhan Liu,Jose Carrasco,Jens Eisert,Armando Bellante*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限阿贝尔群上态隐藏子群问题（StateHSP）在采样模型与查询模型下的最优复杂度：查询模型可达 Θ(log(|G/H|)/√ε)（含匹配下界），采样模型则为 Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在寻找更多具有超多项式加速的量子算法时，问题的复杂度很大程度上取决于输入访问模型。作者希望研究这一现象在 StateHSP（HSP 的量子推广，目标是识别未知量子态的对称性）中的表现，并解决阿贝尔群情形下已有傅里叶采样算法所用副本数 O(log|G|/ε) 是否最优这一开放问题。

Method: 同时研究两种访问模型：（1）已有文献中的采样模型；（2）新提出的查询模型，该模型更强且操作上更自然，可访问态制备酉算及其逆。在查询模型下给出时间高效的量子算法；在采样模型下刻画信息论最优性，并允许任意集体测量。下界在更强的共轭查询与受控查询设置下依然成立。

Result: 查询模型中，算法使用 O(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 次正向与逆向查询，并证明匹配的 Ω(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 下界。采样模型中，Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε) 个副本既是充分的也是信息论必要的，即使允许任意集体测量。即 ε 上的二次改进确实来源于对制备电路的相干访问。应用上得到学习 stabilizer 群、判定非纠缠以及识别隐藏平移对称性的更快算法。

Conclusion: 相干访问制备电路（查询模型）相比仅能获得副本（采样模型）可在 ε 依赖上带来二次加速，且该分离是紧的；这一复杂度刻画同时为多个学习与对称性识别任务提供加速。

Abstract: In the quest to identify further quantum algorithms exhibiting superpolynomial speed-ups, a recurring theme is that the complexity of a problem is largely shaped by the input access model. Here, we study this phenomenon for the state hidden subgroup problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalization of the hidden subgroup problem in which the goal is to identify the symmetries of an unknown quantum state. For finite abelian groups, existing Fourier-sampling algorithms use $O(\log(|G|)/ε)$ copies of the state, but whether this scaling is optimal has remained open. We settle the complexity of the abelian StateHSP in both the previously studied sample model and a new query model, which is a stronger and operationally natural generalization that provides access to the state-preparation unitary and its inverse. In the query model, we give a time-efficient quantum algorithm using $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ forward and inverse queries, and prove a matching $Ω(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ lower bound which holds even in the stronger conjugate-query and controlled-query settings. By contrast, we show that in the sample model, $Θ(\log(|G/H|)/ε)$ copies are both sufficient and information-theoretically necessary, even if one allows for arbitrary collective measurements. Thus, the quadratic improvement in $ε$ genuinely arises from coherent access to the preparation circuit. As applications, we obtain faster algorithms for learning stabilizer groups, locating unentanglement, and identifying hidden translation symmetries.

</details>


### [189] [An Optimal Quantum Linear Systems Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35660)
*Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Aram W. Harrow,Robin Kothari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定量子线性系统问题(QLSP)的复杂度为Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))，并解决了Berry和Childs的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLSP此前上界为O(κd log(1/ε))与κ√d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}，下界为Ω(κ log(1/ε))与Ω(κ√d)，上下界不匹配；同时Berry和Childs的开放问题悬而未决。

Method: 针对d-稀疏N×N矩阵A(条件数κ)与可制备向量b的量子查询访问，改进量子算法与下界分析，并研究一般N×N酉算子的矩阵项查询实现。

Result: 证明QLSP复杂度为Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))；并证明任意N×N酉算子可用O(√N)次矩阵项查询以有界误差实现。

Conclusion: QLSP的查询复杂度被完全刻画，同时也确定了酉算子实现的查询复杂度量级。

Abstract: In the quantum linear systems problem (QLSP), we are given query access to a $d$-sparse $N\times N$ matrix $A$ with condition number $κ$, and the ability to prepare a quantum state proportional to a vector $\vec b$. The goal is to output an $ε$-approximation to the quantum state proportional to the solution $\vec x$ of $A\vec{x}=\vec{b}$. Following a long line of work, the best previously known quantum algorithms for the QLSP had query complexities $O(κd\log(1/ε))$ and $κ\sqrt d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}$, while the best known lower bounds were $Ω(κ\log(1/ε))$ and $Ω(κ\sqrt d)$. We improve these bounds and show that the complexity of the QLSP is $Θ(κ\sqrt d\log(1/ε))$. We also resolve an open problem of Berry and Childs by showing that any $N\times N$ unitary can be implemented with bounded error using $O(\sqrt N)$ queries to its matrix entries.

</details>


### [190] [Optimal Query Complexity for Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35668)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在已知能量阈值（位于谱隙中点）情形下，基态制备达到迹距离误差 $\varepsilon$ 的最优查询复杂度，给出期望与最坏情况两类算法，并证明匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带唯一基态哈密顿量的基态制备查询复杂度，在已知谱隙内能量阈值的前提下，刻画达到迹距离精度 $\varepsilon$ 所需的对块编码 $U_H$ 和态制备预言机 $U_I$ 的最优调用次数。

Method: 设 $U_H$ 为 $\alpha$-块编码，$U_I$ 制备态满足 $|\langle\psi_0|U_I|0\rangle|\ge\gamma$，阈值位于基态能量上方 $\Delta/2$、各激发态下方 $\Delta/2$。先用常数精度谱滤波器构造 purifier，再在振幅放大中顺序复合该 purifier 制备与基态有常数交叠的态。期望算法重复"制备 + 一次高精度谱滤波"直至成功；最坏情况算法使用精度递增的滤波器并限制总查询次数。

- 期望算法：$U_H$ 期望 $O((\alpha/\Delta)(\gamma^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ 次，$U_I$ 期望 $O(1/\gamma)$ 次。
- 最坏情况算法：$U_H$ 最坏 $O((\alpha/(\gamma\Delta))\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次，$U_I$ 最坏 $O(\gamma^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次。

Result: 证明下界与期望查询数匹配，对应最坏情况下界由 Somma 与 de Wolf [SdW26] 给出。在 $(N+1)$ 维系统上，当期望或最坏情况的 $U_H$ 调用数为 $o((\alpha/\Delta)\sqrt{N})$ 时，相应的 $U_I$ 界也是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了已知能量阈值下基态制备的最优查询复杂度，期望与最坏情况算法及其匹配下界共同刻画了 $U_H$ 与 $U_I$ 调用次数的最优权衡。

Abstract: We determine the optimal query complexity of ground-state preparation to trace-distance error $\varepsilon$ when an energy threshold in the spectral gap is known. Let $U_H$ be an $α$-block-encoding of a Hamiltonian with unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$, and suppose $|\langleψ_0|U_I|0\rangle|\geγ$ for a state-preparation oracle $U_I$. The threshold lies at least $Δ/2$ above the ground-state energy and at least $Δ/2$ below every excited-state energy. We give two algorithms that prepare a state within trace distance $\varepsilon$ of the ground state. One uses $O((α/Δ)(γ^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ calls to $U_H$ in expectation; the other uses $O((α/(γΔ))\log(1/\varepsilon))$ calls to $U_H$ in the worst case. We prove a lower bound matching the expected query count; the corresponding worst-case lower bound follows from Somma and de Wolf [SdW26]. The respective bounds on calls to $U_I$ are $O(1/γ)$ in expectation and $O(γ^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ in the worst case. On $(N+1)$-dimensional systems, these $U_I$ bounds are also optimal when the expected or worst-case count of $U_H$ calls, respectively, is $o((α/Δ)\sqrt N)$. Both algorithms use a constant-accuracy spectral filter to construct a purifier, which we then sequentially compose during amplitude amplification to prepare a state with constant overlap with the ground state. The expected-query algorithm repeats the preparation followed by one high-accuracy spectral filter until success. The worst-case algorithm uses filters of increasing accuracy and limits the total number of queries.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [OneVoice: An Intermediate Representation for Agentic Speech Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31673)
*Vipul Charugundla,Dancheng Liu,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 OneVoice，一种轻量级、JSON 原生的语音中间表示，用于为多智能体语音流水线提供共享语义结构，并在事件聚合与声学-语音学时间关联两类工作流中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体语音系统需要交换的不仅是文本，还包括说话人身份、时间信息、语音学信息与行为标注等；这些信号常以互不兼容的工具专用格式产生，导致智能体之间的交接（handoff）脆弱、易出错。

Method: 提出 OneVoice——一种轻量级、JSON 原生的中间表示，将异构语音证据组织为经过校验的会话记录（session records），包含稳定标识符、分层转写（layered transcripts）、显式时间关系与溯源信息。随后在两个互补的多智能体工作流（事件聚合、声学-语音学时间关联）中，跨三个语言模型进行评估。

Result: 与隐式、由智能体自定义的交接方式相比，OneVoice 显著提升了聚合可靠性以及时间关系的保持能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，为智能体通信引入显式的、语音专用的表示形式具有重要价值，OneVoice 可作为语音多智能体协作的共享语义基础。

Abstract: Agentic speech systems must exchange more than text, including speaker identity, timing, phonetic information, and behavioral annotations. Yet these signals are often produced in incompatible tool-specific formats, making agent-to-agent handoff fragile. We present OneVoice, a lightweight, JSON-native intermediate representation that provides a shared semantic structure for speech pipelines. OneVoice organizes heterogeneous speech evidence into validated session records with stable identifiers, layered transcripts, explicit timing relationships, and provenance information. We evaluate OneVoice in two complementary multi-agent workflows covering event aggregation and acoustic-phonetic temporal linking across three language models. Compared with implicit agent-defined handoffs, OneVoice substantially improves aggregation reliability and the preservation of temporal relationships, demonstrating the value of an explicit speech-specific representation for agent communication.

</details>


### [192] [DiffVQE2: An Efficient Low-delay Diffusion Model for Acoustic Echo and Noise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31703)
*Haljan Lugo,Ernst Seidel,Pejman Mowlaee,Ziyue Zhao,Tim Fingscheidt*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 首个低延迟（因果）基于扩散的联合声学回声与噪声控制模型 DiffVQE2 / DiffVQE2-S，在 ICASSP 2023 AEC Challenge 盲测集上多项客观指标及主观 MOS 超越 SOTA 的 DeepVQE / DeepVQE-S，且复杂度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免提设备与扬声器电话固有地受声学回声与背景噪声影响；端到端判别式神经网络虽为当前最佳方案，但现有基于扩散的 AEC 研究仅限于非因果、整句级处理，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出低延迟（因果）的扩散式联合 AEC 与噪声控制模型 DiffVQE2 / DiffVQE2-S，并对高效模型 DiffVQE2-S 引入有限前瞻（limited lookahead）。

Result: 在 ICASSP 2023 AEC Challenge 盲测集上，多项客观指标与主观 MOS 均优于 DeepVQE / DeepVQE-S，且模型复杂度更低；有限前瞻使 DiffVQE2-S 性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 据称是首个可流式、基于扩散的声学回声与噪声控制方法，性能超越最先进的判别式方法。

Abstract: Hands-free communication devices and speakerphones are inherently affected by acoustic echo and background noise. To mitigate these impairments, end-to-end discriminatively trained neural networks have emerged as the best-performing approach in research and deployment. While recent advancements in generative methods have provided remarkable results for various speech enhancement tasks, diffusion-based acoustic echo control (AEC) research is still restricted to non-causal, utterance-level processing, thereby not widely applicable in practice. With this work, we are the first to propose low-delay (i.e., causal) diffusion-based joint AEC and noise control models DiffVQE2 / DiffVQE2-S, excelling the so-far state of the art DeepVQE / DeepVQE-S models in multiple objective metrics, and, most importantly, in subjective MOS, respectively. In addition, our models are less complex. Furthermore, we show that a limited lookahead applied to the efficient DiffVQE2-S model allows for an even higher performance. These results have been obtained on the ICASSP 2023 AEC Challenge blind test set. We claim the first streaming-capable, diffusion-based acoustic echo and noise control that excels state-of-the-art discriminative approaches.

</details>


### [193] [RadarVox: Radar-Audio Multimodal Cocktail-Party Speech Separation with Speaker-Aware Cross-Modal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31708)
*Yanlin Xu,Yiwei Ru,Mupei Li,Yongji Liu,Jie Wang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出RadarVox，一个雷达-音频多模态基准，用于身份感知的鸡尾酒会语音分离，通过雷达先验与说话人匹配器解决置换歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯音频盲源分离存在置换歧义，分离出的语音流与物理说话人之间的对应关系不明确，难以满足具身语音交互中鸡尾酒会场景下语音分离与说话人归属的双重需求。

Method: 构建RadarVox基准，提供声学混合信号与来自扬声器和人类语音的源级雷达位移信号；利用FMCW雷达捕获喉部机械运动以获取说话人特定的空间-运动线索；通过门控融合将雷达先验注入DPRNN分离器，并学习说话人感知的跨模态匹配器，将无序语音流与雷达观测到的说话人关联。

Result: 在多说话人混合实验中，雷达线索提供了互补增益，二人和三人场景下SI-SDR分别达到9.75 dB和7.13 dB；相比纯音频方法，所提方法将有序SI-SDR提升超过13 dB，说话人归属准确率超过80%。

Conclusion: RadarVox验证了雷达-音频多模态融合在身份感知鸡尾酒会语音分离中的有效性，能够显著缓解置换歧义并提升说话人归属性能，为具身语音交互提供新思路。

Abstract: In embodied voice interaction, cocktail-party speech perception requires both speech separation and speaker attribution across machine-generated and human speech sources. However, conventional audio-only blind source separation remains permutation ambiguous, making the correspondence between separated streams and physical speakers unclear. This paper presents RadarVox, a radar-audio multimodal benchmark for identity-aware cocktail-party speech separation. RadarVox provides acoustic mixtures and source-level radar displacement signals from loudspeaker-emitted and human speech, enabling source-aware speaker assignment. FMCW radar captures laryngeal mechanical motion, providing speaker-specific spatial-motion cues unavailable to a single-channel microphone. We inject radar-derived priors into a DPRNN separator via gated fusion and learn a speaker-aware cross-modal matcher to associate unordered speech streams with radar-observed speakers. Experiments on multi-speaker mixtures show that radar cues provide complementary benefits, achieving scale-invariant signal-to-distortion ratio (SI-SDR) values of 9.75 dB and 7.13 dB in two- and three-speaker scenarios, respectively. More importantly, the proposed method improves ordered SI-SDR by more than 13 dB over audio-only methods while achieving over 80\% speaker assignment accuracy.

</details>


### [194] [Distributional Metrics for Evaluating Spoken Conversational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31719)
*Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Erica Cooper,Patrícia Schmidtová,Maike Züfle,Éva Székely,Nicholas Sanders,Ondřej Klejch*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出对话分布分数（CDS），以人类对话为参考，通过八个可解释特征（语速、音节节奏、轮次交互）及一个两特征语义基线来刻画对话行为分布，并在语料库层面进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话系统的评估困难且尚未解决；现有特定交互指标不足以完整刻画对话行为，需要一种分布层面的可解释评估方法作为补充。

Method: 引入CDS，比较对话行为分布，设置两种参考尺度：一是基于人类对话内部对话成功度的尺度，二是对比人类与合成对话的尺度；利用域外目标导向对话的听者判断，考察系统排序、对话间偏好及排序稳定性，并分析排序稳定所需的最少分钟数与对话数。

Result: 复合CDS复现了六项听者系统比较中的五项；单个特征与对话间听者偏好呈强相关；并给出了排序稳定所需的对话时长与对话数量。

Conclusion: 分布性比较可作为特定交互指标的补充，用于评估对话与对话模型，并体现其可解释价值。

Abstract: Evaluating conversational systems is a difficult and unresolved problem. We introduce the Conversational Distribution Score (CDS), which compares distributions of conversational behaviour using human conversations as a reference. CDS describes speech rate, syllabic rhythm, and turn interaction through eight interpretable features plus a separate two-feature semantic baseline. We compare conversations with two reference scales: one based on conversational success within human dialogue and another contrasting human and synthetic dialogue. Using listener judgments from out-of-domain goal--oriented dialogues, we examine system ranking, preferences between conversations, and ranking stability. Composite CDS recovers five of six listener system comparisons while individual features show strong correlation with listener preferences between conversations. We examine how many minutes and conversations are required before rankings stabilize. These findings support distributional comparisons as a complement to specific interactional metrics to evaluate conversations and conversational models while showing their interpretable value.

</details>


### [195] [Acoustic domain shift in spoken language identification from systematic domain generalization evaluation to real-world application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31759)
*Francois Derrida,Raphaël Duroselle,Thomas Courtat,Jean-François Bonastre*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个小规模语音数据集与评测协议，用于在受控条件下研究声学域偏移，并将语音模态引入 DomainBed 框架以评测口语语言识别模型，发现域内性能无法可靠预测跨域鲁棒性，且 MMD/DANN 等域不变算法并未优于 ERM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉中的域泛化（DG）已通过受控基准与多样化分布偏移得到系统研究，但口语语言识别领域的 DG 评测仍缺乏结构化。现有语音数据集虽可用于真实声学条件下的鲁棒性评测，但主要面向特定场景与大规模设计，并非用于系统构造与评测域偏移的通用工具。

Method: 工作包含三部分：(1) 提出一个小规模语音数据集与评测协议，支持受控的声学域偏移研究，可在多种声学域偏移下评测口语语言识别模型；(2) 将语音模态引入 DomainBed 域泛化框架，并在其上评测三种域泛化算法；(3) 在 MMS-LID-126 这一先进口语语言识别系统上验证结论的普适性，并开源代码。

Result: 实验表明：域内性能并非跨域鲁棒性的可靠预测指标；MMD、DANN 等显式域不变算法并未优于 ERM；上述发现在 MMS-LID-126 上得到进一步验证，说明结论具有一般性。

Conclusion: 该工作为口语语言识别的声学域偏移研究提供了小型可控数据集与评测协议，并统一到 DomainBed 框架中；结果质疑以域内性能推断鲁棒性的做法，也质疑域不变算法在语音 DG 中的优势，为后续受控评测与算法设计提供了基准与警示。

Abstract: Domain Generalization (DG) aims to develop models that remain robust to conditions unseen during training. While DG has been systematically studied in computer vision through controlled benchmarks and diverse distribution shifts, its evaluation in spoken language recognition remains less structured. Existing speech datasets provide valuable benchmarks for robustness to real-world acoustic conditions, but are primarily designed around specific scenarios and large scale rather than as general-purpose tools for systematically constructing and evaluating domain shifts. In this work, we introduce a smallscale speech dataset and evaluation protocol for controlled studies of acoustic domain shifts. It enables the evaluation of spoken language recognition models under a variety of acousitc domain shifts. We introduce the speech modality into the DomainBed Domain Generalization framework and evaluate three domain generalization algorithms. We show that in-domain performance is not a reliable predictor of cross-domain robustness and verify that explicit domain invariant algorithms such as MMD or DANN algorithms do not outperform ERM. We further validate the generality of these findings on MMS-LID-126, a state-of-the-art spoken language identification system. We release the code.

</details>


### [196] [Oracle Complementarity Is Not Realizable Complementarity in Frozen-Encoder Audio-Visual Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31764)
*Benjamin Hurt*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: Oracle 上限并非融合目标：它衡量的是分支分歧而非可恢复的融合预算，替换编码器溯源后 headroom 大幅变化，但可转化比例有限甚至为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态系统常以 oracle 上限（至少一个单模态分支正确的样本比例）与实际融合精度之差作为"可恢复 headroom"。作者质疑这一解读，旨在证明该上限不是融合的目标或预算，而只是分支分歧的度量。

Method: 使用冻结的自监督音频与视觉编码器加训练头。在 CREMA-D 上用两种音频编码器（一种微调血缘含 CREMA-D，一种干净）测量上限与实现增益，并在 EAV 上使用污染编码器。通过替换为自监督编码器、引入第二个干净编码器及对污染编码器做受控退化来考察 provenance 的影响；对比拼接融合与学习路由器。

Result: 替换微调编码器为自监督后 headroom 从 +0.055 增至 +0.186（三倍）。第二个干净编码器与受控退化落在同一曲线上，说明 provenance 通过改变音频分支精度移动 headroom。拼接最多实现约一半 headroom（转化比例 0.55），EAV 上转化比例与零无区别尽管 headroom 更大。学习路由器在各处均不如朴素拼接。

Conclusion: Oracle headroom 度量的是分支分歧，而非融合预算；不能将上限与融合精度之差简单视为可恢复空间。融合方法的收益受限且依赖数据集，简单拼接往往优于学习路由器。

Abstract: Complementarity analyses of multimodal systems commonly report an oracle ceiling (the fraction of examples on which at least one unimodal branch is correct) and interpret the gap between it and realized fusion accuracy as recoverable headroom. We show that this ceiling is not a fusion target. Using frozen self-supervised audio and visual encoders with a trained head, we measure the ceiling and realized gain on CREMA-D under two audio encoders (one whose fine-tuning lineage includes CREMA-D, one clean) and on EAV under the contaminated encoder. Replacing the fine-tuned encoder with a self-supervised one triples the headroom, from +0.055to +0.186. A second clean encoder and a controlled degradation of the contaminated one fall on the same curve, so provenance moves the headroom by changing audio-branch accuracy. Concatenation realizes at most about half of the headroom (converted fraction 0.55), and on EAV the converted fraction is indistinguishable from zero despite larger headroom. A learned router is beaten by plain concatenation everywhere. Oracle headroom measures branch disagreement, not a fusion budget.

</details>


### [197] [PRIME-ANC: Path-Ratio-Informed Modeling for Efficient Neural Filter Synthesis in Active Noise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31772)
*Yaokun Huang,Chunyang Xu,Haowen Hua,Sen Lin,Shichao Hu,Mengyao Zhu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: PRIME-ANC：共享神经合成器学习有界、路径相关的对数幅度修正，基于正则化路径比，生成FIR滤波器，实现跨路径主动噪声控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 听者声学条件变化导致原有ANC滤波器失配，需为新声学路径重新设计滤波器；传统方法依赖迭代优化，难以快速生成路径特定滤波器。

Method: 提出PRIME-ANC，共享神经合成器学习有界、路径相关的对数幅度修正，叠加于正则化路径比基线上；经最小相位重建与截断得到FIR滤波器；训练直接优化噪声控制性能；对留出听者条件可非迭代生成路径特定FIR，并可结合三次Gauss-Newton更新。

Result: 每个数据集十次随机训练/测试划分下，50 Hz–5 kHz平均留出1/3倍频程降噪：十路径数据集18.81 dB，公开耳机器数据库17.76 dB；原始耳机测量上较路径比基线提升7.89 dB；消融验证解析基线与路径相关修正均有贡献；三次Gauss-Newton更新达21.43 dB，接近直接加权最小二乘设计，且放大更低、控制输出RMS更小。

Conclusion: PRIME-ANC能有效学习路径相关修正并快速合成FIR滤波器，在多种数据集上取得显著ANC降噪，兼顾性能与低放大、低输出，具备非迭代部署潜力。

Abstract: Changes in listener acoustics require active noise control (ANC) filters to be redesigned for new acoustic paths. We introduce PRIME-ANC, a shared neural synthesizer that learns a bounded, path-dependent logmagnitude correction to a regularized path-ratio base. Minimum-phase reconstruction and truncation produce finite-impulse-response (FIR) filters; training optimizes their noise-control performance. Across ten random training/test splits per dataset, PRIME-ANC achieves average held-out one-third-octave reductions of 18.81 and 17.76 dB over 50 Hz-5 kHz on a ten-path dataset and a public earphone database, respectively. On original earphone measurements, it improves upon the path-ratio base by 7.89 dB. Ablation studies support the contributions of both the analytic base and the path-dependent correction. Given calibrated paths for a held-out listener condition, PRIME-ANC generates a path-specific FIR without iterative optimization. With three Gauss-Newton updates, it reaches 21.43 dB reduction, approaching direct weighted least-squares design while producing lower amplification and root-mean-square control output.

</details>


### [198] [Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Acoustic Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31785)
*Yonghyun Kim,Chaeyeon Han,Sancho Gatungay,Subhrajit Guhathakurta,Alexander Lerch*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trust-Filtered Distillation（TFD），在跨模态知识蒸馏中抑制教师对行人样本的监督，并将其对数形式解释为共享温度下的条件标签平滑。在 ASPED 数据集五折跨会话验证的十种蒸馏配置中，多数方法仅带来宏观准确率的轻微提升，主要表现为无行人准确率上升、行人召回率下降，且 TFD 未改善平均 PR-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯音频行人检测在城市场景感知中具有吸引力，但受限于声学线索微弱。跨模态知识蒸馏（视频教师监督音频学生，部署时仅用音频）是可行策略，但在严重类别不平衡与显著的视频—音频模态差距下，蒸馏究竟带来何种贡献尚不明确。

Method: 提出 Trust-Filtered Distillation（TFD），选择性地抑制教师对行人样本的监督；并从理论上将其 logit 形式解释为共享温度下的条件标签平滑。在 ASPED 数据集上采用五折跨会话验证，比较十种蒸馏配置。

Result: 多数方法在宏观准确率上有适度提升，但 PR-AUC 变化很小；主要效果是无行人准确率提高而行人召回率下降。在 logit 蒸馏中加入 TFD 会强化这一权衡，但未提升平均 PR-AUC。

Conclusion: 研究通过区分操作点偏移与判别能力增益，厘清了选择性跨模态监督在不平衡声学检测中的收益与局限。

Abstract: Audio-only pedestrian detection is attractive for urban sensing but limited by weak acoustic cues. An appealing strategy is cross-modal knowledge distillation, in which a video teacher supervises the audio student during training so that the deployed model runs on audio alone. Under this task's severe class imbalance and wide video-audio modality gap, however, what such distillation contributes is unclear. We introduce Trust-Filtered Distillation (TFD), which selectively suppresses teacher supervision on pedestrian samples, and interpret its logit formulation as conditional label smoothing under a shared temperature. Across ten distillation configurations under five-fold cross-session validation on ASPED, most methods yield modest gains in macro accuracy accompanied by small changes in PR-AUC. The main effect is higher no-pedestrian accuracy at the cost of lower pedestrian recall. Adding TFD to logit distillation strengthens this trade-off without improving mean PR-AUC. These findings clarify the benefits and limitations of selective cross-modal supervision by distinguishing operating-point shifts from discrimination gains in imbalanced acoustic detection.

</details>


### [199] [Optimal transport meets speech: a tutorial review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31787)
*Xugang Lu,Yu Tsao*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇综述，系统梳理最优传输（OT）的理论基础、深度学习可用的计算算法，并展示其在语音增强、语音识别、语种与说话人识别、音频伪造检测等跨域跨模态语音任务中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输能在分布支撑不重叠时度量分布差异并保持几何结构，已在生成建模、域适应和迁移学习中广泛应用，但在语音研究中仍相对欠缺探索。语音信号具有时间动态、说话人差异、噪声、混响以及音频/文本/视觉多模态异构表示等特点，容易产生分布不匹配，而OT为对齐与解释提供了天然框架，因此有必要推动其在语音处理中的更广泛应用。

Method: 综述性方法，包含三方面：(1) 通过直观物理解释回顾OT基础，并揭示其与现代生成模型的联系；(2) 介绍适合深度学习框架的计算算法；(3) 展示OT在跨域与跨模态语音任务中的应用。

Result: 论文梳理了OT的理论与算法，并在语音增强、自动语音识别、语种识别与说话人识别、音频伪造检测等任务中展示了具体应用，说明OT在应对分布变化方面的适用性。

Conclusion: OT在解决真实语音应用中的分布差异方面具有很强潜力，本工作旨在促进其在语音处理领域的更广泛采用。

Abstract: Optimal Transport (OT) provides a principled framework for comparing and transforming probability distributions while preserving geometric structure. Recently, OT has gained significant attention in machine learning due to its ability to measure discrepancies between distributions, even when their supports do not overlap, making it effective for tasks such as generative modeling, domain adaptation, and transfer learning. Despite its success in fields such as computer vision and natural language processing, OT remains relatively underexplored in speech research. Speech signals present unique challenges, including temporal dynamics, speaker variability, noise, reverberation, and heterogeneous multimodal representations involving audio, text, and visual information. These factors often lead to distribution mismatches, where OT offers a natural framework for alignment and interpretation. This work aims to promote broader adoption of OT in speech processing by: (1) reviewing OT foundations through intuitive physical interpretations and highlighting connections to modern generative models; (2) presenting computational algorithms suitable for deep learning frameworks; and (3) demonstrating OT applications in cross-domain and cross-modal speech tasks, including speech enhancement, automatic speech recognition, language and speaker recognition, and audio spoof detection. We highlight OT's strong potential for addressing distributional variations in real-world speech applications.

</details>


### [200] [MAESTRO: a Multimodal Auditory-attention Egocentric Speech-TRacking Open corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31898)
*K M Naimul Hassan,Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出MAESTRO语料库，这是首个同时记录EEG、眼动注视、瞳孔测量、自我中心视频与头部IMU数据的听觉注意解码（AAD）数据集，包含四说话人竞争与多信噪比条件，并证明多模态融合优于仅EEG方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在嘈杂环境中依赖注视、头部运动与视觉线索来注意目标说话人，但既有听觉注意解码（AAD）研究主要以EEG为唯一模态，缺乏真实多模态听音场景下的数据支撑，限制了对行为与生理信号协同作用的探索。

Method: 构建MAESTRO（Multimodal Auditory-attention Egocentric Speech-TRacking Open）语料库，同步采集EEG、眼动注视、瞳孔测量、自我中心视频与头部IMU数据；设置四个竞争说话人及背景噪声，覆盖多种信噪比（SNR）条件；建立四说话人注意解码基准，对比多模态信号融合与仅EEG方法。

Result: 在四说话人注意解码基准上，融合行为与生理信号的多模态方案相较仅EEG方法取得了更优的解码性能。

Conclusion: MAESTRO为真实听音场景下的多模态AAD提供了公开数据与基准，表明多模态融合可提升解码表现，为后续应用、分析与方法学研究开辟空间；数据集与代码均已公开。

Abstract: Humans rely on gaze, head movements, and visual cues to attend to speakers in noisy environments, yet auditory attention decoding (AAD) has been studied primarily using electroencephalography (EEG). We introduce the Multimodal Auditory-attention Egocentric Speech-TRacking Open (MAESTRO) corpus, the first AAD dataset to simultaneously record EEG, eye gaze, pupillometry, egocentric video, and head inertial measurement unit (IMU) data. MAESTRO includes four competing speakers and background noise across multiple signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions, enabling attention decoding under realistic listening scenarios. Through a four-speaker attention decoding benchmark, we show that combining behavioral and physiological signals improves decoding performance over EEG-only approaches, enabling future advances in multimodal auditory attention decoding.
