These findings open the door to new applications, analyses, and methodological advances in multimodal AAD. The complete dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aspire-osu/maestro-eeg-dataset . The official code repository is available at https://github.com/ASPIRE-OSU/MAESTRO .

</details>


### [201] [Improving Audiovisual Speech Recognition through Synthetic Visual Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31961)
*Pol Buitrago,Pol Gàlvez,Javier Hernando*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文探索用音频驱动的说话头管线生成合成视觉数据，以缓解视听语音识别（AVSR）数据稀缺问题；在西班牙语和加泰罗尼亚语上，合成样本增强使WER相对降低最高16.2%，且合成数据可单独作为低资源语言的AVSR训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVSR通过引入唇部运动的视觉信息提升语音识别性能，但其发展受限于带标注视听（AV）数据集的匮乏，尤其对缺乏AV数据的语言而言，数据稀缺成为扩展语言覆盖面的主要瓶颈。

Method: 利用音频驱动的说话头（talking-head）管线，从现有音频数据生成唇形同步的合成视觉内容；将合成视觉数据分别作为真实AV数据的增强策略与独立训练资源进行评估，并在西班牙语和加泰罗尼亚语上开展实验。

Result: 以合成样本增强真实AV数据，可使词错误率（WER）相对降低最高16.2%；在缺乏AV数据集的语言中，仅使用合成数据即可作为AVSR训练的基线。

Conclusion: 合成视觉数据可作为应对AVSR数据稀缺的可扩展方案，有助于扩大语言覆盖面，并为无AV数据集语言的AVSR建模提供可行路径。

Abstract: Audiovisual Speech Recognition (AVSR) is a multimodal approach to speech recognition that incorporates visual information from lip movements to enhance model performance. Despite its advantages, its development remains constrained by the limited availability of labeled audiovisual (AV) datasets. This work explores the use of synthetic visual data as a solution, using an audio-driven talking-head pipeline to generate lip-synchronized visual content from existing audio data. We evaluate the effectiveness of synthetic visual data both as an augmentation strategy and as a standalone training resource, applying our approach to Spanish and Catalan. Our results show that augmenting real AV data with synthetic samples yields relative Word Error Rate (WER) reductions of up to 16.2%, demonstrating the potential of this approach. Moreover, we demonstrate that synthetic data alone can serve as a baseline for AVSR training in languages lacking AV datasets. These findings provide evidence that synthetic visual data can serve as a scalable solution to AVSR data scarcity, enabling broader language coverage.

</details>


### [202] [Perceptually Motivated Alignment and Interpolation of Pitch-Aligned Time-Frequency Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31971)
*Shahan Nercessian,Jeff Sontag,Alejandro Koretzky*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于音高对齐时频表示的对齐与插值框架：将音调区间向量扩展为可逆算子并构建嵌入协和先验的特征提取器，进而用置换搜索进行对齐、用最优传输进行插值，并学习音阶结构的低维几何表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音高结构建模中，调性检测本身在音乐信息检索里存在歧义，直接依赖显式调性检测会导致对齐不可靠；同时音高分布之间的过渡需要符合和声结构，而音阶的音色与密度也缺乏可分解的低维几何刻画。

Method: 1) 以音调区间向量（tonal interval vector）为基础，将其重新表述为可逆算子，得到嵌入感知协和先验且音高对齐的特征提取器；2) 对齐：在感知加权距离下构造置换搜索，无需显式调性检测；3) 插值：采用最优传输生成音高分布间具有音乐意义的过渡，并在四度/五度圈上提出循环形式以符合和声结构；4) 学习音阶结构的低维几何表示，以分解音阶色彩与密度。

Result: 实验结果表明所提方法在有效性与音乐性方面均表现良好；对齐在无显式调性检测下仍具鲁棒性，插值生成的过渡具有音乐意义，低维几何表示可分离音阶色彩与密度。

Conclusion: 本文贡献共同构成一种原则性的、纯信号处理方法，用于建模音频与符号音乐信号中的音高结构。

Abstract: We propose a framework for the alignment and interpolation of pitch-aligned time-frequency representations. Building on the tonal interval vector, we introduce a series of extensions that reformulate it as an invertible operator, culminating in a new feature extractor that embeds perceptual consonance priors within a pitch-aligned representation. Accordingly, we develop methods for aligning and interpolating between musical structures. For alignment, we cast the problem as a permutation search under perceptually weighted distances, enabling robust matching without explicit key detection, which is inherently ambiguous in music information retrieval. For interpolation, we employ optimal transport to generate musically meaningful transitions between pitch distributions, proposing a circular formulation over the Circle of Fourths/Fifths to respect harmonic structure. Finally, we learn a low-dimensional geometric representation of scale structure aimed to factorize scale color and density. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and musicality of the proposed methods. Together, our contributions provide a principled, purely signal processing approach to modeling pitch structure in audio and symbolic music signals.

</details>


### [203] [Audio Preprocessing Effects on Stuttering Detection: A Class-Specific Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32285)
*Anisha Pattanayak,Hanie Kang,Huang-Cheng Chou,Sudarsana Reddy Kadiri*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文研究音频预处理链路对SEP-28k口吃检测性能的影响，发现阈值调整可恢复大部分F1损失，但ROC-AUC的下降无法通过阈值调整恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频预处理（去噪、响度归一化、Opus编码、VAD）会影响的系统性能，但这一链路对口吃检测的具体影响尚不明确，本文旨在量化该影响并探讨恢复策略。

Method: 在SEP-28k数据集上模拟由去噪、响度归一化、Opus编码和VAD组成的处理链，使用冻结的WavLM Base+特征，并通过episode级bootstrap重采样报告逐点置信区间。

Result: 阈值为0.5时，处理链将block F1从0.638降至0.465，其余四类下降较小；五类的ROC-AUC均下降。Block类F1和ROC-AUC损失最大，声音重复类平均精度损失最大。在处理后验证音频上调阈值可将block F1提升至0.630；在处理后音频上重训练加阈值调整得到0.628。阈值调整可解释大部分block F1恢复；ROC-AUC不受阈值调整影响，重训练仅将其从0.620提升至0.633，而干净音频上为0.724。

Conclusion: 阈值调整能恢复大部分F1损失，但无法弥补ROC-AUC的下降，重训练对ROC-AUC的改善有限，表明预处理对特征判别能力的损害是实质性的。

Abstract: Audio preprocessing can affect how well a system detects stuttering. We study a simulated chain of denoising,loudness normalisation, Opus coding, and voice activity detection on SEP-28k. We use frozen WavLM Base+ features and report pointwise confidence intervals from episode-level bootstrap resampling. At a fixed threshold of 0.5, the chain reduces block F1 from 0.638 to 0.465, with smaller decreases for the other four classes. ROC-AUC decreases for all five classes. Blocks show the largest F1 and ROC-AUC losses, while sound repetitions show the largest average precision loss. Tuning the threshold on processed validation audio raises block F1 to 0.630. Retraining on processed audio with threshold tuning gives 0.628. Threshold adjustment therefore accounts for most of the observed block F1 recovery. It does not change ROC-AUC, which retraining raises only from 0.620 to 0.633, compared with 0.724 on clean audio.

</details>


### [204] [Toward Human-Aligned Judgement of Speech Emotion Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32504)
*Yun-Shao Tsai,Yi-Cheng Lin,Chih-Kai Yang,Ho-Jung Cheng,Tsun-Yi Chang,Sheng-Wei Wu,Yi-Shan Chen,Hsiang-Chun Chang,Liang-Chieh Lee,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出SES-Bench基准与SES-Judge模型，用于评估生成语音与参考语音之间的情感相似度，以自动指标替代高成本的人工听测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表达性语音生成中，情感保持程度需通过生成语音与参考语音的情感相似度来衡量。人工听测虽能评估该相似性，但成本高昂，因此需要与人类判断对齐的自动评估指标；而这类指标的开发与评测又缺乏相应数据支持。

Method: 构建SES-Bench语音情感相似度基准：基于人类对两条候选语音与同一参考语音的比较，标注哪条候选在情感上更接近参考以及偏好强度。利用这些标注数据训练SES-Judge，对两条语音之间的情感相似度进行打分。

Result: 在偏好准确率以及与人类评分（同时包含偏好方向与强度）的相关性上，SES-Judge显著优于嵌入余弦相似度方法和基于提示的大型音频语言模型。

Conclusion: SES-Bench为情感相似度自动度量的研发与评估提供了数据基础，SES-Judge证明基于人类偏好标注训练的模型可作为更贴近人类判断的自动情感相似度度量。

Abstract: Evaluating emotion preservation in expressive speech generation involves assessing how closely generated speech matches a reference in emotion. Human listening tests assess this similarity, but their cost motivates automatic measures aligned with human judgments. To support the development and evaluation of such measures, we introduce SES-Bench, a speech emotion similarity benchmark built from human comparisons of two candidate utterances against a shared reference. These comparisons record which candidate listeners find emotionally closer to the reference and the strength of their preference. Using these annotations, we train SES-Judge to score emotion similarity between two utterances. SES-Judge significantly outperforms embedding cosine similarity and prompted large audio-language models in preference accuracy and correlation with human ratings that capture both preference direction and strength.

</details>


### [205] [VoxMem: Benchmarking Multimodal Memory in Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32607)
*Yang Xiao,Vidhyasaharan Sethu,Eun-Jung Holden,Ting Dang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出VoxMem，一个面向口语对话记忆的全新评测基准，联合刻画"音频证据类型"与"记忆操作"两个维度，包含3,196个实例、34,743个会话（177小时），覆盖8K–64K上下文预算。评测15个LALM显示无一模型在32K处超过40%，且对"说了什么"的记忆显著优于"谁说的、怎么说、有什么声音"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语对话记忆评测存在三重不足：(1)主要关注词汇/文本内容，忽略了仅存在于音频信号中的说话人身份、副语言线索与环境声音等信息；(2)记忆操作有限且临时拼凑，未覆盖检索单一事实、跨轮整合证据、追踪演化状态等多样操作；(3)将记忆视为单会话问题，未考虑真实交互跨会话、跨片段累积的特性。因此需要一种有原则的记忆评测框架。

Method: 作者提出一个双轴分类法（taxonomy）：一轴为需保留的声学证据类型（语音语义、说话人身份、副语言线索、环境声音），另一轴为施加于其上的记忆操作（信息抽取、多会话推理、时间追踪、拒答）。基于该分类法构建VoxMem基准。

Result: VoxMem包含3,196个评测实例，基于34,743个口语会话（共177小时），呈多会话历史结构，并按8K至64K token的上下文预算分层。评测15个LALM：在32K预算下无一模型准确率超过40%；模型对"说了什么"的保留远好于"谁说的、怎么说、有什么声音"；该差距在复杂操作下扩大、随历史长度增长，并表现为不同证据类型间质性不同的失败模式。

Conclusion: VoxMem为全面衡量并推动口语对话记忆研究提供了基础基准，揭示当前LALM在音频证据保留与复杂记忆操作上的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Spoken conversational systems must recover information from prior interactions (i.e., memory), yet relevant information in speech extends beyond what was said to who said it, how it was spoken, and what was audible, information that exists only in the audio signal and cannot be recovered from a transcript. Beyond what to remember, memory also demands diverse operations: retrieving a single fact, integrating evidence across turns, tracking an evolving state. Real interactions further unfold across sessions, meaning information accumulates across distinct episodes rather than a single continuous recording. Existing benchmarks fall short on all three dimensions: they focus primarily on lexical content, adopt limited and ad hoc memory operations, and treat memory as a single-session problem. We argue that principled memory evaluation requires jointly characterizing the acoustic evidence to be retained and the operations applied to it, and introduce a taxonomy along these two axes. Building on this taxonomy, we present VoxMem: 3,196 evaluation instances over 34,743 spoken sessions (177 hours) crossing four acoustic evidence types (speech semantics, speaker identity, paralinguistic cues, environmental sound) with four memory operations (information extraction, multi-session reasoning, temporal tracking, and answer refusal), grounded in multi-session histories and stratified across context budgets from 8K to 64K tokens. Evaluating 15 LALMs, no model exceeds 40% at 32K. Models retain what was said far better than who said it, how, or what was audible, a gap that widens for complex operations, grows with history length, and manifests as qualitatively distinct failure modes across evidence types. VoxMem aims to provide a foundation to measure and drive progress on the full scope of spoken conversational memory.

</details>


### [206] [WhisperVC-AV: Audio-Visual Content Restoration for Noise-Robust Whisper-to-Normal Voice Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32843)
*Ziyue Yin,Dong Liu,Ming Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Environmental noise obscures the content cues needed for whisper-to-normal conversion. We propose WhisperVC-AV, which restores acoustic content features while leaving the original WhisperVC conversion module unchanged. Its context-guided restoration module combines temporal acoustic context with synchronized lip features through attention and gated residual correction. Experiments on AISHELL6-Whisper show lower character error rates (CERs) than WhisperVC across three ASR systems, on clean speech and under all six signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions with MUSAN noise. The largest gains occur at 0 dB SNR, where Qwen3-ASR CER falls from 36.29% to 28.88%. WhisperVC-AV also improves predicted speech quality while maintaining speaker similarity. The CER gains extend to unseen background noise without retraining, while visual controls support the use of utterance-specific lip cues. Audio examples are available on our demo page.

</details>


### [207] [Acoustic Progress Propagation for Long-Horizon Speculative Decoding in ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33245)
*Yuanyuan Jia,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出进度感知的投机解码草稿模型（ProgDraft），通过递归传播声学进度状态并反馈至音频交叉注意力以指导token生成，缓解对齐感知草稿器接受长度饱和问题，在五个ASR测试集上实现无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码可加速自回归ASR，但对齐感知草稿器（alignment-aware drafter）的接受长度随草稿视野（draft horizon）增大而趋于饱和，限制了进一步的加速收益。

Method: 提出进度感知投机草稿器：在草稿步间递归传播声学进度状态，并将其反馈到音频交叉注意力中引导token生成；同时对草稿器与进度预测器在可变草稿视野下进行联合训练。

Result: 在五个ASR测试集上，基于Qwen3-ASR-0.6B与Qwen3-ASR-1.7B，相比纯目标自回归解码分别取得1.657x与1.227x的宏平均端到端无损加速；相比AnchorDraft，宏平均加速分别提升34.3%与9.0%；视野扫描显示接受长度在基线饱和后仍持续增长。

Conclusion: 进度感知机制有效突破了草稿器接受长度饱和的瓶颈，在不损失识别精度前提下显著提升ASR推理速度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive automatic speech recognition (ASR), but the acceptance length of alignment-aware drafters can saturate as the draft horizon increases. We propose a progress-aware speculative drafter that recurrently propagates an acoustic progress state across draft steps and feeds it back into audio cross-attention to guide token generation. We jointly train the drafter and progress predictor over variable draft horizons. On five ASR test sets, our method achieves lossless, macro-averaged end-to-end speedups of 1.657x and 1.227x over target-only autoregressive decoding with Qwen3-ASR-0.6B and Qwen3-ASR-1.7B, respectively. Relative to AnchorDraft, our method improves the macro-averaged speedup by 34.3% and 9.0%, respectively. Horizon sweeps show continued growth in acceptance length beyond the baselines' saturation. Code is available at https://github.com/yuanyuanjia71-spec/ProgDraft.

</details>


### [208] [From Script to Drama: An Agentic Framework for Controllable Multi-Speaker Dialogue TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33362)
*Kangxiang Xia,Xinfa Zhu,HangRui Hu,Kexin Huang,Wenjie Tian,Ziyue Jiang,Bingshen Mu,Jingbin Hu,Ting He,Lei Xie,Jin Xu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出可控多说话人多轮对话TTS框架，将合成建模为批评驱动的迭代精炼，语音骨干ControlEdit-TTS统一指令跟随合成与自然语言引导的属性编辑，并结合话语级与场景级分层批评进行路由修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多说话人对话TTS需同时满足自然语音生成、说话人身份一致、跨轮次衔接连贯，以及情感、语速、响度等表达属性的细粒度控制。单次生成难以在长对话中稳定满足这些要求。

Method: 框架将合成表述为批评驱动的迭代精炼流程：语音骨干ControlEdit-TTS统一指令跟随合成与自然语言引导的属性编辑，可在不全量重生成的前提下纠正表达错误；框架进一步执行话语级与场景级分层批评，将检测到的问题路由至编辑、重合成或时序调整。

Result: 在中英双语对话基准上，话语级指令跟随能力提升；对话级偏好优于直接对话模型与智能体基线；相比仅重生成方案精炼更有效，同时保持说话人身份。消融实验验证了场景级批评与基于编辑的修正的收益。

Conclusion: 批评驱动的迭代精炼与统一"合成-编辑"骨干可有效提升长对话多说话人TTS的指令跟随与对话级质量，分层批评与编辑式修正是关键增益来源。

Abstract: Multi-speaker dialogue TTS requires natural speech generation, consistent speaker identity, coherent cross-turn transitions, and fine-grained control of expressive attributes such as emotion, speaking rate, and loudness. These requirements are difficult to satisfy reliably with one-shot generation, especially in long-form dialogue. We propose a controllable multi-speaker dialogue TTS framework that formulates synthesis as critique-driven iterative refinement. Its speech backbone, ControlEdit-TTS, unifies instruction-following synthesis and natural-language-guided attribute editing, enabling correction of expressive errors without full regeneration. The framework further performs hierarchical utterance-level and scene-level critique, routing detected issues to editing, resynthesis, or timing adjustment. Experiments on a bilingual Chinese--English dialogue benchmark show improved utterance-level instruction following, better dialogue-level preference than direct dialogue models and agentic baselines, and more effective refinement than regeneration-only alternatives while preserving speaker identity. Ablations further confirm the benefits of scene-level critique and edit-based correction.

</details>


### [209] [An Efficient Parametric Codec for Low-Bitrate First-Order Ambisonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33554)
*Wei-Ting Lai,Amy Bastine,Lachlan Birnie,Thushara D. Abhayapala,Prasanga N. Samarasinghe*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量参数化FOA编解码器，用3-D指向性向量的跨频带残差矢量量化(RVQ)替代独立量化DOA与扩散度，实现恒定码率、数分钟训练，性能优于低码率感知编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式远程会议与生成式空间音频快速发展，使一阶Ambisonics(FOA)的低码率高效编码需求日益突出；已有低码率方案在重建质量、元数据码率随频带数增长、以及训练成本方面存在不足。

Method: 保留标准DirAC分析与合成框架，重新设计空间元数据量化：将到达方向(DOA)与扩散度合并为3-D指向性向量，并跨频带通过残差矢量量化(RVQ)联合量化；码本采用逐级k-means优化，无需反向传播，可在数分钟内完成，输出恒定码率(CBR)表示，使元数据码率与频带数解耦。

Result: 在FOA重建上优于低码率感知编码器；在下游声事件定位与检测(SELD)任务上与神经编解码器相当；与不同外部单声道编解码器搭配时性能保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该方法训练轻量、性能强且为CBR设计，可作为未来FOA编解码研究易于复现的基线。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid growth of immersive teleconferencing and generative spatial audio, efficient low-bitrate coding of first-order Ambisonics (FOA) has become increasingly important. In this work, we develop a lightweight parametric FOA codec that retains the standard Directional Audio Coding analysis and synthesis while redesigning the spatial metadata quantization scheme. Rather than quantizing direction-of-arrival (DOA) and diffuseness independently, we combine them into a 3-D directivity vector and jointly quantize these vectors across frequency bands via residual vector quantization (RVQ). The RVQ codebooks are optimized within minutes via stage-wise k-means without backpropagation, yielding a constant-bitrate (CBR) representation that decouples metadata rate from the number of frequency bands. Evaluations show that our approach outperforms low-bitrate perceptual codecs in FOA reconstruction, remains competitive with neural codecs on the downstream Sound Event Localization and Detection task, and maintains robust performance when paired with different external monaural codecs. Given its lightweight training, strong performance, and CBR design, we consider the proposed method to be a favorable and reproducible baseline for future FOA codec research.

</details>


### [210] [Pruned CTC for Memory-Efficient Large-Vocabulary ASR Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33645)
*Yifan Yang,Xiaoyu Yang,Zengrui Jin,Xian Shi,Yuxuan Wang,Yu Xi,Ziyang Ma,Qi Chen,Ruiyang Xu,Hui Wang,Dongchao Yang,Jin Xu,Xie Chen*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Pruned CTC，通过将 CTC 对齐计算限制在批次内目标 token 与 blank 构成的小型子集上，同时保留全词表归一化，实现与标准 CTC 在损失和梯度上的精确等价；并在此基础上构建 LLM-CTC，用于支持原生词表的非自回归与有界历史流式 ASR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CTC 实现需要将帧级与词表维度上的激活完整物化到内存中，当使用原生大词表（如 180K）训练 LLM 时，内存开销极大，严重限制了 CTC 在大词表 LLM 语音识别中的可扩展性。作者观察到：每个有效的 CTC 对齐仅使用目标 token 和 blank，且一个批次内这些 token 的并集通常只占全词表的很小一部分，因而存在大幅缩减计算规模的空间。

Method: 核心方法是 Pruned CTC：将对齐计算限制在批次内目标 token 与 blank 构成的子集上，但保留全词表归一化，并证明该词表缩减在损失与梯度上与全词表 CTC 完全等价，从而使 head 与损失激活内存不再随词表大小线性增长；进一步引入有限 beam 的对齐剪枝。基于此构建 LLM-CTC，将预训练 LLM 适配为非自回归 ASR，保留因果注意力与原生词表，并扩展到有界历史流式场景，避免块级语音-文本对齐。

Result: 在 Zipformer-M 编码器与 180K 词表设置下，Pruned CTC 将全步内存降低 5.1 倍，仅增加 17% 的步时开销；在三个语料上达到与标准 CTC 相当的准确率。在 GigaSpeech 上，六个 Qwen3 模型规模（0.6B 至 32B）中，LLM-CTC 相对 LLM-CE 的 WER 差距在 7% 以内，识别速度提升 7 至 10 倍。微调 Qwen3-ASR 用于有界历史流式时，LLM-CTC 在测试集上与匹配的离线模型 WER 差距在 3% 以内。

Conclusion: Pruned CTC 作为一种可扩展的序列目标，在大词表原生 LLM ASR 中兼具内存效率与精度保持能力，适用于离线与流式两种场景，为 LLM 语音识别提供了可行的训练方案。

Abstract: Connectionist temporal classification (CTC) naturally supports offline and streaming speech recognition with utterance-level supervision, but conventional implementations materialize frame-by-vocabulary activations in memory, making CTC training with native LLM vocabularies prohibitively memory-intensive. A key observation is that every valid CTC alignment uses only target tokens and blank, and their union across a batch typically forms a small subset of the full vocabulary. We introduce Pruned CTC, which restricts alignment computation to this subset while retaining full-vocabulary normalization. We prove that this vocabulary reduction is exactly equivalent to full-vocabulary CTC in loss and gradients. Head-and-loss activation memory no longer scales linearly with vocabulary size. We further apply finite-beam alignment pruning. Building on Pruned CTC, we develop LLM-CTC, which adapts pretrained LLMs for non-autoregressive ASR while retaining causal attention and native vocabularies, and extend it to bounded-history streaming, avoiding chunk-level speech--text alignments. Experiments show that, with Zipformer-M encoder and 180K vocabulary, Pruned CTC reduces full-step memory by 5.1$\times$ with only 17% step-time overhead. Across three corpora, it matches standard CTC accuracy. On GigaSpeech, across six Qwen3 model sizes from 0.6B to 32B, LLM-CTC remains within 7% relative WER of LLM-CE with 7 to 10$\times$ faster recognition; when fine-tuning Qwen3-ASR for bounded-history streaming, LLM-CTC remains within 3% relative WER of matched offline models on the test set. Together, these results establish Pruned CTC as a scalable sequence objective for native-vocabulary LLM ASR across offline and streaming settings.

</details>


### [211] [DGS-MLDG: Domain Gradient Surgery Guided Meta-Learning for Domain Generalization in Speech Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33706)
*Siqing Qin,Kong Aik Lee,Youzhi Tu,Eng Siong Chng,Man-Wai Mak*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 论文针对语音深度伪造检测中的域偏移问题，提出域梯度手术（DGS）方法，通过非对称投影解决MLDG中元训练与元测试梯度冲突，并进一步提出层级DGS（LW-DGS）高效变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音深度伪造检测面临显著的域偏移挑战。领域泛化（DG）中的元学习（MLDG）能模拟并缓解域偏移，但其元训练目标与元测试目标之间存在梯度冲突，导致优化次优。论文旨在解决这一梯度冲突问题以提升域泛化性能。

Method: 提出域梯度手术（DGS）：一种通过非对称投影策略解决冲突的元学习方法。DGS从元测试梯度中移除破坏性分量，确保相对于元训练梯度的无冲突优化轨迹。进一步提出层级DGS（LW-DGS）：DGS的高效变体，动态识别并仅干预易冲突的层。

Result: 在挑战性基准上的大量实验表明，DGS-MLDG和LW-DGS-MLDG分别实现平均相对EER降低5.29%和4.04%。

Conclusion: DGS通过非对称投影有效解决MLDG的梯度冲突问题，提升语音深度伪造检测的域泛化能力；LW-DGS在保持高效的同时进一步降低EER，验证了方法的有效性与效率。

Abstract: Speech deepfake detection faces significant challenges due to domain shifts. Domain generalization (DG), particularly meta-learning for domain generalization (MLDG), offers a promising solution by simulating and mitigating domain shifts. However, MLDG is often hindered by conflicting gradients between its meta-train and meta-test objectives, leading to suboptimal performance. To address this problem, we propose domain gradient surgery (DGS), a meta-learning method that resolves conflicts through an asymmetric projection strategy. DGS removes the destructive component from the meta-test gradient, ensuring a conflict-free optimization trajectory versus the meta-train gradient. Furthermore, we introduce layer-wise DGS (LW-DGS), an efficient variant of DGS that dynamically identifies and intervenes only conflict-prone layers. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DGS-MLDG and LW-DGS-MLDG achieve an average relative EER reduction of 5.29% and 4.04%, respectively.

</details>


### [212] [Domain-Adaptive Dual-Gating Mixture of Experts for Generalizable Speech Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33709)
*Siqing Qin,Zhe Li,Kong Aik Lee,Man-Wai Mak*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出领域自适应双门控专家混合框架（DADGMoE）用于语音深度伪造检测，通过Sinc层滤波器与SSL表示构成的双门控机制并结合域原型引导专家路由，在跨数据集基准上实现最高40.8%的相对EER下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于专家混合（MoE）的语音深度伪造检测虽提升了泛化能力，但其门控网络往往忽视深度伪造中的声学与时间线索，导致在未见攻击类型与声学条件下的泛化受限。

Method: 提出DADGMoE框架：1）双门控机制，利用基于Sinc层的滤波器同时处理低层声学信号（原始波形）与来自大规模自监督学习（SSL）模型的高层语音表示；2）引入域原型，基于隐式深度伪造模式引导专家路由；3）采用轻量级仿射专家处理被路由的输入。

Result: 在具有挑战性的跨数据集（out-of-dataset）基准上，DADGMoE显著优于基线，等错误率（EER）相对降低最高达40.8%，展现出更优的泛化能力与高效设计。

Conclusion: 双门控与域原型引导的MoE设计能有效捕捉声学与时序线索，提升语音深度伪造检测在未见攻击类型与声学条件下的泛化性能，并保持高效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in speech deepfake detection (SDD) have leveraged the Mixture of Experts (MoE) to enhance generalization capacity. However, existing gating networks often overlook the acoustic and temporal cues of deepfakes. In this work, we propose a novel domain-adaptive dual-gating MoE (DADGMoE) framework for SDD under unseen attack types and acoustic conditions. Our innovative dual-gating mechanism leverages Sinc-layer-based filters to process both low-level acoustic signals (raw waveforms) and high-level speech representations from a large self-supervised learning (SSL) model. It further incorporates domain prototypes to guide expert routing based on implicit deepfake patterns. The lightweight affine experts process the routed inputs. Experiments show that our DADGMoE significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving up to a 40.8% relative EER reduction on challenging out-of-dataset benchmarks. This framework demonstrates superior generalization capabilities and efficient design.

</details>


### [213] [YuE2: Unifying Symbolic and Audio Music Generation at Frontier Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33757)
*Ruibin Yuan,Jiahao Pan,Junyan Jiang,Zhiyue Wu,Ziya Zhou,Jiankai Sun,Yizhi Li,Ge Zhang,Yicheng Gu,Zeyue Tian,Junyu Dai,Hanfeng Lin,Kai Li,Shangda Wu,Xuanjie Liu,Jiaming Wang,Zihan Liu,Yue Wang,Yinghao Ma,Hanzhi Yin,Kangrui Chen,Xinyue Zhang,Ziyang Ma,Mengqi Liao,Hejia Zhao,Guowei Huang,Chao Yan,Lei Ke,Jianwei Yu,Bei Liu,Joe Guo,Liumeng Xue,Gus Xia,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: YuE2通过符号规划统一符号与音频音乐生成，以单一AR-NAR MoT模型完成从可读乐谱到完整歌曲音频的生成，在人工评测与WildSongBench上均超越公开基线并可媲美专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号模型能显式建模旋律、和声、节奏与曲式，但通常止步于录制之前；音频模型虽能生成完整歌曲，却将作曲过程隐式化。论文旨在弥合两类范式，兼得可解释的作曲规划与前沿质量的音频生成。

Method: 提出YuE2：单一AR-NAR Mixture-of-Transformers先写出显式可读乐谱（旋律与和声），再扩展为语义音乐token，最终实现完整歌曲音频。为在无对齐乐谱的条件下从录音中学习该生成过程，引入MERT2提供语义监督、SheetSage2提供符号监督。

Result: 同一检查点下，专家更偏好符号规划，整体质量偏好率49.3%对34.6%；专家也偏好统一模型而非独立语言模型加扩散Transformer。WildSongBench上SongBench Global Avg达6.73，超过所有公开基线；best-of-8达6.96，为所评系统中最高均值。与专有系统相比，best-of-8优于Suno v4.5，与Suno v5接近持平。MERT2在15项MARBLE指标中14项刷新SOTA；SheetSage2在15个基准-指标对中12项领先。

Conclusion: 同一检查点可跟随乐谱编辑并基本保留未编辑的音乐内容，且无需专门训练即可实现零样本翻唱；可读乐谱还支持代理式音乐编辑，由外部语言模型将用户反馈转化为作曲修订。

Abstract: Symbolic models make melody, harmony, rhythm, and form explicit but typically stop before a finished recording; audio models produce complete songs while leaving composition implicit. We introduce YuE2, which unifies symbolic and audio music generation at frontier quality through symbolic planning. A single AR-NAR Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) first writes a readable score specifying melody and harmony, expands it into semantic music tokens, and realizes it as full-song audio. In comparisons using the same checkpoint, experts prefer symbolic planning for overall quality and musicality, with 49.3% of overall preferences versus 34.6% without planning. Experts also favor the unified model over a separate language model and diffusion Transformer. On WildSongBench, YuE2 scores 6.73 on SongBench Global Avg, exceeding all evaluated public baselines. Selecting from eight candidates (best-of-8), YuE2 reaches 6.96, the highest observed mean among all evaluated systems. Expert listening further establishes its competitiveness with proprietary song generators, favoring best-of-8 over Suno v4.5 and yielding nearly balanced preferences against Suno v5. To learn this generation process from recordings without aligned scores, we introduce MERT2 and SheetSage2 to supply semantic and symbolic supervision. MERT2 sets a new state of the art in music representation learning, surpassing previous best results on 14 of 15 MARBLE metrics; SheetSage2 leads 12 of 15 benchmark-metric pairs in our lead-sheet transcription comparison. The same checkpoint follows score edits while largely preserving unedited musical content and generates zero-shot covers without cover-specific training. Its readable score also enables agentic music editing, with external language models translating user feedback into revisions of the composition.

</details>


### [214] [Rethinking Automated Voice Similarity by Shifting from EER to Embedding Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33999)
*Szu-Chi Chen,Jia-Kai Dong,Yi-Cheng Lin,Sung-Feng Huang,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 说话人验证(SV)模型的感知对齐主要由训练目标决定而非EER性能；分类损失导致嵌入高维化并与人类低维语音感知冲突，施加维度瓶颈可将感知对齐从0.08提升至0.74。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区普遍假设SV模型验证准确率(EER)越高，越能捕捉说话人特征细节，因而被广泛用作语音生成任务中人类语音相似度的自动代理。作者旨在检验该隐含假设是否成立。

Method: 建立人类感知对齐度量(ρ_align)，对多种SV模型进行系统分析，比较不同训练目标（基于margin的分类损失如AAM-Softmax vs. 原型度量损失）与EER对感知对齐的影响；并通过嵌入几何分析引入有效维度d_eff，考察其与感知对齐的关系，进而施加维度瓶颈进行验证。

Result: 感知对齐更多由学习目标而非EER决定；分类损失(AAM-Softmax)的感知对齐显著低于原型度量损失，且EER无法追踪人类判断。d_eff与感知对齐呈-0.95的秩相关，表明分类损失偏好的高维分布与人类语音感知的低维本质相冲突。施加维度瓶颈压缩d_eff后，感知对齐ρ_align由0.08升至0.74。

Conclusion: 验证准确率不能作为人类语音相似度感知的可靠代理，训练目标与嵌入几何（有效维度）才是关键因素；低维嵌入更契合人类感知，为评估语音相似度提供了原则性的几何准则。

Abstract: Speaker verification (SV) models are commonly assumed to better capture nuances among speaker characteristics as verification accuracy improves, leading to their widespread use as automated proxies for human voice similarity in speech generation tasks. However, by establishing a human perceptual alignment metric and conducting systematic analysis, we demonstrate that perceptual alignment is governed far more by how a model is trained (its learning objective) than by how well it performs (EER). Notably, standard margin-based classification losses (e.g., AAM-Softmax) yield substantially lower perceptual alignment than prototypical metric losses, while EER itself fails to track human judgment, directly challenging the community's implicit assumption. We trace this divergence to embedding geometry, where a model's effective dimensionality ($d_{\mathrm{eff}}$) tracks perceptual alignment with a $-0.95$ rank correlation, revealing that the dimensional spread favored by classification losses fundamentally clashes with the low-dimensional nature of human voice perception. Imposing a dimensionality bottleneck compresses $d_{\mathrm{eff}}$ and raises perceptual alignment ($ρ_{\mathrm{align}}$) from 0.08 to 0.74, establishing a principled geometric criterion for evaluating voice similarity.

</details>


### [215] [SPEAR-Gen: Generation-Aware Pre-training for Unified Speech Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34147)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Arthur Hinsvark,Antonios Alexos,Osama Hanna,Philip C. Woodland,Yiting Lu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: SPEAR-Gen 提出一种统一语音表示模型，通过任务对齐特征聚合与粗到细目标（log-Mel重构+残差流匹配），用单一表示同时支持语音理解与生成，在SUPERB和重合成任务上兼顾理解性能与重合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音理解与生成对语音表示的需求不同，现有模型通常只针对其中一种能力优化，导致理解与生成之间存在表示鸿沟，缺乏能同时服务两种能力的统一表示。

Method: SPEAR-Gen 学习用于理解与生成的单一表示：任务对齐特征聚合在冻结编码器上整合互补的语言学与副语言学信息，形成离散的掩码预测目标；并采用粗到细目标，将 log-Mel 重构与残差流匹配相结合，以保留频谱结构和细粒度声学变化。

Result: 在 SUPERB 与语音重合成实验中，SPEAR-Gen 在保持较强理解性能的同时，显著提升了重合成质量和说话人保持度。

Conclusion: 单一语音表示能够有效同时支撑语音理解与生成，表明统一表示在弥合两种能力鸿沟方面具有可行性。

Abstract: Speech understanding and generation place different demands on speech representations, and existing models are typically optimised towards one capability or the other. To reduce this gap, we introduce SPEAR-Gen, a speech representation model that learns a single representation for both capabilities. Task-aligned feature aggregation consolidates complementary linguistic and paralinguistic information across a frozen encoder into discrete targets for masked prediction, while a coarse-to-fine objective combines log-Mel reconstruction with residual flow matching to preserve spectral structure and fine-grained acoustic variation. Experiments on SUPERB and speech resynthesis show that SPEAR-Gen maintains strong understanding performance while substantially improving resynthesis quality and speaker preservation. These results demonstrate that a single speech representation can effectively support both understanding and generation.

</details>


### [216] [GAMF: Learned and Analytical Array Transfer Function Matching for Array-Generic Direction-of-Arrival Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34216)
*Zhiheng Jin,Shichao Hu,Chunyang Xu,Mengyao Zhu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出GAMF框架，以阵列传递函数（ATF）作为声学描述符，实现跨阵列几何与麦克风数量的DOA估计，混合配置在仿真与LOCATA实测中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦克风位置编码支持跨阵列DOA估计，但仅凭坐标无法充分刻画设备遮挡（shadowing）与麦克风指向性等声学特性，导致跨几何泛化能力受限。

Method: GAMF包含两个分支：学习分支将ATF嵌入融入几何条件化神经估计，用于将声学观测与候选方向匹配；解析分支执行由广义可控响应功率（SRP）改造而来的归一化ATF匹配；混合配置通过自适应门控融合二者得分。

Result: 多种阵列配置的仿真显示，学习与混合配置均优于基于位置编码的代表性神经基线，在低混响干净场景下与解析ATF匹配相当，在强噪声或强混响下显著更优；在八麦克风LOCATA Task 1录音上，混合配置优于所评估的SOTA基线，三维DOA平均误差3.76°，方位角误差2.87°。

Conclusion: 以ATF作为声学描述符可有效弥补坐标位置编码的不足，学习与解析分支的自适应融合在跨阵列几何、跨麦克风数量及恶劣声学环境下均具优势，并在真实数据上达到领先精度。

Abstract: Microphone positional encoding supports cross-array direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation, but coordinates alone cannot fully describe device shadowing or microphone directivity. We propose a Generalizable ATF Matching Framework (GAMF) for DOA estimation across array geometries and microphone counts, using array transfer functions (ATFs) as acoustic descriptors. The learned branch incorporates ATF embeddings into geometry-conditioned neural estimation to match acoustic observations with candidate directions. The analytical branch performs normalized ATF matching adapted from generalized steered response power. A hybrid configuration combines their scores through adaptive gating. Simulations across array configurations show that both learned and hybrid configurations outperform a representative positional-encoding-based neural baseline, remain competitive with analytical ATF matching in clean, low-reverberation scenes, and substantially improve upon it under stronger noise or reverberation. On eight-microphone LOCATA Task 1 recordings, the hybrid configuration outperforms the evaluated state-of-the-art baselines for three-dimensional DOA estimation, achieving mean errors of $3.76^\circ$ for three-dimensional DOA and $2.87^\circ$ for azimuth.

</details>


### [217] [Explainable and Generalisable LLM-based Cognitive Decline Detection with Spontaneous Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34217)
*Ziyun Cui,Wen Wu,Chuan Shi,Shuguang Yang,Xueying Gui,Yan Zheng,Qiong Yang,Haiyan Zhao,Wei-Qiang Zhang,Ji Wu,Yelei Li,Nan Li,Chao Zhang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种双语语音大语言模型框架，直接处理原始语音学习声学-语义联合表征，实现可解释的认知筛查，在六个数据集/任务条件下取得最高平均准确率与AUROC，并获临床医生认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）与轻度认知障碍（MCI）早期表现为细微的语言与声学变化，但传统诊断资源消耗大、难以大规模筛查；且常规流程依赖易出错的自动语音识别，会丢失关键韵律线索。

Method: 构建双语语音大语言模型框架，绕过ASR直接处理原始语音以学习声学-语义联合表征；结合新采集的PUTH-AD数据集与多个开源语料库，采用多任务学习目标，同步进行认知状态分类与生成临床医生可理解的自然语言解释。

Result: 在六个数据集/任务条件下，平均准确率与AUROC均优于三个代表性基线；在完全未见过的PUTH-AD任务子集上实现跨任务迁移，无需任务特定微调仍保持分类准确率；临床医生评估表明生成解释具有临床相关性且与语音证据基本一致。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可扩展、客观、可解释的语音认知筛查框架，将认知状态分类与临床可评估的自然语言解释相结合，弥合了先进AI与临床实用性之间的差距。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) and mild cognitive impairment (MCI), which may precede AD, manifest early through subtle linguistic and acoustic alterations. Traditional diagnostics, however, are often resource-intensive and lack scalability for mass screening. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel bilingual speech large language model framework for automated, explainable cognitive screening. Unlike conventional pipelines that rely on error-prone automatic speech recognition, our system directly processes raw speech to learn joint acoustic-semantic representations, preserving critical prosodic cues often lost in transcription. Utilising our newly collected PUTH-AD dataset alongside multiple open-source corpora, we implemented a multi-task learning objective that simultaneously performs cognitive status classification and generates clinician-understandable natural language explanations. Our system achieved the highest average accuracy and AUROC across six dataset/task conditions, comparing three representative baselines. The system demonstrated cross-task transfer to held-out PUTH-AD task subsets, maintaining classification accuracy on an entirely unseen cognitive task without task-specific fine-tuning. Furthermore, clinician evaluation confirms that the generated explanations are both clinically relevant and largely consistent with the underlying speech evidence, supporting their potential utility in clinical interpretation. This study provides a scalable, objective, and explainable framework for speech-based cognitive screening, combining cognitive status classification with natural language explanations that clinicians can assess and verify, bridging the gap between advanced AI and clinical utility.

</details>


### [218] [Audio Tokens as a Budgeted Resource: Marginal-Utility Allocation for Scalable Audio Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34337)
*Mingyu Zhao,Jinchao Zhang,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: UniAdapt 在冻结的 RVQ 编解码器上学习细化的边际效用，并在精确序列化比特预算下自适应分配深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定深度 RVQ 分词器对每帧分配相同容量，忽视各帧细化价值差异，导致容量分配低效。

Method: 提出 UniAdapt：用率无关因果控制器预测声学效用，可选语义头支持纯语音句子级分配；因果分配用原对偶分配器选前缀有效深度，并用精确守卫将每个序列前缀约束至匹配的固定深度序列化预算。

Result: 句子级分配在不增大预算下将语音、音乐、环境音频的 Log-STFT 失真降低 1.07–4.39%；因果模式在四种语音码率中三种有提升，800 次句子-码率评估零违规，且快于实时；20 人 MUSHRA 显示语音显著提升 3.52 分，音乐与环境音频无显著差异；声学与语义边际增益相关性为 0.42。

Conclusion: 将效用预测与预算执行分离，可支持可扩展、预算条件化的音频表示。

Abstract: Discrete audio tokens are widely used as a representation interface, yet fixed-depth RVQ tokenizers allocate equal capacity to every frame despite varying refinement value. We introduce UniAdapt, which learns marginal utility of RVQ refinements on a frozen codec and allocates them under exact serialized-bit budgets. A rate-independent causal controller predicts acoustic utility, while an optional semantic head supports speech-only utterance-level allocation; measured acoustic and semantic marginal gains on speech have a correlation of 0.42. For causal allocation, a primal-dual allocator selects prefix-valid depths, while an exact guard constrains each sequence prefix to its matched fixed-depth serialized budget. Under utterance-level allocation, UniAdapt reduces Log-STFT distortion by 1.07-4.39 percent across speech, music, and environmental audio without larger budgets. Causally, it improves three of four speech rates with zero violations across 800 utterance-rate evaluations and runs faster than real time. A 20-listener utterance-level MUSHRA study shows a significant 3.52-point speech improvement, with no significant differences on music or environmental audio. These results support separating utility prediction from budget enforcement for scalable, budget-conditioned audio representations.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Textual Chain-of-Thought: JEPA-Conditioned Latent Reasoning for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34407)
*Donghang Wu,Haoyang Zhang,Yizhou Peng,Shreyas Gopal,Yi-Wen Chao,Chen Chen,Hexin Liu,William Tjhi,Eng-Siong Chng*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: JELAR：基于JEPA的音频潜在推理框架，以原始波形声学表征替代文本CoT，在MMAU-mini和MMAR上分别提升2.70和9.10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式文本CoT虽提升了大音频语言模型（LALM）的推理能力，但其通常由音频的文本描述构建，难以直接获取声学证据，导致模态鸿沟与幻觉等问题。

Method: 提出JELAR，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的潜在推理框架：训练时用冻结的WavJEPA模型从原始波形提取表征；非因果专家先构造答案感知查询，通过交叉注意力作用于WavJEPA嵌入生成潜在推理目标；LALM在生成回答前先预测这些目标，从而实现以声学表征为条件的潜在推理监督。

Result: 在MMAU-mini和MMAR上分别较Audio-Reasoner基线提升2.70和9.10个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: JEPA条件化的潜在推理可作为显式文本CoT监督的有效替代方案，验证了其在音频推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Explicit textual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has improved the reasoning ability of large audio language models (LALMs). However, textual CoTs are often constructed from text captions of audio and provide limited access to the acoustic evidence, which can introduce problems like hallucination. To address this modality-gap issue, we introduce JELAR, a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)-based latent reasoning framework that conditions latent reasoning supervision on acoustic representations learned from raw waveforms. During training, a frozen WavJEPA model provides representations learned directly from raw waveforms. A non-causal expert first constructs answer-aware queries, which cross-attend to WavJEPA embeddings to produce latent reasoning targets. The LALM is trained to predict these targets before generating its response. Experimental results show that JELAR improves the Audio-Reasoner baseline by 2.70 and 9.10 absolute percentage points on MMAU-mini and MMAR, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of JEPA-conditioned latent reasoning as an alternative to explicit textual CoT supervision.

</details>


### [220] [CharDuplex: Building Character-Consistent Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34461)
*Donghang Wu,Yisi Liu,Chen Chen,Hexin Liu,Eng Siong Chng*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: CharDuplex 是一个角色驱动的全双工语音模型，将实时语音交互与人格化行为结合，通过双流架构适配、角色条件化数据微调及 FDGym 强化学习实现角色一致的全双工对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然对话不仅取决于智能体何时说话，还取决于其作为对话角色如何表现行为。现有全双工语音模型主要关注轮次转换时机，缺乏对人格/角色一致性的建模能力。

Method: 1) 将 GLM-4-Voice 适配为常开（always-on）双流架构并进行全双工对话训练；2) 构建全自动流水线，从开源角色描述生成角色条件化对话数据，用于角色条件化监督微调（SFT）；3) 提出 FDGym，由 LLM 模拟用户与模型动态交互，在演进的多轮交互上进行强化学习精调。

Result: 在 SpeechRole-Eval 上，CharDuplex 在所评估的开源模型中取得最高平均分，并与闭源系统保持竞争力；同时展现出有竞争力的通用语音智能和较强的全双工交互能力。

Conclusion: CharDuplex 提供了一套实用的训练方案，用于构建不仅可交互、而且角色一致的语音助手，验证了角色驱动全双工语音模型的可行性。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models are moving voice interaction beyond conventional turn-taking, yet natural conversation is shaped not only by when an agent speaks, but also by how it behaves as a conversational character. We present CharDuplex, a character-driven full-duplex speech model that combines real-time spoken interaction with persona-conditioned behavior. We first adapt GLM-4-Voice to an always-on dual-stream architecture and train the model for full-duplex conversation. Then a fully automated pipeline constructs character-conditioned dialogue data from open-source character descriptions for character-conditioned supervised fine-tuning. The model is further refined with the proposed FDGym, where an LLM-simulated user dynamically interacts with the model, enabling reinforcement learning over evolving multi-turn interactions. On SpeechRole-Eval, CharDuplex achieves the highest average score among the evaluated open-source models, while remaining competitive with closed-source systems. It also demonstrates competitive general speech intelligence and strong full-duplex interaction capabilities. CharDuplex demonstrates a practical training recipe for building full-duplex voice assistants that are not only interactive, but also character-consistent.

</details>


### [221] [Measurement-Based Bitrate-Energy-Quality Analysis of Neural Audio Codec Decoders on Laptop and Phone Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34524)
*Seunghyeon Shin,Seokjin Lee*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文对四种神经音频编解码器（EnCodec、DAC、HILCodec、SNAC）与两种传统基线（AAC-LC、Opus）在笔记本与手机平台上进行基于测量的率—能量—质量分析，语声与音乐分开评估（ViSQOL），并推导成对能量盈亏平衡阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器能在更低码率下达到相当的客观质量，但其解码端计算开销可能抵消低码率优势，尤其在电池供电的客户端设备上。因此需要量化码率、能量与质量之间的实际权衡。

Method: 在笔记本与手机平台上测量四种神经编解码器与两种传统基线，语声与音乐分别用原始参考ViSQOL协议评估；主比较通过选取最接近共同重叠区间内治疗水平平均ViSQOL中点的测点来匹配工作点，辅以仅含显式评估码率设置的补充分析。解码能量以空闲扣除后的每音频秒焦耳（J/s）度量，结果取三次重复运行的中位数（仅限执行有效的运行时与设备路径）。随后由实测解码能量与码率解析推导成对传输能量盈亏平衡阈值。

Result: 在匹配质量的工作点上，所评估的神经编解码器以更低码率取得相近ViSQOL分数，但解码端能量普遍高于对应的传统编解码器。EnCodec在两个匹配质量队列、两个平台上均给出最低的神经盈亏平衡阈值；相反，Phone XNNPACK CPU路径上若干DAC与SNAC比较超过200 mJ/kbit，其全频带Phone配置解码1秒音频需超过1秒。

Conclusion: 仅凭更低码率并不能保证能量收益；部署效率还取决于解码器复杂度、有效运行时与设备映射、执行有效性、加速器可用性以及传输能量系数。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs can achieve similar objective quality at lower bitrates than conventional codecs, but their decoder-side computational cost may offset this bitrate advantage on battery-powered client devices. This paper presents a measurement-based rate--energy--quality analysis of four neural audio codecs--EnCodec, DAC, HILCodec, and SNAC--and two conventional baselines, AAC-LC and Opus, on laptop and phone platforms. Speech and music are evaluated separately using the original-reference ViSQOL protocol. For the main comparison, operating points are matched by selecting the measured point nearest to the midpoint of the common overlap in treatment-level mean ViSQOL. A secondary analysis includes only explicitly evaluated bitrate settings. Decoder energy is measured as idle-subtracted energy per second of audio (J/s), and results are summarized as the median of three repeated runs for execution-valid runtime and device paths. Pairwise break-even transmission-energy thresholds are then derived analytically from the measured decoder energy and bitrate. At the matched-quality operating points, the evaluated neural codecs achieved similar ViSQOL scores at lower bitrates but generally required more decoder-side energy than the applicable conventional codecs. EnCodec produced the lowest neural break-even thresholds in both matched-quality cohorts on both platforms. By contrast, several DAC and SNAC comparisons on the Phone XNNPACK CPU path exceeded 200 mJ/kbit, and their full-band Phone configurations required more than 1 s to decode 1 s of audio. These results show that lower bitrate alone does not guarantee an energy benefit: deployment efficiency also depends on decoder complexity, the effective runtime and device mapping, execution validity, accelerator availability, and the transmission-energy coefficient.

</details>


### [222] [Domain-Incremental Learning for Generative Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34901)
*Manjunath Mulimani,Annamaria Mesaros,Minje Kim,Jesper Rindom Jensen*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种面向生成式语音增强的域增量学习框架：以LM-based生成式SE模型为预训练骨干，用轻量级域特定LoRA进行增量适配，在四个异构数据集上实现适应新域且不遗忘旧域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音增强模型需在声学条件多样的数据流上持续学习。直接在不断演化的域上微调会造成对已有知识的灾难性遗忘；而零样本泛化又难以充分适应未见域。需同时兼顾新域适应与旧域性能保持。

Method: 1) 构建基于语言模型的生成式语音增强模型，作为预训练骨干；2) 采用域增量学习设定，按域序列逐次适配；3) 使用轻量级、域特定的低秩适配（LoRA）模块进行增量参数更新，冻结主干以隔离各域知识，从而在获取新域增强能力的同时保留旧域性能。

Result: 在四个异构语音数据集上评估，方法能有效适应新域，且未出现对已学域的遗忘，即新域适应与旧域保持性能同时达成。

Conclusion: 将语言模型驱动的生成式SE骨干与域特定LoRA增量适配结合，可有效缓解灾难性遗忘并优于零样本泛化，为跨声学条件的持续语音增强提供可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a domain-incremental learning framework for generative speech enhancement (SE) that learns from a sequence of datasets or domains recorded under diverse acoustic conditions. Fine-tuning a pretrained model on continuously evolving domains leads to catastrophic forgetting of previously acquired knowledge, while zero-shot generalization often fails to adequately adapt to unseen domains. To address these challenges, we first develop a novel language model-based generative SE model that we then use as a pretrained backbone and incrementally adapt it to acoustically mismatched domains using lightweight domain-specific Low-Rank Adaptation. The proposed framework enables the model to acquire enhancement capabilities for new domains while preserving performance on previously learned domains. Evaluated on four heterogeneous speech datasets, our approach effectively adapts to new domains without forgetting previously learned domains.

</details>


### [223] [Perceptual Quality Loss or Loss of Perceptual Quality?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35054)
*Danilo de Oliveira,Tal Peer,Maurício do V. M. da Costa,Timo Gerkmann*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文通过客观与主观实验表明，以PESQ作为辅助损失优化语音增强模型虽能提升测试集PESQ分数，但未必改善听感，且PESQ在CSIG、CBAK、COVL复合指标中占主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度语音增强（SE）模型常引入特定辅助损失项（如PESQ）以提升感知指标性能，但更高的感知指标分数并不必然对应更好的主观听音体验，因此需要重新审视对PESQ的过度依赖问题。

Method: 训练使用两类不同PESQ辅助损失项的SE模型，并开展客观与主观实验：在标准指标集合上进行数值评估，组织正式听音实验进行主观偏好测试，同时分析PESQ在CSIG、CBAK、COVL复合指标中的相对重要性。

Result: 以PESQ为优化目标的模型在测试集上确实获得更高的PESQ分数，但大多数其他指标无显著变化；在失配数据上，PESQ损失甚至导致PESQ分数下降。主观听音实验显示，在所有设置下，未使用PESQ损失的模型总体更受偏好。此外，PESQ在CSIG、CBAK和COVL中均占主导作用。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了过度依赖PESQ指标的风险，强调语音增强评估应采用完整、多维度的评估流程，而非单一感知指标的优化与报告。

Abstract: Contemporary deep speech enhancement (SE) models are often trained with specific auxiliary terms in the loss function as a way to improve their performance in terms of perceptual metrics. Nevertheless, a higher score on a perceptual metric does not necessarily correlate with an improved listening experience. Through objective and subjective experiments, we assess the performance of SE models trained with two different types of auxiliary PESQ loss terms. The numerical evaluation on a suite of standard metrics suggests that, while models optimized for PESQ naturally obtain higher PESQ scores in the test set, for most other metrics the scores do not significantly change. In some cases, the PESQ loss even results in worse PESQ scores on mismatched data. A formal listening experiment reveals that the models without a PESQ loss were generally preferred over models that include it, across all settings. Finally, we analyze the relative importance of PESQ in the composite metrics CSIG, CBAK and COVL, and find that PESQ dominates all of them. Our study highlights the perils of over-reliance on PESQ and stresses the importance of a complete evaluation procedure for SE.

</details>


### [224] [Simulation-Based Inference for Plate Reverb System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35295)
*Dylan Sechet,Marc Evrard,Matthieu Kowalski*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文使用基于仿真的推断（SBI）框架，通过神经网络从脉冲响应中估计板模型的物理参数，并对每个测试样本进行针对性微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决第1届DAFx参数估计挑战赛任务A：从脉冲响应中反演板模型的物理参数。

Method: 采用基于仿真的推断（SBI）框架，用模拟器生成数据集训练神经网络，学习给定脉冲响应下板参数的概率密度分布；推断时仅需网络前向传播而无需调用模拟器。针对每个测试观测，通过从当前估计分布中采样参数、模拟对应脉冲响应并进行额外仿真轮次的微调，得到专门的网络。

Result: 未在摘要中给出具体数值结果（摘要未报告评估指标）。

Conclusion: 该方法将SBI与逐样本微调相结合，实现无需模拟器的快速前向推断，并可通过额外仿真轮次提升对单个测试观测的参数估计精度。

Abstract: We address Task A of the 1st DAFx Parameter Estimation Challenge, which aims to retrieve the physical parameters of a plate model from an impulse response. To do so, we use the Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) framework, in which we train a neural network to estimate a density over plate parameters given an impulse response, using a dataset generated by the simulator. Inference for a new impulse response then requires only a forward pass through the network, without involving the simulator. For each test observation, we fine-tune a specific network: additional simulation rounds are performed by sampling parameters from the current estimated distribution, simulating the corresponding impulse responses, and fine-tuning to produce the specialized network.

</details>
